āvilaināti pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi pariyaunaddhaina| attatthaṃ vātiādīsu attanau diṭṭhadhammikau laukiyalaukuttaramissakau atthau attatthau nāma| attanauva samparāyai laukiyalaukuttaramissakau  atthau paratthau nāma hauti| sau hi  parattha atthauti paratthau| tadubhayaṃ ubhayatthau nāma| apica attanau diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaupi laukiyalaukuttarau atthau attatthau nāma, parassa tādisauva atthau paratthau nāma, tadubhayampi ubhayatthau nāma| uttariṃ vā manussadhammāti dasakusalakammapathasaṅkhātā manussadhammā uttariṃ| ayañhi dasavidhau dhammau vināpi añ‍ñaṃ samādāpakaṃ satthantarakappāvasānai jātasaṃvaigaihi manussaihi sayamaiva samādin‍nattā manussadhammauti vuc‍cati, tatau uttariṃ pana jhānavipassanāmaggaphalāni vaiditabbāni| alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisanti ariyānaṃ yuttaṃ, ariyabhāvaṃ vā kātuṃ samatthaṃ ñāṇadassanasaṅkhātaṃ visaisaṃ| ñāṇamaiva hi jānanaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, dassanaṭṭhaina dassananti vaiditabbaṃ, dibbacakkhuñāṇavipassanāñāṇamaggañāṇaphalañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

46. chaṭṭhai acchauti abahalau, pasan‍nautipi vaṭṭati| vippasan‍nauti suṭṭhu pasan‍nau| anāvilauti na āvilau, parisuddhauti atthau, phaiṇapubbuḷasaṅkhasaivālapaṇakavirahitauti vuttaṃ hauti| anāvilaināti pañ‍canīvaraṇavimuttaina| saisaṃ catutthai vuttanayamaiva| imasmimpi suttadvayai vaṭṭavivaṭṭamaiva kathitaṃ|

47. sattamai rukkhajātānanti pac‍cattai sāmivacanaṃ, rukkhajātānīti atthau| rukkhānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| yadidanti nipātamattaṃ| mudutāyāti  mudubhāvaina| kauci hi rukkhau vaṇṇaina aggau hauti, kauci gandhaina, kauci rasaina, kauci thaddhatāya| phandanau pana mudutāya caiva kammañ‍ñatāya ca aggau saiṭṭhauti dassaiti| cittaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvitaṃ bahulīkatanti aittha samathavipassanāvasaina bhāvitañ‍caiva punappunakatañ‍ca cittaṃ  adhippaitaṃ| kurundakavāsi phussamittatthairau panāha – ‘‘aikantaṃ mudu caiva kammaniyañ‍ca cittaṃ nāma abhiñ‍ñāpādakacatutthajjhānacittamaiva, āvusau’’ti|

48. aṭṭhamai aivaṃ lahuparivattanti aivaṃ lahuṃ uppaj‍jitvā lahuṃ nirujjhanakaṃ| yāvañ‍cāti adhimattapamāṇatthai nipātau, ativiya na sukarāti atthau| idanti nipātamattaṃ| cittanti aikac‍cai tāva ācariyā ‘‘bhavaṅgacitta’’nti vadanti, taṃ pana paṭikkhipitvā ‘‘idha cittanti yaṃkiñ‍ci antamasau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇampi adhippaitamaivā’’ti vuttaṃ| imasmiṃ panatthai milindarājā dhammakathikaṃ nāgasainatthairaṃ pucchi, ‘‘bhantai nāgasaina, aikasmiṃ accharākkhaṇai pavattitacittasaṅkhārā  sacai rūpinau assu, kīva mahārāsi bhavaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘vāhasatānaṃ khau, mahārāja, vīhīnaṃ aḍḍhacūḷañ‍ca vāhā vīhisattambaṇāni dvai ca tumbā aikaccharākkhaṇai pavattitassa cittassa saṅkhampi na upainti, kalampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upaintī’’ti (mi॰ pa॰ 4.1.2)| atha kasmā sammāsambuddhaina ‘‘upamāpi na sukarā’’ti vuttaṃ? yathaiva hi upamaṃ paṭikkhipitvāpi kappadīghabhāvassa  yaujanikapabbataina yaujanikasāsapapuṇṇanagaraina, nirayadukkhassa sattisatāhataupamaina, saggasukhassa ca cak‍kavattisampattiyā upamā katā, aivamidhāpi kātabbāti? tattha ‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamā kātu’’nti aivaṃ pucchāvasaina upamā katā, imasmiṃ suttai pucchāya abhāvaina na katā| idañhi suttaṃ dhammadaisanāpariyausānai vuttaṃ| iti imasmiṃ suttai cittarāsi nāma kathitauti|

49. navamai pabhassaranti paṇḍaraṃ parisuddhaṃ| cittanti bhavaṅgacittaṃ| kiṃ pana cittassa vaṇṇau nāma atthīti? natthi| nīlādīnañhi añ‍ñataravaṇṇaṃ vā hautu avaṇṇaṃ vā yaṃkiñ‍ci parisuddhatāya ‘‘pabhassara’’nti vuc‍cati| idampi nirupak‍kilaisatāya parisuddhanti pabhassaraṃ| tañ‍ca khauti taṃ bhavaṅgacittaṃ| āgantukaihīti asahajātaihi pacchā javanakkhaṇai uppaj‍janakaihi| upak‍kilaisaihīti rāgādīhi upak‍kiliṭṭhattā upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ nāmāti vuc‍cati| kathaṃ? yathā hi sīlavantā ācārasampan‍nā mātāpitarau vā ācariyupajjhāyā vā dussīlānaṃ durācārānaṃ avattasampan‍nānaṃ puttānañ‍caiva antaivāsikasaddhivihārikānañ‍ca vasaina ‘‘attanau puttai vā antaivāsikasaddhivihārikai vā na taj‍jainti na sikkhāpainti na auvadanti nānusāsantī’’ti  avaṇṇaṃ akittiṃ labhanti, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| ācārasampan‍nā mātāpitarau viya ca ācariyupajjhāyā viya ca bhavaṅgacittaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, puttādīnaṃ vasaina taisaṃ akittilābhau viya javanakkhaṇai raj‍janadussanamuyhanasabhāvānaṃ laubhasahagatādīnaṃ cittānaṃ vasaina uppan‍naihi āgantukaihi  upak‍kilaisaihi pakatiparisuddhampi bhavaṅgacittaṃ upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ nāma hautīti|

50. dasamaipi bhavaṅgacittamaiva cittaṃ| vippamuttanti javanakkhaṇai araj‍jamānaṃ adussamānaṃ amuyhamānaṃ tihaitukañāṇasampayuttādikusalavasaina uppaj‍jamānaṃ āgantukaihi upak‍kilaisaihi vippamuttaṃ nāma hauti| idhāpi yathā sīlavantānaṃ ācārasampan‍nānaṃ puttādīnaṃ vasaina mātādayau ‘‘saubhanā aitaiyaiva attanau puttakādayau sikkhāpainti auvadanti anusāsantī’’ti vaṇṇakittilābhinau  haunti, aivaṃ javanakkhaṇai uppan‍nakusalacittavasaina idaṃ bhavaṅgacittaṃ āgantukaihi upak‍kilaisaihi vippamuttanti vuc‍catīti|

paṇihitaacchavaggavaṇṇanā|



6. accharāsaṅghātavaggavaṇṇanā

51. chaṭṭhassa  paṭhamai taṃ assutavā puthuj‍janauti taṃ bhavaṅgacittaṃ sutavirahitau puthuj‍janau| tattha āgamādhigamābhāvā ñaiyyau assutavā iti| yau hi idaṃ suttaṃ āditau paṭṭhāya atthavasaina upaparikkhantau ‘‘idaṃ bhavaṅgacittaṃ nāma pakatiparisuddhampi javanakkhaṇai uppan‍naihi laubhādīhi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭha’’nti naiva āgamavasaina na adhigamavasaina jānāti, yassa ca khandhadhātuāyatanapac‍cayākārasatipaṭṭhānādīsu uggahaparipucchāvinicchayavirahitattā yathābhūtañāṇapaṭivaidhasādhakau naiva āgamau, paṭipattiyā adhigantabbassa anadhigatattā na adhigamau atthi| sau āgamādhigamābhāvā ñaiyyau assutavā iti| svāyaṃ –

‘‘puthūnaṃ  jananādīhi, kāraṇaihi puthuj‍janau|

puthuj‍janantaugadhattā, puthuvāyaṃ janau iti’’||

sau  hi puthūnaṃ nānappakārānaṃ kilaisādīnaṃ jananādīhi kāraṇaihi puthuj‍janau| yathāha –

‘‘puthu kilaisai janaintīti puthuj‍janā, puthu avihatasak‍kāyadiṭṭhikāti puthuj‍janā, puthu satthārānaṃ mukhul‍laukikāti puthuj‍janā, puthu sabbagatīhi avuṭṭhitāti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānābhisaṅkhārai abhisaṅkharauntīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāaughaihi vuyhantīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāsantāpaihi santappantīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāpariḷāhaihi pariḍayhantīti puthuj‍janā, puthu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhaupan‍nā laggā laggitā palibuddhāti puthuj‍janā, puthu pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi āvutā nivutā auvutā pihitā paṭicchan‍nā paṭikuj‍jitāti puthuj‍janā’’ti (mahāni॰ 51, 94)|

puthūnaṃ vā gaṇanapathamatītānaṃ ariyadhammaparammukhānaṃ nīcadhammasamācārānaṃ janānaṃ antaugadhattāpi puthuj‍janau, puthu vā ayaṃ visuṃyaiva saṅkhaṃ gatau, visaṃsaṭṭhau sīlasutādiguṇayuttaihi ariyaihi janauti puthuj‍janau| aivamaitaihi ‘‘assutavā puthuj‍janau’’ti dvīhi padaihi yai tai –

‘‘duvai  puthuj‍janā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

andhau puthuj‍janau aikau, kalyāṇaikau puthuj‍janau’’ti|| –

dvai puthuj‍janā vuttā, taisu andhaputhuj‍janau vuttau hautīti vaiditabbau|

yathābhūtaṃ nappajānātīti ‘‘idañ‍ca bhavaṅgacittaṃ aivaṃ āgantukaihi  upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ nāma hauti, aivaṃ vippamuttaṃ nāmā’’ti yathāsabhāvatau na jānāti| tasmāti yasmā na jānāti, tasmā| cittabhāvanā natthīti cittaṭṭhiti cittapariggahau natthi, natthibhāvainaiva ‘‘natthī’’ti vadāmīti dassaiti|

52. dutiyai  sutavāti sutasampan‍nau| vitthāratau panaittha assutavāti padassa paṭipakkhavasaina atthau vaiditabbau| ariyasāvakauti atthi ariyau na sāvakau, saiyyathāpi buddhā caiva pac‍caikabuddhā ca; atthi sāvakau na ariyau, saiyyathāpi gihī anāgataphalau; atthi naiva ariyau na sāvakau saiyyathāpi puthutitthiyā| atthi ariyaucaiva sāvakau ca, saiyyathāpi samaṇā sakyaputtiyā āgataphalā viñ‍ñātasāsanā| idha pana gihī vā hautu pabbajitau vā, yau kauci sutavāti aittha vuttassa atthassa vasaina sutasampan‍nau, ayaṃ ariyasāvakauti vaiditabbau| yathābhūtaṃ pajānātīti ‘‘aivamidaṃ bhavaṅgacittaṃ āgantukaihi upak‍kilaisaihi vippamuttaṃ hauti, aivaṃ upak‍kiliṭṭha’’nti yathāsabhāvatau jānāti| cittabhāvanā atthīti cittaṭṭhiti cittapariggahau atthi, atthibhāvainaiva ‘‘atthī’’ti vadāmīti dassaiti| imasmiṃ suttai balavavipassanā kathitā| kaici taruṇavipassanāti vadanti|

53. tatiyaṃ aṭṭhuppattiyaṃ kathitaṃ| katarāyaṃ pana aṭṭhuppattiyaṃ? aggikkhandhaupamasuttantaaṭṭhuppattiyaṃ| bhagavā kira aikasmiṃ samayai sāvatthiṃ upanissāya jaitavanamahāvihārai paṭivasati| buddhānañ‍ca yattha katthaci  paṭivasantānaṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ kic‍caṃ avijahitamaiva hauti| pañ‍ca hi buddhakic‍cāni – puraibhattakic‍caṃ, pacchābhattakic‍caṃ, purimayāmakic‍caṃ, majjhimayāmakic‍caṃ, pacchimayāmakic‍canti|

tatridaṃ puraibhattakic‍caṃ – bhagavā hi pātauva vuṭṭhāya upaṭṭhākānuggahatthaṃ sarīraphāsukatthañ‍ca mukhadhauvanādisarīraparikammaṃ  katvā yāva bhikkhācāravailā tāva vivittāsanai vītināmaitvā bhikkhācāravailāya nivāsaitvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā cīvaraṃ pārupitvā pattamādāya kadāci aikakauva, kadāci bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati kadāci pakatiyā, kadāci anaikaihi pāṭihāriyaihi vattamānaihi| saiyyathidaṃ – piṇḍāya pavisatau laukanāthassa puratau puratau gantvā mudugatavātā pathaviṃ saudhainti, valāhakā udakaphusitāni muñ‍cantā maggai raiṇuṃ vūpasamaitvā upari vitānaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhanti, aparai vātā pupphāni upasaṃharitvā maggai aukiranti, un‍natā  bhūmippadaisā aunamanti, aunatā un‍namanti, pādanikkhaipasamayai samāva bhūmi hauti, sukhasamphassāni padumapupphāni vā pādai sampaṭicchanti| indakhīlassa antau ṭhapitamattai dakkhiṇapādai sarīratau chabbaṇṇarasmiyau nikkhamitvā suvaṇṇarasapiñ‍jarāni viya citrapaṭaparikkhittāni viya ca pāsādakūṭāgārādīni alaṅkarauntiyau itau citau ca dhāvanti, hatthiassavihaṅgādayau sakasakaṭṭhānaisu ṭhitāyaiva madhurainākāraina saddaṃ karaunti, tathā bhairivīṇādīni tūriyāni manussānañ‍ca kāyūpagāni ābharaṇāni| taina sañ‍ñāṇaina manussā jānanti ‘‘aj‍ja bhagavā idha piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau’’ti| tai sunivatthā supārutā gandhapupphādīni ādāya gharā nikkhamitvā antaravīthiṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā bhagavantaṃ gandhapupphādīhi sak‍kac‍caṃ pūjaitvā  vanditvā ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, dasa bhikkhū, amhākaṃ vīsati, pañ‍ñāsaṃ…pai॰… sataṃ daithā’’ti yācitvā bhagavataupi pattaṃ gahaitvā āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaina paṭimānainti| bhagavā katabhattakic‍cau taisaṃ upanissayacittasantānāni aulaukaitvā tathā dhammaṃ daisaiti, yathā kaici saraṇagamanaisu patiṭṭhahanti, kaici pañ‍casu sīlaisu, kaici sautāpattisakadāgāmianāgāmiphalānaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ, kaici pabbajitvā aggaphalai arahattaiti| aivaṃ mahājanaṃ anuggahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ gacchati| tattha gantvā gandhamaṇḍalamāḷai pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisīdati bhikkhūnaṃ bhattakic‍capariyausānaṃ āgamayamānau| tatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhattakic‍capariyausānai upaṭṭhākau bhagavatau nivaidaiti| atha bhagavā gandhakuṭiṃ pavisati| idaṃ tāva puraibhattakic‍caṃ|

atha bhagavā aivaṃ katapuraibhattakic‍cau gandhakuṭiyā upaṭṭhānai nisīditvā pādai pakkhālaitvā pādapīṭhai ṭhatvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ auvadati – ‘‘bhikkhavai, appamādaina sampādaitha, dul‍labhau buddhuppādau laukasmiṃ, dul‍labhau manussattapaṭilābhau, dul‍labhā khaṇasampatti, dul‍labhā pabbaj‍jā, dul‍labhaṃ saddhammassavana’’nti| tattha kaici bhagavantaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ pucchanti| bhagavā taisaṃ cariyānurūpaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ  daiti| tatau sabbaipi bhagavantaṃ vanditvā attanau attanau rattiṭṭhānadivāṭṭhānāni gacchanti| kaici arañ‍ñaṃ, kaici rukkhamūlaṃ, kaici pabbatādīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ, kaici cātumahārājikabhavanaṃ…pai॰… kaici vasavattibhavananti| tatau bhagavā gandhakuṭiṃ pavisitvā sacai ākaṅkhati, dakkhiṇaina passaina satau sampajānau muhuttaṃ sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti| atha samassāsitakāyau uṭṭhahitvā dutiyabhāgai laukaṃ vaulaukaiti| tatiyabhāgai yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati, tattha mahājanau puraibhattaṃ  dānaṃ datvā pacchābhattaṃ sunivatthau supārutau gandhapupphādīni ādāya vihārai san‍nipatati| tatau bhagavā sampattaparisāya anurūpaina pāṭihāriyaina gantvā dhammasabhāyaṃ pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisaj‍ja dhammaṃ daisaiti kālayuttaṃ samayayuttaṃ, atha kālaṃ viditvā parisaṃ uyyaujaiti, manussā bhagavantaṃ vanditvā pak‍kamanti| idaṃ pacchābhattakic‍caṃ|

sau aivaṃ niṭṭhitapacchābhattakic‍cau sacai gattāni ausiñ‍citukāmau hauti, buddhāsanā uṭṭhāya nhānakauṭṭhakaṃ pavisitvā upaṭṭhākaina paṭiyāditaudakaina gattāni utuṃ gaṇhāpaiti| upaṭṭhākaupi buddhāsanaṃ ānaitvā gandhakuṭiparivaiṇai pañ‍ñapaiti| bhagavā surattadupaṭṭaṃ nivāsaitvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā uttarāsaṅgaṃ aikaṃsaṃ katvā tattha āgantvā nisīdati aikakauva muhuttaṃ paṭisal‍līnau, atha bhikkhū tatau tatau āgamma bhagavatau upaṭṭhānaṃ āgacchanti| tattha aikac‍cai pañhaṃ pucchanti, aikac‍cai kammaṭṭhānaṃ, aikac‍cai dhammassavanaṃ yācanti| bhagavā taisaṃ adhippāyaṃ sampādaintau purimayāmaṃ vitināmaiti| idaṃ purimayāmakic‍caṃ|

purimayāmakic‍capariyausānai pana bhikkhūsu bhagavantaṃ vanditvā pak‍kantaisu sakaladasasahassilaukadhātudaivatāyau aukāsaṃ labhamānā bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti yathābhisaṅkhataṃ antamasau caturakkharampi| bhagavā tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ pañhaṃ vissaj‍jaintau majjhimayāmaṃ vītināmaiti| idaṃ majjhimayāmakic‍caṃ|

pacchimayāmaṃ pana tayau kauṭṭhāsai katvā puraibhattatau paṭṭhāya nisaj‍jāpīḷitassa sarīrassa kilāsubhāvamaucanatthaṃ aikaṃ kauṭṭhāsaṃ caṅkamaina vītināmaiti, dutiyakauṭṭhāsai gandhakuṭiṃ pavisitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina satau sampajānau sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti| tatiyakauṭṭhāsai pac‍cuṭṭhāya nisīditvā purimabuddhānaṃ  santikai dānasīlādivasaina katādhikārapuggaladassanatthaṃ  buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaiti| idaṃ pacchimayāmakic‍caṃ|

tampi  divasaṃ bhagavā imasmiṃyaiva kic‍cai ṭhitau laukaṃ aulaukaintau idaṃ addasa – mayā kausalaraṭṭhai cārikaṃ carantaina aggikkhandhaina upamaitvā aikasmiṃ suttai daisitai saṭṭhi bhikkhū arahattaṃ pāpuṇissanti, saṭṭhimattānaṃ uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggacchissati, saṭṭhimattā gihibhāvaṃ gamissanti| tattha yai arahattaṃ pāpuṇissanti, tai yaṃkiñ‍ci dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā pāpuṇissantaiva| itaraisaṃ pana bhikkhūnaṃ saṅgahatthāya cārikaṃ caritukāmau hutvā, ‘‘ānanda, bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaihī’’ti āha|

thairau anuparivaiṇaṃ gantvā, ‘‘āvusau, satthā mahājanassa saṅgahatthāya cārikaṃ caritukāmau, gantukāmā āgacchathā’’ti āha| bhikkhū mahālābhaṃ labhitvā viya tuṭṭhamānasā ‘‘labhissāma vata mahājanassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa bhagavatau suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sarīraṃ aulaukaituṃ madhurañ‍ca dhammakathaṃ sautu’’nti paruḷhakaisā kaisai auhāraitvā malaggahitapattā pattai pacitvā kiliṭṭhacīvarā cīvarāni dhauvitvā gamanasaj‍jā ahaisuṃ| satthā aparicchin‍naina bhikkhusaṅghaina parivutau kausalaraṭṭhaṃ cārikāya nikkhantau gāmanigamapaṭipāṭiyā aikadivasaṃ gāvutaaḍḍhayaujanatigāvutayaujanaparamaṃ cārikaṃ carantau aikasmiṃ padaisai mahantaṃ susirarukkhaṃ agginā sampaj‍jalitaṃ disvā ‘‘imamaiva vatthuṃ katvā sattahi aṅgaihi paṭimaṇḍaitvā dhammadaisanaṃ kathaissāmī’’ti gamanaṃ pacchinditvā añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ upasaṅkamitvā nisaj‍jākāraṃ dassaisi| ānandatthairau satthu adhippāyaṃ  ñatvā ‘‘addhā kāraṇaṃ bhavissati, na akāraṇaina tathāgatā gamanaṃ pacchinditvā nisīdantī’’ti catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaisi| satthā nisīditvā bhikkhū āmantaitvā ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandha’’nti aggikkhandhaupamasuttantaṃ (a॰ ni॰ 7.72) daisaiti|

imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇai bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggañchi, saṭṭhimattā bhikkhū sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattiṃsu, saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu| tañhi vaiyyākaraṇaṃ sutvā saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ nāmakāyau santattau, nāmakāyai santattai karajakāyau santattau, karajakāyai santattai nidhānagataṃ uṇhaṃ  lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggañchi| saṭṭhimattā bhikkhū ‘‘duk‍karaṃ vata buddhasāsanai yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattā, saṭṭhimattā  bhikkhū satthu daisanābhimukhaṃ ñāṇaṃ paisaitvā saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pattā|

tattha yaisaṃ uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggañchi, tai pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| yai gihibhāvaṃ pattā, tai khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni maddantā vicariṃsu| yai arahattaṃ pattā, tai parisuddhasīlāva ahaisuṃ| satthu dhammadaisanā imaisaṃ tiṇṇampi saphalāva jātāti| arahattaṃ pattānaṃ tāva saphalā hautu, itaraisaṃ kathaṃ saphalā jātāti? taipi hi sacai imaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ na suṇaiyyuṃ, pamattāva hutvā ṭhānaṃ jahituṃ na sak‍kuṇaiyyuṃ| tatau naisaṃ taṃ pāpaṃ vaḍḍhamānaṃ apāyaisuyaiva saṃsīdāpaiyya| imaṃ pana daisanaṃ sutvā jātasaṃvaigā ṭhānaṃ jahitvā sāmaṇairabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitā dasa sīlāni pūraitvā yaunisau manasikārai yuttappayuttā kaici sautāpan‍nā kaici sakadāgāminau kaici anāgāminau ahaisuṃ, kaici daivalaukai nibbattiṃsu, aivaṃ pārājikāpan‍nānampi saphalā ahausi| itarai pana sacai imaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ na suṇaiyyuṃ, gacchantai gacchantai kālai anupubbaina saṅghādisaisampi pārājikampi pāpuṇitvā apāyaisuyaiva uppaj‍jitvā mahādukkhaṃ anubhavaiyyuṃ| imaṃ pana daisanaṃ sutvā ‘‘ahau sal‍laikhitaṃ buddhasāsanaṃ, na sak‍kā amhaihi yāvajīvaṃ imaṃ paṭipattiṃ pūraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya upāsakadhammaṃ pūraitvā dukkhā muc‍cissāmā’’ti gihibhāvaṃ upagamiṃsu| tai tīsu saraṇaisu patiṭṭhāya pañ‍ca sīlāni rakkhitvā upāsakadhammaṃ pūraitvā kaici sautāpan‍nā kaici sakadāgāminau kaici anāgāminau jātā, kaici daivalaukai nibbattāti| aivaṃ taisampi saphalāva ahausi|

imaṃ pana satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā daivasaṅghā yaihipi sutā, yaihipi na sutā, sabbaisaṃyaiva āraucaintā vicariṃsu| bhikkhū sutvā sutvā ‘‘duk‍karaṃ, bhau, buddhānaṃ sāsanai yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti aikakkhaṇainaiva dasapi bhikkhū vīsatipi saṭṭhipi satampi sahassampi bhikkhū gihī haunti| satthā yathāruciyā cārikaṃ caritvā puna jaitavanamaiva āgantvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavai, tathāgatau cārikaṃ caramānau ciraṃ ākiṇṇau vihāsi, icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhamāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ, nāmhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| aḍḍhamāsaṃ  aikībhāvaina vītināmaitvā paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau ānandatthairaina saddhiṃ vihāracārikaṃ caramānau aulaukitaulaukitaṭṭhānai tanubhūtaṃ  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ disvā jānantauyaiva thairaṃ pucchi – ‘‘ānanda, añ‍ñasmiṃ kālai tathāgatai cārikaṃ caritvā jaitavanaṃ āgatai sakalavihārau kāsāvapaj‍jautau isivātappaṭivātau hauti, idāni pana tanubhūtau bhikkhusaṅghau dissati, yaibhuyyaina ca uppaṇḍupaṇḍukajātā  bhikkhū, kiṃ nu khau aita’’nti? aitarahi bhagavā tumhākaṃ aggikkhandhaupamadhammadaisanaṃ kathitakālatau paṭṭhāya bhikkhū saṃvaigappattā hutvā ‘‘mayaṃ aitaṃ dhammaṃ sabbappakāraina paripūraituṃ na sakkhissāma, asammāvattantānañ‍ca janassa saddhādaiyyaṃ paribhuñ‍jituṃ ayutta’’nti gihibhāvaṃ saṅkamantīti|

tasmiṃ khaṇai bhagavatau dhammasaṃvaigau uppaj‍ji| tatau thairaṃ āha – ‘‘mayi paṭisal‍lānai vītināmaintai na kauci mama puttānaṃ aikaṃ assāsaṭṭhānaṃ kathaisi| sāgarassa hi autaraṇatitthāni viya bahūni imasmiṃ sāsanai assāsakāraṇāni| gacchānanda, gandhakuṭiparivaiṇai buddhāsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātaihī’’ti| thairau tathā akāsi| satthā buddhāsanavaragatau bhikkhū āmantaitvā, ‘‘bhikkhavai, maittāya sabbapubbabhāgau nāma naiva appanā, na upacārau, sattānaṃ hitapharaṇamattamaivā’’ti vatvā imissā aṭṭhuppattiyā imaṃ cūḷaccharāsaṅghātasuttaṃ daisaisi|

tattha accharāsaṅghātamattanti accharāpaharaṇamattaṃ, dvai aṅguliyau paharitvā saddakaraṇamattanti atthau| maittācittanti sabbasattānaṃ hitapharaṇacittaṃ| āsaivatīti kathaṃ āsaivati? āvaj‍jaintau āsaivati, jānantau āsaivati, passantau āsaivati, pac‍cavaikkhantau āsaivati, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahantau āsaivati, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau āsaivati, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau āsaivati, satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaintau āsaivati, cittaṃ samādahantau āsaivati, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau āsaivati, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau āsaivati, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau āsaivati, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau āsaivati, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvaintau āsaivati, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau āsaivatīti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.2)| idha pana maittāpubbabhāgaina hitapharaṇappavattanamattainaiva āsaivatīti vaiditabbau|

arittajjhānauti  atucchajjhānau aparic‍cattajjhānau vā| viharatīti iriyati pavattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati viharatīti| iminā padaina maittaṃ āsaivantassa bhikkhunau iriyāpathavihārau kathitau| satthusāsanakarauti satthu anusāsanikarau| auvādapatikarauti auvādakārakau| aittha ca sakiṃvacanaṃ auvādau, punappunavacanaṃ anusāsanī| sammukhāvacanampi  auvādau, paisaitvā parammukhāvacanaṃ, anusāsanī| autiṇṇai vatthusmiṃ vacanaṃ auvādau, autiṇṇai vā anautiṇṇai vā vatthusmiṃ tantiṭhapanavasaina vacanaṃ anusāsanī| aivaṃ visaisau vaiditabbau| paramatthatau pana auvādauti vā anusāsanīti vā aisai aikai aikaṭṭhai samai samabhāgai taj‍jātai tañ‍ñaivāti| aittha ca ‘‘accharāsaṅghātamattampi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu maittācittaṃ āsaivatī’’ti idamaiva satthusāsanañ‍caiva auvādau ca, tassa karaṇatau aisa sāsanakarau auvādapatikarauti vaiditabbau|

amaughanti atucchaṃ| raṭṭhapiṇḍanti ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya raṭṭhaṃ nissāya pabbajitaina paraisaṃ gaihatau paṭiladdhattā piṇḍapātau raṭṭhapiṇḍau nāma vuc‍cati| paribhuñ‍jatīti cattārau paribhaugā thaiyyaparibhaugau iṇaparibhaugau dāyaj‍japaribhaugau sāmiparibhaugauti| tattha dussīlassa paribhaugau thaiyyaparibhaugau nāma| sīlavatau apac‍cavaikkhitaparibhaugau iṇaparibhaugau nāma| sattan‍naṃ saikkhānaṃ paribhaugau dāyaj‍japaribhaugā nāma| khīṇāsavassa paribhaugau sāmiparibhaugau nāma| tattha  imassa bhikkhunau ayaṃ raṭṭhapiṇḍaparibhaugau dvīhi kāraṇaihi amaughau hauti| accharāsaṅghātamattampi maittācittaṃ āsaivantau bhikkhu raṭṭhapiṇḍassa sāmikau hutvā, aṇaṇau hutvā, dāyādau hutvā paribhuñ‍jatītipissa amaughau raṭṭhapiṇḍaparibhaugau| accharāsaṅghātamattampi maittaṃ āsaivantassa bhikkhunau din‍nadānaṃ mahaṭṭhiyaṃ hauti mahapphalaṃ mahānisaṃsaṃ mahājutikaṃ mahāvipphārantipissa amaughau raṭṭhapiṇḍaparibhaugau| kau pana vādau yai naṃ bahulīkarauntīti yai pana imaṃ maittācittaṃ bahulaṃ āsaivanti bhāvainti punappunaṃ karaunti, tai amaughaṃ raṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ paribhuñ‍jantīti aittha vattabbamaiva kiṃ? aivarūpā hi bhikkhū raṭṭhapiṇḍassa sāminau aṇaṇā dāyādā hutvā paribhuñ‍jantīti|

54-55. catutthai  bhāvaitīti uppādaiti vaḍḍhaiti| pañ‍camai manasi karautīti manasmiṃ karauti| saisaṃ imaisu dvīsupi tatiyai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| yau hi āsaivati, ayamaiva bhāvaiti, ayaṃ manasi karauti| yaina cittaina āsaivati, tainaiva bhāvaiti, taina manasi karauti| sammāsambuddhau pana yāya dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā daisanāvilāsappattau nāma hauti, tassā suppaṭividdhattā attanau daisanāvilāsaṃ dhammissariyataṃ paṭisambhidāpabhaidakusalataṃ appaṭihatasabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca nissāya aikakkhaṇai uppan‍naṃ aikacittamaiva tīhi kauṭṭhāsaihi vibhajitvā dassaisīti|

56. chaṭṭhai  yai kaicīti aniyāmitavacanaṃ| akusalāti taisaṃ niyāmitavacanaṃ| aittāvatā sabbākusalā asaisatau pariyādin‍nā haunti| akusalabhāgiyā  akusalapakkhikāti akusalānamaivaitaṃ nāmaṃ| akusalāyaiva hi aikac‍cai akusalaṃ sahajātavasaina, aikac‍cai upanissayavasaina bhajanti caiva, taisañ‍ca pakkhā bhavantīti ‘‘akusalabhāgiyā akusalapakkhikā’’ti vuc‍canti| sabbaitai manaupubbaṅgamāti manau pubbaṃ paṭhamataraṃ gacchati aitaisanti manaupubbaṅgamā| aitai hi kiñ‍cāpi manaina saddhiṃ aikuppādā aikavatthukā aikaniraudhā aikārammaṇā ca haunti| yasmā pana taisaṃ manau uppādakau kārakau janakau samuṭṭhāpakau nibbattakau, tasmā manaupubbaṅgamā nāma haunti|

paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jatīti yathā nāma ‘‘rājā nikkhantau’’ti vuttai ‘‘rājāyaiva nikkhantau, saisā rājasainā nikkhantā anikkhantā’’ti pucchitabbakāraṇaṃ natthi, sabbā nikkhantātaiva pañ‍ñāyanti, aivamaiva manau uppan‍nauti vuttakālatau paṭṭhāya avasaisā sahajātasaṃsaṭṭhasampayuttā uppan‍nā na uppan‍nāti pucchitabbakāraṇaṃ natthi, sabbai tai uppan‍nā tvaiva pañ‍ñāyanti| aitamatthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca taihi saṃsaṭṭhasampayuttau aikuppādaikaniraudhaupi samānau manau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jatīti vuttau| anvadaivāti anudaiva, sahaiva aikatauyaivāti atthau| byañ‍janacchāyaṃ pana gahaitvā paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā caitasikāti na gahaitabbaṃ| atthau hi paṭisaraṇaṃ, na byañ‍janaṃ| ‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā, manausaiṭṭhā manaumayā’’ti gāthāyapi aisaiva nayau|

57. sattamai kusalāti catubhūmakāpi kusalā dhammā kathitā| saisaṃ chaṭṭhai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

58. aṭṭhamai  yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, pamādauti aittha, bhikkhavaiti ālapanaṃ, yathā ayaṃ pamādauti atthau| pamādauti pamaj‍janākārau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘tattha katamau pamādau? kāyaduc‍caritai vā vacīduc‍caritai vā manauduc‍caritai vā pañ‍casu vā kāmaguṇaisu cittassa vaussaggau vaussaggānuppadānaṃ kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya asak‍kac‍cakiriyatā asātac‍cakiriyatā anaṭṭhitakiriyatā aulīnavuttitā nikkhittachandatā nikkhittadhuratā anadhiṭṭhānaṃ ananuyaugau anāsaivanā abhāvanā abahulīkammaṃ| yau aivarūpau pamādau pamaj‍janā pamaj‍jitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pamādau’’ti (vibha॰ 846)|

uppan‍nā ca kusalā dhammā parihāyantīti idaṃ jhānavipassanānaṃ vasaina vuttaṃ| maggaphalānaṃ pana sakiṃ uppan‍nānaṃ puna parihānaṃ nāma natthi|

59. navamai appamādau pamādassa paṭipakkhavasaina vitthāratau vaiditabbau|

60. dasamai kausaj‍janti kusītabhāvau| saisaṃ vuttanayamaivāti|

accharāsaṅghātavaggavaṇṇanā|



7. vīriyārambhādivaggavaṇṇanā

61. sattamassa  paṭhamai vīriyārambhauti catukic‍cassa sammappadhānavīriyassa ārambhau, āraddhapaggahitaparipuṇṇavīriyatāti atthau|

62. dutiyai  mahicchatāti mahālaubhau| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘tattha katamā mahicchatā? itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi pañ‍cahi vā kāmaguṇaihi asantuṭṭhassa bhiyyaukamyatā, yā aivarūpā icchā icchāgatā mahicchatā rāgau sārāgau cittassa sārāgau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati mahicchatā’’ti (vibha॰ 850)|

63. tatiyai  appicchatāti alaubhau| appicchassāti anicchassa| aittha hi byañ‍janaṃ sāvasaisaṃ viya, atthau pana niravasaisau| na hi appamattikāya icchāya atthibhāvaina sau appicchauti vuttau, icchāya pana abhāvaina punappunaṃ āsaivitassa alaubhassaiva bhāvaina appicchauti vuttau|

apicaittha atricchatā, pāpicchatā, mahicchatā appicchatāti ayaṃ bhaidau vaiditabbau| tattha sakalābhai atittassa paralābhai patthanā atricchatā nāma, yāya saman‍nāgatassa aikabhājanai pak‍kapūvaipi attanau pattai patitai na supak‍kau viya khuddakau ca viya khāyati, svaiva pana parassa pattai pakkhittau supak‍kau viya mahantau viya ca khāyati| asantaguṇasambhāvanatā pana paṭiggahaṇai ca amattañ‍ñutā pāpicchatā nāma, sā ‘‘idhaikac‍cau assaddhau samānau saddhauti maṃ janau jānātū’’tiādinā nayaina abhidhammai āgatāyaiva, tāya saman‍nāgatau puggalau kauhañ‍ñai patiṭṭhāti| santaguṇasambhāvanatā pana paṭiggahaṇai ca amattañ‍ñutā mahicchatā nāma, sāpi ‘‘idhaikac‍cau saddhau samānau saddhauti maṃ janau jānātūti icchati, sīlavā samānau sīlavāti maṃ  janau jānātū’’ti iminā nayaina āgatāyaiva| tāya saman‍nāgatau puggalau dussantappayau hauti, vijātamātāpissa cittaṃ gahaituṃ na sak‍kauti| tainaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘aggikkhandhau samuddau ca, mahicchau cāpi puggalau|

sakaṭaina pac‍cayai daintu, tayaupaitai atappayā’’ti||

santaguṇanigūhanatā  pana paṭiggahaṇai ca mattañ‍ñutā appicchatā nāma, tāya saman‍nāgatau puggalau attani vij‍jamānampi guṇaṃ paṭicchādaitukāmatāya saddhau samānau ‘‘saddhauti maṃ janau jānātū’’ti na icchati| sīlavā, pavivittau, bahussutau, āraddhavīriyau, samādhisampan‍nau, pañ‍ñavā, khīṇāsavau  samānau ‘‘khīṇāsavauti maṃ janau jānātū’’ti na icchati saiyyathāpi majjhantikatthairau|

thairau kira mahākhīṇāsavau ahausi, pattacīvaraṃ panassa pādamattamaiva agghati| sau asaukassa dhammarañ‍ñau vihāramahadivasai saṅghatthairau ahausi| athassa atilūkhabhāvaṃ disvā manussā, ‘‘bhantai, thaukaṃ bahi hauthā’’ti āhaṃsu| thairau ‘‘mādisai khīṇāsavai rañ‍ñau saṅgahaṃ akarauntai añ‍ñau kau karissatī’’ti pathaviyaṃ nimuj‍jitvā saṅghatthairassa ukkhittapiṇḍaṃ gaṇhantauyaiva ummuj‍ji| aivaṃ khīṇāsavau samānau ‘‘khīṇāsavauti maṃ janau jānātū’’ti na icchati| aivaṃ appicchau ca pana bhikkhu anuppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ thāvaraṃ karauti, dāyakānaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaiti| yathā yathā hi sau attanau appicchatāya appaṃ gaṇhāti, tathā tathā tassa vattai pasan‍nā manussā bahū dainti|

aparaupi catubbidhau appicchau – pac‍cayaappicchau, dhutaṅgaappicchau, pariyattiappicchau, adhigamaappicchauti| tattha catūsu pac‍cayaisu appicchau pac‍cayaappicchau nāma| sau dāyakassa vasaṃ jānāti, daiyyadhammassa vasaṃ jānāti, attanau thāmaṃ jānāti| yadi hi daiyyadhammau bahu hauti, dāyakau appamattakaṃ dātukāmau, dāyakassa vasaina appaṃ gaṇhāti| daiyyadhammau appau, dāyakau bahuṃ dātukāmau, daiyyadhammassa vasaina appaṃ gaṇhāti| daiyyadhammaupi bahu, dāyakaupi bahuṃ dātukāmau, attanau thāmaṃ ñatvā pamāṇainaiva gaṇhāti|

dhutaṅgasamādānassa  attani atthibhāvaṃ najānāpaitukāmau dhutaṅgaappicchau nāma| tassa vibhāvanatthaṃ imāni vatthūni – sausānikamahākumāratthairau  kira saṭṭhi vassāni susānai vasi, añ‍ñau aikabhikkhupi na añ‍ñāsi| tainaivāha –

‘‘susānai  saṭṭhi vassāni, abbaukiṇṇaṃ vasāmahaṃ|

dutiyau maṃ na jānaiyya, ahau sausānikuttamau’’ti||

caitiyapabbatai dvai bhātikatthairā vasiṃsu| kaniṭṭhau upaṭṭhākaina paisitaṃ ucchukhaṇḍikaṃ gahaitvā jaiṭṭhassa santikaṃ agamāsi ‘‘paribhaugaṃ, bhantai, karauthā’’ti| thairassa ca bhattakic‍caṃ katvā mukhavikkhālanakālau ahausi| sau ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau’’ti āha| kac‍ci, bhantai, aikāsanikatthāti? āharāvusau, ucchukhaṇḍikanti pañ‍ñāsa vassāni aikāsanikau samānaupi dhutaṅgaṃ nigūhamānau paribhaugaṃ katvā mukhaṃ vikkhālaitvā puna dhutaṅgaṃ adhiṭṭhāya gatau|

yau pana sākaitatissatthairau viya bahussutabhāvaṃ jānāpaituṃ na icchati, ayaṃ pariyattiappicchau nāma| thairau kira ‘‘khaṇau natthīti uddaisaparipucchāsu aukāsaṃ akarauntau kadā maraṇakkhaṇaṃ, bhantai, labhissathā’’ti cauditau gaṇaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā kaṇikāravālikasamuddavihāraṃ gatau| tattha antauvassaṃ thairanavamajjhimānaṃ upakārau hutvā mahāpavāraṇāya upausathadivasai dhammakathāya janapadaṃ khaubhaitvā gatau|

yau pana sautāpan‍nādīsu añ‍ñatarau hutvā sautāpan‍nādibhāvaṃ jānāpaituṃ na icchati, ayaṃ adhigamappicchau nāma tayau kulaputtā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.325) viya ghaṭīkārakumbhakārau (ma॰ ni॰ 2.282 ādayau) viya ca| imasmiṃ panatthai laddhāsaivanaina balavaalaubhaina saman‍nāgatau saikkhaupi puthuj‍janaupi appicchauti vaiditabbau|

64. catutthai asantuṭṭhitāti asantuṭṭhai puggalai saivantassa bhajantassa payirupāsantassa uppan‍nau asantausasaṅkhātau laubhau|

65. pañ‍camai  santuṭṭhitāti santuṭṭhai puggalai saivantassa bhajantassa payirupāsantassa uppan‍nau  alaubhasaṅkhātau santausau| santuṭṭhassāti itarītarapac‍cayasantausaina saman‍nāgatassa| sau panaisa santausau  dvādasavidhau hauti| saiyyathidaṃ – cīvarai yathālābhasantausau, yathābalasantausau, yathāsāruppasantausauti tividhau| aivaṃ piṇḍapātādīsu|

tassāyaṃ pabhaidasaṃvaṇṇanā – idha bhikkhu cīvaraṃ labhati sundaraṃ vā asundaraṃ vā| sau tainaiva yāpaiti añ‍ñaṃ na patthaiti, labhantaupi na gaṇhāti| ayamassa cīvarai yathālābhasantausau| atha pana pakatidubbalau vā hauti ābādhajarābhibhūtau vā, garuṃ cīvaraṃ pārupantau kilamati| sau sabhāgaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ taṃ parivattaitvā lahukaina yāpaintaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa cīvarai yathābalasantausau| aparau paṇītapac‍cayalābhī hauti| sau paṭṭacīvarādīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ mahagghacīvaraṃ bahūni vā pana cīvarāni labhitvā ‘‘idaṃ thairānaṃ cirapabbajitānaṃ, idaṃ bahussutānaṃ anurūpaṃ, idaṃ gilānānaṃ, idaṃ appalābhānaṃ hautū’’ti datvā taisaṃ purāṇacīvaraṃ vā saṅkārakūṭāditau vā nantakāni uc‍cinitvā taihi saṅghāṭiṃ katvā dhāraintaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa cīvarai yathāsāruppasantausau|

idha pana bhikkhu piṇḍapātaṃ labhati lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, sau tainaiva yāpaiti, añ‍ñaṃ na patthaiti, labhantaupi na gaṇhāti| ayamassa piṇḍapātai yathālābhasantausau| yau pana attanau pakativiruddhaṃ vā byādhiviruddhaṃ vā piṇḍapātaṃ labhati, yainassa paribhuttaina aphāsu hauti, sau sabhāgassa bhikkhunau taṃ datvā tassa hatthatau sappāyabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauntaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa piṇḍapātai yathābalasantausau| aparau bahuṃ paṇītaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ labhati| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ viya thairacirapabbajitabahussutaappalābhagilānānaṃ  datvā taisaṃ vā saisakaṃ piṇḍāya vā caritvā missakāhāraṃ bhuñ‍jantaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa piṇḍapātai yathāsāruppasantausau|

idha pana bhikkhu saināsanaṃ labhati manāpaṃ vā amanāpaṃ vā, sau taina naiva saumanassaṃ na daumanassaṃ uppādaiti, antamasau tiṇasanthārakaināpi yathāladdhainaiva  tussati| ayamassa saināsanai yathālābhasantausau| yau pana attanau pakativiruddhaṃ vā byādhiviruddhaṃ vā saināsanaṃ labhati, yatthassa vasatau aphāsu hauti, sau taṃ sabhāgassa bhikkhunau datvā tassa santakai sappāyasaināsanai vasantaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa saināsanai yathābalasantausau|

aparau  mahāpuñ‍ñau laiṇamaṇḍapakūṭāgārādīni bahūni paṇītasaināsanāni labhati| sau tāni cīvarādīni viya thairacirapabbajitabahussutaappalābhagilānānaṃ datvā yattha katthaci vasantaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa saināsanai yathāsāruppasantausau| yaupi ‘‘uttamasaināsanaṃ nāma pamādaṭṭhānaṃ, tattha nisin‍nassa thinamiddhaṃ auk‍kamati, niddābhibhūtassa puna paṭibujjhatau pāpavitak‍kā pātubhavantī’’ti paṭisañ‍cikkhitvā tādisaṃ saināsanaṃ pattampi na sampaṭicchati, sau taṃ paṭikkhipitvā abbhaukāsarukkhamūlādīsu vasantaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayampissa saināsanai yathāsāruppasantausau|

idha pana bhikkhu bhaisaj‍jaṃ labhati lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, sau yaṃ labhati, tainaiva tussati, añ‍ñaṃ na patthaiti, labhantaupi na gaṇhāti| ayamassa gilānapac‍cayai yathālābhasantausau| yau pana tailaina atthikau phāṇitaṃ labhati, sau taṃ sabhāgassa bhikkhunau datvā tassa hatthatau tailaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñadaiva vā pariyaisitvā bhaisaj‍jaṃ karauntaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa gilānapac‍cayai yathābalasantausau|

aparau mahāpuñ‍ñau bahuṃ tailamadhuphāṇitādipaṇītabhaisaj‍jaṃ labhati| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ viya thairacirapabbajitabahussutaappalābhagilānānaṃ datvā taisaṃ ābhataina yaina kainaci  yāpaintaupi santuṭṭhauva hauti| yau pana aikasmiṃ bhājanai muttaharītakaṃ ṭhapaitvā aikasmiṃ catumadhuraṃ – ‘‘gaṇhatha, bhantai, yadicchaka’’nti vuc‍camānau ‘‘sacassa taisu añ‍ñatarainapi raugau vūpasammati, atha muttaharītakaṃ nāma buddhādīhi vaṇṇita’’nti catumadhuraṃ paṭikkhipitvā muttaharītakaina bhaisaj‍jaṃ karauntaupi paramasantuṭṭhauva hauti| ayamassa gilānapac‍cayai yathāsāruppasantausau| imaisaṃ pana pac‍caikapac‍cayaisu tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇaṃ santausānaṃ yathāsāruppasantausauva aggau|

66-67. chaṭṭhasattamaisu  ayaunisaumanasikārayaunisaumanasikārā haiṭṭhā vuttalakkhaṇāva| saisamaittha uttānatthamaivāti|

68. aṭṭhamai asampajañ‍ñanti asampajānabhāvau, mauhassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| asampajānassāti ajānantassa sammuḷhassa|

69. navamai  sampajañ‍ñanti sampajānabhāvau, pañ‍ñāyaitaṃ nāmaṃ| sampajānassāti sampajānantassa|

70. dasamai pāpamittatāti yassa pāpā lāmakā mittā, sau pāpamittau| pāpamittassa bhāvau pāpamittatā, tainākāraina pavattānaṃ catun‍naṃ khandhānamaivaitaṃ nāmaṃ| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘tattha katamā pāpamittatā? yai tai puggalā assaddhā dussīlā appassutā maccharinau duppañ‍ñā| yā taisaṃ saivanā  nisaivanā saṃsaivanā bhajanā sambhajanā bhatti sambhatti sampavaṅkatā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati pāpamittatā’’ti (vibha॰ 901)|

vīriyārambhādivaggavaṇṇanā|



8. kalyāṇamittatādivaggavaṇṇanā

71. aṭṭhamassa  paṭhamai kalyāṇamittatāti kalyāṇā mittā assāti kalyāṇamittau, tassa bhāvau kalyāṇamittatā| saisaṃ vuttapaṭipakkhanayaina vaiditabbaṃ|

72-73. dutiyai anuyaugauti yaugau payaugau| ananuyaugauti ayaugau appayaugau| anuyaugāti anuyaugaina| ananuyaugāti ananuyaugaina| kusalānaṃ dhammānanti catubhūmakakusaladhammānaṃ| tatiyaṃ uttānatthamaiva|

74. catutthai baujjhaṅgāti bujjhanakasattassa aṅgabhūtā satta dhammā| yāya vā dhammasāmaggiyā sau bujjhati, sammauhaniddātau vā vuṭṭhāti, catusac‍cadhammaṃ  vā sacchikarauti| tassā baudhiyā aṅgabhūtātipi baujjhaṅgā| ‘‘baujjhaṅgāti kainaṭṭhaina baujjhaṅgā? bujjhantīti baujjhaṅgā, anubujjhantīti baujjhaṅgā, paṭibujjhantīti baujjhaṅgā, sambujjhantīti baujjhaṅgā, baudhāya saṃvattantīti baujjhaṅgā’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.17)| aivaṃ panaitaṃ padaṃ vibhattamaiva|

75. pañ‍camai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantīti iminā padaina baujjhaṅgānaṃ yāthāvasarasabhūmi nāma kathitā| sā panaisā catubbidhā hauti – vipassanā, vipassanāpādakajjhānaṃ, maggau, phalanti| tattha vipassanāya uppaj‍janakālai baujjhaṅgā kāmāvacarā haunti, vipassanāpādakajjhānamhi uppaj‍janakālai rūpāvacaraarūpāvacarā, maggaphalaisu uppaj‍janakālai laukuttarā| iti imasmiṃ suttai baujjhaṅgā catubhūmakā kathitā|

76. chaṭṭhassa aṭṭhuppattikau nikkhaipau| aṭṭhuppattiyaṃ haitaṃ nikkhittaṃ, sambahulā kira bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā| taisaṃ antarai bandhulamal‍lasaināpatiṃ ārabbha ayaṃ kathā udapādi, ‘‘āvusau, asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai bahuñātikaṃ ahausi bahupakkhaṃ, idāni appañātikaṃ appapakkhaṃ jāta’’nti| atha bhagavā taisaṃ cittācāraṃ ñatvā ‘‘mayi gatai mahatī daisanā bhavissatī’’ti ñatvā gandhakuṭitau nikkhamma dhammasabhāyaṃ pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisīditvā  ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā’’ti āha| bhagavā añ‍ñā gāmanigamādikathā natthi, asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai bahuñātikaṃ ahausi bahupakkhaṃ, idāni appañātikaṃ appapakkhaṃ jātanti vadantā nisin‍namhāti| satthā imissā aṭṭhuppattiyā appamattikā aisā, bhikkhavai, parihānīti idaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi|

tattha appamattikāti parittā parittappamāṇā| aitāya hi parihāniyā saggatau vā maggatau vā parihāni nāma natthi, diṭṭhadhammikaparihānimattamaiva aitanti āha| aitaṃ patikiṭṭhanti aitaṃ pacchimaṃ aitaṃ lāmakaṃ| yadidaṃ pañ‍ñāparihānīti yā aisā mama sāsanai kammassakatapañ‍ñāya jhānapañ‍ñāya vipassanāpañ‍ñāya maggapañ‍ñāya phalapañ‍ñāya ca parihāni, aisā pacchimā, aisā lāmakā, aisā chaḍḍanīyāti atthau|

77. sattamampi  aṭṭhuppattiyamaiva kathitaṃ| dhammasabhāyaṃ kira nisin‍naisu bhikkhūsu aikac‍cai aivaṃ āhaṃsu – ‘‘asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai appañātikaṃ appapakkhaṃ ahausi, idāni taṃ bahuñātikaṃ bahupakkhaṃ jāta’’nti| kaṃ sandhāya aivamāhaṃsūti? visākhaṃ upāsikaṃ vaisālikai ca licchavī| satthā taisaṃ cittācāraṃ ñatvā purimanayainaiva āgantvā dhammāsanai nisin‍nau ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā’’ti pucchi| tai yathābhūtaṃ kathayiṃsu| satthā imissā aṭṭhuppattiyā imaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi| tattha appamattikāti taṃ sampattiṃ nissāya saggaṃ vā maggaṃ vā sampattānaṃ abhāvatau parittā| yadidaṃ pañ‍ñāvuddhīti kammassakatapañ‍ñādīnaṃ vuddhi| tasmāti yasmā ñātīnaṃ vuddhi nāma diṭṭhadhammikamattā appā parittā, sā saggaṃ vā maggaṃ vā pāpaituṃ asamatthā, tasmā| pañ‍ñāvuddhiyāti kammassakatādipañ‍ñāya vuddhiyā|

78. aṭṭhamampi aṭṭhuppattiyamaiva kathitaṃ| sambahulā kira bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā mahādhanasaiṭṭhiputtaṃ ārabbha ‘‘asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai mahābhaugaṃ mahāhirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ ahausi, taṃ idāni appabhaugaṃ jāta’’nti kathayiṃsu| satthā purimanayainaiva āgantvā taisaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā imaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi|

79. navamampi aṭṭhuppattiyamaiva vuttaṃ| dhammasabhāyaṃ kira san‍nisin‍nā bhikkhū kākavaliyasaiṭṭhiñ‍ca  puṇṇasaiṭṭhiñ‍ca ārabbha ‘‘asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai appabhaugaṃ ahausi, taṃ idāni mahābhaugaṃ jāta’’nti kathayiṃsu| satthā purimanayainaiva āgantvā taisaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā imaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi| saisaṃ imaisu dvīsupi haiṭṭhā vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

80. dasamampi  aṭṭhuppattiyaṃ vuttaṃ| dhammasabhāyaṃ kira bhikkhū kausalamahārājānaṃ ārabbha ‘‘asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai mahāyasaṃ mahāparivāraṃ ahausi, idāni appayasaṃ appaparivāraṃ jāta’’nti kathayiṃsu| bhagavā purimanayainaiva āgantvā taisaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā imaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ ārabhi| saisaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbanti|

kalyāṇamittatādivaggavaṇṇanā|



9. pamādādivaggavaṇṇanā

81. navamassāpi  paṭhamaṃ aṭṭhuppattiyamaiva kathitaṃ| sambahulā kira bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ nisin‍nā kumbhaghausakaṃ ārabbha ‘‘asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ pubbai appayasaṃ appaparivāraṃ ahausi, idāni mahāyasaṃ mahāparivāraṃ jāta’’nti kathayiṃsu| satthā purimanayainaiva āgantvā taisaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā imaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau|

82. dutiyādīsu mahatau anatthāyāti mahantassa anatthassa atthāya| saisamaittha uttānamaivāti|

pamādādivaggavaṇṇanā|



10. dutiyapamādādivaggavaṇṇanā

98. dasamai  ajjhattikanti niyakajjhattavasaina ajjhattikaṃ| aṅganti kāraṇaṃ| iti karitvāti aivaṃ katvā| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ attanau santānai samuṭṭhitaṃ kāraṇanti katvā na añ‍ñaṃ aikaṃ kāraṇampi samanupassāmīti|

110-114. bāhiranti  ajjhattasantānatau bahi bhavaṃ| saddhammassāti suddhammassa, sāsanassāti atthau| sammausāyāti vināsāya| antaradhānāyāti apañ‍ñāṇatthāya|

115. ṭhitiyāti ciraṭṭhitatthaṃ| asammausāya anantaradhānāyāti vuttapaṭipakkhanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| saisamaittha catuk‍kauṭikai vuttanayamaiva|

130. itau paraisu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintītiādīsu suttantapariyāyaina tāva dasa kusalakammapathā dhammau, dasa akusalakammapathā adhammau| tathā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggauti sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyadhammā dhammau nāma; tayau satipaṭṭhānā tayau  sammappadhānā tayau iddhipādā cha indriyāni cha balāni aṭṭha baujjhaṅgā navaṅgikau maggauti ca cattārau upādānā pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni satta anusayā aṭṭha micchattāni ca ayaṃ adhammau|

tattha yaṃkiñ‍ci aikaṃ adhammakauṭṭhāsaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘imaṃ adhammaṃ dhammauti karissāma, aivaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyakulaṃ niyyānikaṃ bhavissati, mayaṃ ca laukai pākaṭā bhavissāmā’’ti taṃ adhammaṃ ‘‘dhammau aya’’nti kathayantā adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti nāma| tathaiva dhammakauṭṭhāsaisu aikaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ adhammau’’ti kathaintā dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti nāma| vinayapariyāyaina pana bhūtaina vatthunā caudaitvā sāraitvā yathāpaṭiñ‍ñāya kattabbaṃ kammaṃ dhammau nāma, abhūtaina vatthunā acaudaitvā asāraitvā apaṭiñ‍ñāya kattabbaṃ kammaṃ adhammau nāma|

suttantapariyāyaina  rāgavinayau dausavinayau mauhavinayau saṃvarau pahānaṃ paṭisaṅkhāti ayaṃ vinayau nāma, rāgādīnaṃ avinayau asaṃvarau appahānaṃ apaṭisaṅkhāti ayaṃ  avinayau nāma| vinayapariyāyaina vatthusampatti, ñattisampatti, anussāvanasampatti, sīmāsampatti, parisasampattīti ayaṃ vinayau nāma| vatthuvipatti, ñattivipatti, anussāvanavipatti, sīmāvipatti parisavipattīti ayaṃ avinayau nāma|

suttantapariyāyaina cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā…pai॰… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggauti idaṃ bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina; tayau satipaṭṭhānā tayau sammappadhānā tayau iddhipādā cha indriyāni cha balāni aṭṭha baujjhaṅgā navaṅgikau maggauti idaṃ abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina| vinayapariyāyaina cattārau pārājikā tairasa saṅghādisaisā dvai aniyatā tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyāti idaṃ bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina; tayau pārājikā cuddasa saṅghādisaisā tayau aniyatā aikatiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyāti idaṃ abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina|

suttantapariyāyaina daivasikaṃ phalasamāpattisamāpaj‍janaṃ mahākaruṇāsamāpattisamāpaj‍janaṃ buddhacakkhunā laukavaulaukanaṃ aṭṭhuppattivasaina suttantadaisanā jātakakathāti idaṃ āciṇṇaṃ, na daivasikaṃ phalasamāpattisamāpaj‍janaṃ…pai॰… na jātakakathāti idaṃ anāciṇṇaṃ| vinayapariyāyaina  nimantitassa vassāvāsaṃ vasitvā apalaukaitvā cārikāpak‍kamanaṃ pavāraitvā cārikāpak‍kamanaṃ, āgantukaihi saddhiṃ paṭhamaṃ paṭisanthārakaraṇanti idaṃ āciṇṇaṃ, tassaiva āciṇṇassa akaraṇaṃ anāciṇṇaṃ nāma|

suttantapariyāyaina cattārau satipaṭṭhānā…pai॰… aṭṭhaṅgikau maggauti idaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ nāma; tayau satipaṭṭhānā…pai॰… navaṅgikau maggauti idaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ nāma| vinayapariyāyaina cattārau pārājikā…pai॰… tiṃsanissaggiyā pācittiyāti idaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ nāma; tayau pārājikā…pai॰… aikatiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyāti idaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ nāma|

yaṃ  panaitaṃ sabbasuttānaṃ pariyausānai taicimaṃ saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaintīti vuttaṃ, tattha pañ‍ca antaradhānāni nāma adhigamaantaradhānaṃ, paṭipattiantaradhānaṃ, pariyattiantaradhānaṃ, liṅgaantaradhānaṃ, dhātuantaradhānanti| tattha adhigamauti cattārau maggā, cattāri phalāni, catassau paṭisambhidā, tissau vij‍jā, cha abhiñ‍ñāti| sau parihāyamānau paṭisambhidātau paṭṭhāya parihāyati| buddhānaṃ hi parinibbānatau vassasahassamaiva paṭisambhidā nibbattaituṃ sak‍kaunti, tatau paraṃ cha abhiñ‍ñā, tatau tāpi nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntā tissau vij‍jā nibbattainti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai tāpi nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntā sukkhavipassakā haunti| aitainaiva upāyaina anāgāminau sakadāgāminau sautāpan‍nāti| taisu dharantaisu adhigamau anantarahitau nāma na hauti| pacchimakassa pana sautāpan‍nassa jīvitakkhayaina adhigamau antarahitau nāma hauti| idaṃ adhigamaantaradhānaṃ nāma|

paṭipattiantaradhānaṃ nāma jhānavipassanāmaggaphalāni nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntā catupārisuddhisīlamattaṃ rakkhanti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai ‘‘sīlaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ katvā rakkhāma, padhānañ‍ca anuyuñ‍jāma, na ca maggaṃ vā phalaṃ vā sacchikātuṃ sak‍kauma, natthi idāni ariyadhammapaṭivaidhau’’ti vausānaṃ āpaj‍jitvā kausaj‍jabahulā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ na caudainti na sārainti akuk‍kuc‍cakā haunti, tatau paṭṭhāya khuddānukhuddakāni maddanti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai pācittiyathul‍lac‍cayāni āpaj‍janti, tatau garukāpattiṃ| pārājikamattamaiva tiṭṭhati| cattāri pārājikāni rakkhantānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sataipi sahassaipi dharamānai paṭipatti anantarahitā nāma na hauti| pacchimakassa pana bhikkhunau  sīlabhaidaina  vā jīvitakkhayaina vā antarahitā hautīti idaṃ paṭipattiantaradhānaṃ nāma|

pariyattīti taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ sāṭṭhakathā pāḷi| yāva sā tiṭṭhati, tāva pariyatti paripuṇṇā nāma hauti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai rājayuvarājānau adhammikā haunti, taisu adhammikaisu rājāmac‍cādayau adhammikā haunti, tatau raṭṭhajanapadavāsinauti| aitaisaṃ adhammikatāya daivau na sammā vassati, tatau sassāni na sampaj‍janti| taisu asampaj‍jantaisu pac‍cayadāyakā bhikkhusaṅghassa pac‍cayai dātuṃ na sak‍kaunti, bhikkhū pac‍cayaihi kilamantā antaivāsikai saṅgahaituṃ na sak‍kaunti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai pariyatti parihāyati, atthavasaina dhāraituṃ na sak‍kaunti, pāḷivasainaiva dhārainti| tatau gacchantai gacchantai kālai pāḷimpi sakalaṃ dhāraituṃ na sak‍kaunti, paṭhamaṃ abhidhammapiṭakaṃ parihāyati| parihāyamānaṃ matthakatau paṭṭhāya parihāyati| paṭhamamaiva hi paṭṭhānamahāpakaraṇaṃ parihāyati, tasmiṃ parihīnai yamakaṃ, kathāvatthu, puggalapañ‍ñatti, dhātukathā, vibhaṅgau, dhammasaṅgahauti|

aivaṃ abhidhammapiṭakai parihīnai matthakatau paṭṭhāya suttantapiṭakaṃ parihāyati| paṭhamañhi aṅguttaranikāyau parihāyati, tasmimpi paṭhamaṃ aikādasakanipātau, tatau dasakanipātau…pai॰… tatau aikakanipātauti| aivaṃ aṅguttarai parihīnai matthakatau paṭṭhāya saṃyuttanikāyau parihāyati| paṭhamaṃ hi mahāvaggau parihāyati, tatau saḷāyatanavaggau, khandhavaggau, nidānavaggau, sagāthāvaggauti| aivaṃ saṃyuttanikāyai parihīnai matthakatau paṭṭhāya majjhimanikāyau parihāyati| paṭhamaṃ hi uparipaṇṇāsakau parihāyati, tatau majjhimapaṇṇāsakau, tatau mūlapaṇṇāsakauti| aivaṃ majjhimanikāyai parihīnai matthakatau paṭṭhāya dīghanikāyau parihāyati| paṭhamañhi  pāthikavaggau parihāyati, tatau mahāvaggau, tatau sīlakkhandhavaggauti| aivaṃ dīghanikāyai parihīnai suttantapiṭakaṃ parihīnaṃ nāma hauti| vinayapiṭakaina saddhiṃ jātakamaiva dhārainti| vinayapiṭakaṃ laj‍jinauva dhārainti, lābhakāmā pana ‘‘suttantai kathitaipi sal‍lakkhaintā natthī’’ti jātakamaiva dhārainti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai  jātakampi dhāraituṃ na sak‍kaunti| atha taisaṃ paṭhamaṃ vaissantarajātakaṃ parihāyati, tatau paṭilaumak‍kamaina puṇṇakajātakaṃ, mahānāradajātakanti pariyausānai apaṇṇakajātakaṃ parihāyati| aivaṃ jātakai parihīnai vinayapiṭakamaiva dhārainti|

gacchantai gacchantai kālai vinayapiṭakampi matthakatau paṭṭhāya parihāyati| paṭhamañhi parivārau parihāyati, tatau khandhakau, bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau, mahāvibhaṅgauti anuk‍kamaina upausathakkhandhakamattamaiva dhārainti| tadāpi pariyatti antarahitā na hauti| yāva pana manussaisu cātuppadikagāthāpi pavattati, tāva pariyatti anantarahitāva hauti| yadā saddhau pasan‍nau rājā hatthikkhandhai suvaṇṇacaṅkauṭakamhi sahassatthavikaṃ ṭhapāpaitvā ‘‘buddhaihi kathitaṃ cātuppadikagāthaṃ jānantau imaṃ sahassaṃ gaṇhatū’’ti nagarai bhairiṃ carāpaitvā gaṇhanakaṃ alabhitvā ‘‘aikavāraṃ carāpitai nāma suṇantāpi haunti assuṇantāpī’’ti yāvatatiyaṃ carāpaitvā gaṇhanakaṃ alabhitvā rājapurisā taṃ sahassatthavikaṃ puna rājakulaṃ pavaisainti, tadā pariyatti antarahitā nāma hauti| idaṃ pariyattiantaradhānaṃ nāma|

gacchantai  gacchantai kālai cīvaraggahaṇaṃ pattaggahaṇaṃ sammiñ‍janapasāraṇaṃ ālaukitavilaukitaṃ  na pāsādikaṃ hauti| nigaṇṭhasamaṇā viya alābupattaṃ bhikkhū pattaṃ aggabāhāya pakkhipitvā ādāya vicaranti, aittāvatāpi liṅgaṃ anantarahitamaiva hauti| gacchantai gacchantai pana kālai aggabāhatau autāraitvā hatthaina vā sik‍kāya vā aulambitvā vicaranti, cīvarampi rajanasāruppaṃ akatvā auṭṭhaṭṭhivaṇṇaṃ katvā vicaranti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai rajanampi na hauti dasacchindanampi auvaṭṭikavijjhanampi, kappamattaṃ katvā vaḷañ‍jainti| puna auvaṭṭikaṃ vijjhitvā kappaṃ na karaunti| tatau ubhayampi akatvā dasā chaitvā paribbājakā viya caranti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai ‘‘kau iminā amhākaṃ atthau’’ti khuddakaṃ kāsāvakhaṇḍaṃ hatthai vā gīvāya vā bandhanti, kaisaisu vā al‍līyāpainti, dārabharaṇaṃ vā karauntā kasitvā vapitvā jīvikaṃ kappaitvā vicaranti| tadā dakkhiṇaṃ daintā saṅghaṃ uddissa aitaisaṃ dainti| idaṃ sandhāya bhagavatā vuttaṃ – ‘‘bhavissanti khau, panānanda, anāgatamaddhānaṃ gautrabhunau kāsāvakaṇṭhā dussīlā pāpadhammā, taisu dussīlaisu saṅghaṃ uddissa dānaṃ dassanti, tadāpāhaṃ, ānanda, saṅghagataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ asaṅkhaiyyaṃ  appamaiyyaṃ vadāmī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.380)| tatau gacchantai kālai nānāvidhāni kammāni karauntā ‘‘papañ‍cau aisa, kiṃ iminā amhāka’’nti kāsāvakhaṇḍaṃ chinditvā arañ‍ñai khipanti| aitasmiṃ kālai liṅgaṃ antarahitaṃ nāma hauti| kassapadasabalassa kira kālatau paṭṭhāya yaunakānaṃ  saitavatthaṃ pārupitvā caraṇaṃ cārittaṃ jātanti| idaṃ liṅgaantaradhānaṃ nāma|

dhātuantaradhānaṃ pana aivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ – tīṇi parinibbānāni, kilaisaparinibbānaṃ – khandhaparinibbānaṃ, dhātuparinibbānanti| tattha kilaisaparinibbānaṃ baudhipal‍laṅkai ahausi, khandhaparinibbānaṃ kusinārāyaṃ, dhātuparinibbānaṃ anāgatai bhavissati| kathaṃ? tatau tattha tattha sak‍kārasammānaṃ alabhamānā dhātuyau buddhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānabalaina sak‍kārasammānalabhanakaṭṭhānaṃ gacchanti| gacchantai gacchantai kālai sabbaṭṭhānaisu sak‍kārasammānau na hauti| sāsanassa hi ausak‍kanakālai imasmiṃ tambapaṇṇidīpai sabbā dhātuyau san‍nipatitvā mahācaitiyaṃ, tatau nāgadīpai rājāyatanacaitiyaṃ, tatau baudhipal‍laṅkaṃ gamissanti| nāgabhavanataupi daivalaukataupi brahmalaukataupi dhātuyau mahābaudhipal‍laṅkamaiva gamissanti| sāsapamattāpi dhātu antarā na nassissati| sabbā dhātuyau mahābaudhimaṇḍai san‍nipatitvā buddharūpaṃ gahaitvā baudhimaṇḍai pal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nabuddhasarīrasiriṃ dassainti| dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni asīti anubyañ‍janāni byāmappabhāti sabbaṃ paripuṇṇamaiva hauti| tatau yamakapāṭihāriyadivasai viya pāṭihāriyaṃ katvā  dassainti| tadā manussabhūtasattau nāma tattha gatau natthi, dasasahassacak‍kavāḷai pana daivatā sabbāva san‍nipatitvā ‘‘aj‍ja dasabalau parinibbāyati, itaudāni paṭṭhāya andhakāraṃ bhavissatī’’ti paridaivanti| atha dhātusarīratau taijau samuṭṭhāya taṃ sarīraṃ apaṇṇattikabhāvaṃ gamaiti| dhātusarīratau samuṭṭhitā jālā yāva brahmalaukā uggacchissati, sāsapamattāya saisāyapi dhātuyā sati aikajālāva bhavissati| dhātūsu pariyādānaṃ gatāsu pacchij‍jissati| aivaṃ mahantaṃ ānubhāvaṃ dassaitvā dhātuyau  antaradhāyanti| tadā san‍nipatitā daivasaṅghā buddhānaṃ parinibbutadivasai viya dibbagandhamālātūriyādīhi sak‍kāraṃ katvā tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ katvā vanditvā ‘‘anāgatai uppaj‍janakaṃ buddhaṃ passituṃ labhissāma bhagavā’’ti vatvā sakasakaṭṭhānamaiva gacchanti| idaṃ dhātuantaradhānaṃ nāma|

imassa pañ‍cavidhassa antaradhānassa pariyattiantaradhānamaiva  mūlaṃ| pariyattiyā hi antarahitāya paṭipatti antaradhāyati, pariyattiyā ṭhitāya paṭipatti patiṭṭhāti| tainaiva imasmiṃ dīpai caṇḍālatissamahābhayai sak‍kau daivarājā mahāuḷumpaṃ māpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ āraucāpaisi ‘‘mahantaṃ bhayaṃ bhavissati, na sammā daivau vassissati, bhikkhū pac‍cayaihi kilamantā pariyattiṃ sandhāraituṃ na sakkhissanti, paratīraṃ gantvā ayyaihi jīvitaṃ rakkhituṃ vaṭṭati| imaṃ mahāuḷumpaṃ āruyha gacchatha, bhantai| yaisaṃ aittha nisaj‍jaṭṭhānaṃ nappahauti, tai kaṭṭhakhaṇḍaipi uraṃ ṭhapaitvā gacchantu, sabbaisampi bhayaṃ na bhavissatī’’ti| tadā samuddatīraṃ patvā saṭṭhi bhikkhū katikaṃ katvā ‘‘amhākaṃ aittha gamanakic‍caṃ natthi, mayaṃ idhaiva hutvā taipiṭakaṃ rakkhissāmā’’ti tatau nivattitvā dakkhiṇamalayajanapadaṃ gantvā kandamūlapaṇṇaihi jīvikaṃ kappaintā vasiṃsu| kāyai vahantai nisīditvā sajjhāyaṃ karaunti, avahantai vālikaṃ ussāraitvā parivāraitvā sīsāni aikaṭṭhānai katvā pariyattiṃ sammasanti| iminā niyāmaina dvādasa saṃvaccharāni sāṭṭhakathaṃ taipiṭakaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ katvā dhārayiṃsu|

bhayai vūpasantai sattasatā bhikkhū attanau gataṭṭhānai sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai aikakkharampi aikabyañ‍janampi anāsaitvā imamaiva dīpamāgamma kal‍lagāmajanapadai maṇḍalārāmavihāraṃ pavisiṃsu| thairānaṃ āgamanappavattiṃ sutvā imasmiṃ dīpai auhīnā saṭṭhi bhikkhū ‘‘thairai passissāmā’’ti gantvā thairaihi saddhiṃ taipiṭakaṃ saudhaintā aikakkharampi aikabyañ‍janampi asamaintaṃ nāma na passiṃsu| tasmiṃ ṭhānai thairānaṃ ayaṃ kathā udapādi ‘‘pariyatti nu khau sāsanassa mūlaṃ, udāhu paṭipattī’’ti| paṃsukūlikatthairā ‘‘paṭipattimūla’’nti āhaṃsu, dhammakathikā ‘‘pariyattī’’ti| atha nai thairā ‘‘tumhākaṃ dvin‍nampi janānaṃ vacanamattainaiva na karauma, jinabhāsitaṃ suttaṃ āharathā’’ti āhaṃsu| suttaṃ āharituṃ na bhārauti ‘‘imai ca, subhadda, bhikkhū sammā viharaiyyuṃ, asuñ‍ñau laukau arahantaihi assāti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.214)| paṭipattimūlakaṃ, mahārāja, satthusāsanaṃ paṭipattisārakaṃ| paṭipattiyā dharantāya tiṭṭhatī’’ti (mi॰ pa॰ 4.1.7) suttaṃ āhariṃsu| imaṃ suttaṃ sutvā dhammakathikā attanau vādaṭhapanatthāya imaṃ suttaṃ āhariṃsu –

‘‘yāva tiṭṭhanti suttantā, vinayau yāva dippati|

tāva dakkhanti ālaukaṃ, sūriyai abbhuṭṭhitai yathā||

‘‘suttantaisu asantaisu, pamuṭṭhai vinayamhi ca|

tamau bhavissati laukai, sūriyai atthaṅgatai yathā||

‘‘suttantai rakkhitai santai, paṭipatti hauti rakkhitā|

paṭipattiyaṃ ṭhitau dhīrau, yaugakkhaimā na dhaṃsatī’’ti||

imasmiṃ suttai āhaṭai paṃsukūlikatthairā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ, dhammakathikatthairānaṃyaiva vacanaṃ puratau ahausi| yathā hi gavasatassa vā gavasahassassa vā antarai pavaiṇipālikāya dhainuyā asati sau vaṃsau sā pavaiṇi na ghaṭīyati, aivamaivaṃ āraddhavipassakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sataipi sahassaipi saṃvij‍jamānai pariyattiyā asati ariyamaggapaṭivaidhau nāma na hauti| yathā ca nidhikumbhiyā jānanatthāya pāsāṇapiṭṭhai akkharaisu ṭhapitaisu yāva akkharāni dharanti, tāva nidhikumbhi naṭṭhā nāma na hauti| aivamaivaṃ pariyattiyā dharamānāya sāsanaṃ antarahitaṃ nāma na hautīti|

dutiyapamādādivaggavaṇṇanā|



11. adhammavaggavaṇṇanā

140. aikādasamai  vaggai adhammaṃ adhammautiādīni vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbāni| saisamaittha uttānamaivāti|

adhammavaggavaṇṇanā|



12. anāpattivaggavaṇṇanā

150. dvādasamai  pana anāpattiṃ āpattītiādīsu ‘‘anāpatti ajānantassa athaiyyacittassa namaraṇādhippāyassa anul‍lapanādhippāyassa namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti tattha tattha vuttā anāpatti anāpatti nāma, ‘‘jānantassa thaiyyacittassā’’tiādinā nayaina vuttā āpatti āpatti nāma, pañ‍cāpattikkhandhā lahukāpatti nāma, dvai āpattikkhandhā garukāpatti nāma| dvai āpattikkhandhā duṭṭhul‍lāpatti nāma, pañ‍cāpattikkhandhā aduṭṭhul‍lāpatti nāma| cha āpattikkhandhā sāvasaisāpatti nāma, aikau pārājikāpattikkhandhau anavasaisāpatti nāma| sappaṭikammāpatti nāma sāvasaisāpattiyaiva, appaṭikammāpatti nāma anavasaisāpattiyaiva| saisaṃ sabbattha uttānamaivāti|

anāpattivaggavaṇṇanā|



13. aikapuggalavaggavaṇṇanā

170. aikapuggalavaggassa  paṭhamai aikapuggalauti aikau puggalau| aittha aikauti dutiyādipaṭikkhaipatthau gaṇanaparicchaidau| puggalauti sammutikathā, na paramatthakathā| buddhassa hi bhagavatau duvidhā daisanā – sammutidaisanā, paramatthadaisanā cāti| tattha ‘‘puggalau sattau itthī purisau khattiyau brāhmaṇau daivau mārau’’ti  aivarūpā sammutidaisanā, ‘‘anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā khandhā dhātū āyatanāni satipaṭṭhānā’’ti aivarūpā paramatthadaisanā| tattha bhagavā yai sammutivasaina daisanaṃ sutvā atthaṃ paṭivijjhitvā mauhaṃ pahāya visaisaṃ adhigantuṃ samatthā, taisaṃ sammutidaisanaṃ daisaiti| yai pana paramatthavasaina daisanaṃ sutvā atthaṃ paṭivijjhitvā mauhaṃ pahāya visaisamadhigantuṃ samatthā, taisaṃ paramatthadaisanaṃ daisaiti|

tatrāyaṃ upamā – yathā hi daisabhāsākusalau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ atthasaṃvaṇṇanakau ācariyau yai damiḷabhāsāya vuttai atthaṃ jānanti, taisaṃ damiḷabhāsāya ācikkhati| yai andhabhāsādīsu añ‍ñatarāya bhāsāya, taisaṃ tāya tāya bhāsāya| aivaṃ tai māṇavakā chaikaṃ byattaṃ ācariyamāgamma khippamaiva sippaṃ uggaṇhanti| tattha ācariyau viya buddhau bhagavā, tayau vaidā viya kathaitabbabhāvai ṭhitāni tīṇi piṭakāni, daisabhāsākausal‍lamiva sammutiparamatthakausal‍laṃ, nānādaisabhāsā māṇavakā viya sammutiparamatthavasaina paṭivijjhanasamatthā vainaiyyasattā, ācariyassa damiḷabhāsādiācikkhanaṃ viya bhagavatau sammutiparamatthavasaina daisanā vaiditabbā| āha caittha –

‘‘duvai sac‍cāni akkhāsi, sambuddhau vadataṃ varau|

sammutiṃ paramatthañ‍ca, tatiyaṃ nupalabbhati||

‘‘saṅkaitavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, laukasammutikāraṇā|

paramatthavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, dhammānaṃ bhūtakāraṇā||

‘‘tasmā  vauhārakusalassa, laukanāthassa satthunau|

sammutiṃ vauharantassa, musāvādau na jāyatī’’ti||

apica aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā puggalakathaṃ kathaiti – hirauttappadīpanatthaṃ, kammassakatādīpanatthaṃ, pac‍cattapurisakāradīpanatthaṃ, ānantariyadīpanatthaṃ, brahmavihāradīpanatthaṃ, pubbainivāsadīpanatthaṃ, dakkhiṇāvisuddhidīpanatthaṃ, laukasammutiyā appahānatthañ‍cāti| ‘‘khandhadhātuāyatanāni  hiriyanti auttappantī’’ti hi vuttai mahājanau na jānāti, sammauhamāpaj‍jati, paṭisattu hauti ‘‘kimidaṃ khandhadhātuāyatanāni hiriyanti auttappanti nāmā’’ti? ‘‘itthī hiriyati auttappati, purisau khattiyau brāhmaṇau daivau mārau’’ti vuttai pana jānāti, na sammauhamāpaj‍jati, na paṭisattu hauti| tasmā bhagavā hirauttappadīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā kammassakā, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā kammassakatādīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘vaiḷuvanādayau mahāvihārā khandhaihi kārāpitā, dhātūhi āyatanaihī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā pac‍cattapurisakāradīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā  mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupainti, pitaraṃ, arahantaṃ, ruhiruppādakammaṃ, saṅghabhaidakammaṃ karaunti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā ānantariyadīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā maittāyanti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā brahmavihāradīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā pubbainivāsamanussaranti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā pubbainivāsadīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā  dānaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi mahājanau na jānāti, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, paṭisattu hauti ‘‘kimidaṃ khandhadhātuāyatanāni paṭiggaṇhanti nāmā’’ti? ‘‘puggalā paṭiggaṇhanti sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammau’’ti vuttai pana jānāti, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, na paṭisattu hauti| tasmā bhagavā dakkhiṇāvisuddhidīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

laukasammutiñ‍ca  buddhā bhagavantau nappajahanti, laukasamañ‍ñāya laukaniruttiyā laukābhilāpai ṭhitāyaiva dhammaṃ daisainti| tasmā bhagavā laukasammutiyā appahānatthampi puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

iti aikau ca sau puggalau cāti aikapuggalau| kainaṭṭhaina aikapuggalau? asadisaṭṭhaina guṇavisiṭṭhaṭṭhaina asamasamaṭṭhaināti| sau hi dasan‍naṃ pāramīnaṃ paṭipāṭiyā āvaj‍janaṃ ādiṃ katvā baudhisambhāraguṇaihi caiva buddhaguṇaihi ca saisamahājanaina asadisauti asadisaṭṭhainapi aikapuggalau| yai cassa tai guṇā, tai saisasattānaṃ guṇaihi visiṭṭhāti guṇavisiṭṭhaṭṭhainapi aikapuggalau| purimakā sammāsambuddhā sabbasattaihi asamā, taihi saddhiṃ ayamaiva aikau rūpakāyaguṇaihi caiva nāmakāyaguṇaihi ca samauti asamasamaṭṭhainapi aikapuggalau|

laukaiti tayau laukā – sattalaukau, aukāsalaukau, saṅkhāralaukauti| taisaṃ vitthārakathā visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 1.135-136) vuttā| taisu idha sattalaukau adhippaitau| sattalaukai uppaj‍jamānaupi caisa na daivalaukai, na brahmalaukai, manussalaukaiyaiva uppaj‍jati| manussalaukaipi  na añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai, imasmiṃyaiva cak‍kavāḷai uppaj‍jati| tatrāpi na sabbaṭṭhānaisu|

‘‘puratthimāya disāya gajaṅgalaṃ nāma nigamau, tassa paraina mahāsālā, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai| puratthimadakkhiṇāya disāya sal‍lavatī nāma nadī, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai| dakkhiṇāya disāya saitakaṇṇikaṃ nāma nigamau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai| pacchimāya  disāya thūṇaṃ nāma brāhmaṇagāmau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai| uttarāya disāya usīraddhajau nāma pabbatau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai’’ti (mahāva॰ 259) aivaṃ paricchin‍nai āyāmatau tiyaujanasatai vitthāratau aḍḍhataiyyayaujanasatai parikkhaipatau navayaujanasatai majjhimadaisai  uppaj‍jati| na kaivalañ‍ca tathāgatauva, pac‍caikabuddhā aggasāvakā asīti mahāthairā buddhamātā buddhapitā cak‍kavattī rājā añ‍ñai ca sārappattā brāhmaṇagahapatikā aitthaiva uppaj‍janti|

uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jatīti idaṃ pana ubhayampi vippakatavacanamaiva| uppaj‍jamānau bahujanahitāya uppaj‍jati, na añ‍ñaina kāraṇaināti aivaṃ panaittha atthau vaiditabbau| aivarūpañ‍caittha lakkhaṇaṃ na sak‍kā aitaṃ añ‍ñaina saddalakkhaṇaina paṭibāhituṃ|

apica uppaj‍jamānau nāma, uppaj‍jati nāma, uppan‍nau nāmāti ayamaittha bhaidau vaiditabbau| aisa hi dīpaṅkarapādamūlatau paṭṭhāya laddhabyākaraṇau buddhakārakai dhammai pariyaisantau dasa pāramiyau disvā ‘‘imai dhammā mayā pūraitabbā’’ti katasan‍niṭṭhānau dānapāramiṃ pūraintaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| sīlapāramī…pai॰… upaikkhāpāramīti imā dasa pāramiyau pūraintaupi, dasa upapāramiyau pūraintaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| dasa paramatthapāramiyau pūraintaupi uppaj‍jamānauva nāma| pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajantaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| attatthacariyaṃ ñātatthacariyaṃ laukatthacariyaṃ pūrayamānaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni buddhakārakai dhammai matthakaṃ pāpaintaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| vaissantarattabhāvaṃ pahāya  tusitapurai paṭisandhiṃ  gahaitvā saṭṭhivassasatasahassādhikā sattapaṇṇāsavassakauṭiyau tiṭṭhantaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| daivatāhi yācitau pañ‍camahāvilaukitaṃ vilaukaitvā mahāmāyādaiviyā kucchimhi paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhantaupi, anūnādhikai dasa māsai gabbhavāsaṃ vasantaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| aikūnatiṃsa vassāni agāramajjhai tiṭṭhantaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ naikkhammai ca ānisaṃsaṃ disvā rāhulabhaddassa jātadivasai chan‍nasahāyau kaṇḍakaṃ vāhanavaraṃ āruyha nikkhamantaupi uppaj‍jamānauva nāma| tīṇi raj‍jāni atik‍kamantaupi anaumānadītīrai pabbajantaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| chabbassāni mahāpadhānaṃ karauntaupi uppaj‍jamānau nāma| paripak‍kagatai ñāṇai auḷārikāhāraṃ āharantaupi uppaj‍jamānauva nāma| sāyanhasamayai visākhapuṇṇamāya mahābaudhimaṇḍaṃ āruyha mārabalaṃ vidhamaitvā paṭhamayāmai pubbainivāsaṃ anussaritvā majjhimayāmai dibbacakkhuṃ parisaudhaitvā pacchimayāmasamanantarai dvādasaṅgaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumatau sammasitvā sautāpattimaggaṃ paṭivijjhantaupi uppaj‍jamānauva nāma| sautāpattiphalakkhaṇaipi sakadāgāmimaggakkhaṇaipi sakadāgāmiphalakkhaṇaipi anāgāmimaggakkhaṇaipi  anāgāmiphalakkhaṇaipi uppaj‍jamānauva nāma| arahattamaggakkhaṇai pana uppaj‍jati nāma| arahattaphalakkhaṇai uppan‍nau nāma| buddhānaṃ hi sāvakānaṃ viya na paṭipāṭiyā iddhividhañāṇādīni uppaj‍janti, sahaiva pana arahattamaggaina sakalaupi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādi guṇarāsi āgatauva nāma hauti| tasmā tai nipphattasabbakic‍cattā arahattaphalakkhaṇai uppan‍nā nāma haunti| imasmimpi suttai arahattaphalakkhaṇaṃyaiva sandhāya ‘‘uppaj‍jatī’’ti vaiditabbau, uppan‍nau hautīti ayañhaittha atthau|

bahujanahitāyāti  mahājanassa hitatthāya uppaj‍jati| bahujanasukhāyāti mahājanassa sukhatthāya uppaj‍jati| laukānukampāyāti sattalaukassa anukampaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati| katarasattalaukassāti? yau tathāgatassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā amatapānaṃ pivitvā dhammaṃ paṭivijjhi, tassa| bhagavatā hi mahābaudhimaṇḍai sattasattāhaṃ vītināmaitvā baudhimaṇḍā isipatanaṃ āgamma ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, antā pabbajitaina na saivitabbā’’ti  dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttai (mahāva॰ 13; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081) daisitai āyasmatā añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairaina saddhiṃ aṭṭhārasakauṭisaṅkhā brahmānau amatapānaṃ piviṃsu, aitassa sattalaukassa anukampāya uppan‍nau| pañ‍camadivasai anattalakkhaṇasuttantapariyausānai (mahāva॰ 20; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.59) pañ‍cavaggiyā thairā arahattai patiṭṭhahiṃsu, aitassapi sattalaukassa anukampāya uppan‍nau| tatau yasadārakappamukhai pañ‍capaṇṇāsa purisai arahattai patiṭṭhāpaisi, tatau kappāsikavanasaṇḍai tiṃsa bhaddavaggiyai tayau maggai ca tīṇi phalāni ca sampāpaisi, aitassapi sattalaukassa anukampāya uppan‍nau| gayāsīsai ādittapariyāyasuttapariyausānai (mahāva॰ 54) jaṭilasahassaṃ arahattai patiṭṭhāpaisi, tālaṭṭhivanai bimbisārappamukhā aikādasa nahutā brāhmaṇagahapatikā satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhahiṃsu, aikaṃ nahutaṃ saraṇaisu patiṭṭhitaṃ| tiraukuṭṭaanumaudanāvasānai caturāsītiyā pāṇasahassaihi amatapānaṃ pītaṃ| sumanamālākārasamāgamai caturāsītiyā ca| dhanapālakasamāgamai dasahi pāṇasahassaihi, khadiraṅgārajātakasamāgamai caturāsītiyā pāṇasahassaihi, jambukaājīvakasamāgamai caturāsītiyā ca| ānandasaiṭṭhisamāgamai  caturāsītiyā ca pāṇasahassaihi amatapānaṃ pītaṃ| pāsāṇakacaitiyai pārāyanasuttantakathādivasai cuddasa kauṭiyau amatapānaṃ piviṃsu| yamakapāṭihāriyadivasai vīsati pāṇakauṭiyau, tāvatiṃsabhavanai paṇḍukambalasilāya nisīditvā mātaraṃ kāyasakkhiṃ katvā sattappakaraṇaṃ abhidhammaṃ daisaintassa asīti pāṇakauṭiyau, daivaurauhanai tiṃsa pāṇakauṭiyau, sak‍kapañhasuttantai asīti  daivatāsahassāni amatapānaṃ piviṃsu| mahāsamayasuttantai maṅgalasuttantai cūḷarāhulauvādai samacittapaṭipadāyāti imaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu abhisamayaṃ pattasattānaṃ paricchaidau natthi| aitassapi sattalaukassa anukampāya uppan‍nauti| yāvaj‍jadivasā itau paraṃ anāgatai ca sāsanaṃ nissāya saggamaukkhamaggai patiṭṭhahantānaṃ vasaināpi ayamatthau vaiditabbau|

daivamanussānanti na kaivalaṃ daivamanussānaṃyaiva, avasaisānaṃ nāgasupaṇṇādīnampi atthāya hitāya sukhāyaiva uppan‍nau| sahaitukapaṭisandhikai pana  maggaphalasacchikiriyāya bhabbai puggalai dassaituṃ aitaṃ vuttaṃ| tasmā aitaisampi atthāya hitāya sukhāyaiva uppan‍nauti vaiditabbau|

katamau aikapuggalauti ayaṃ pucchā| pucchā ca nāmaisā pañ‍cavidhā hauti – adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā, diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā, vimaticchaidanā pucchā, anumatipucchā, kathaitukamyatāpucchāti|

tāsaṃ idaṃ nānattaṃ – katamā adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ añ‍ñātaṃ hauti adiṭṭhaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ abhāvitaṃ| tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya tulanāya  tīraṇāya vibhūtatthāya vibhāvanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā|

katamā diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ tulitaṃ tīritaṃ vibhūtaṃ vibhāvitaṃ| sau añ‍ñaihi paṇḍitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā|

katamā vimaticchaidanā pucchā? pakatiyā saṃsayapakkhantau hauti vimatipakkhantau dvaiḷhakajātau ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, na nu khau, kiṃ nu khau, kathaṃ nu khau’’ti| sau vimaticchaidanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ vimaticchaidanā pucchā|

katamā anumatipucchā? bhagavā hi bhikkhūnaṃ anumatiyā pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ  nu khau taṃ samanupassituṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti, ayaṃ anumatipucchā|

katamā kathaitukamyatāpucchā? bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ kathaitukamyatāya pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau’’ti? ayaṃ kathaitukamyatāpucchāti|

tattha buddhānaṃ purimā tissau pucchā natthi| kasmā? buddhānaṃ hi tīsu addhāsu kiñ‍ci saṅkhataṃ addhāvimuttaṃ vā asaṅkhataṃ adiṭṭhaṃ ajānitaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ avibhāvitaṃ nāma natthi, tasmā taisaṃ adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā natthi| yaṃ pana bhagavatā attanau ñāṇaina paṭividdhaṃ, tassa añ‍ñaina samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina  vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā saddhiṃ saṃsandanakic‍caṃ  natthi| tainassa diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchā natthi| yasmā panaisa akathaṃkathī tiṇṇavicikicchau sabbadhammaisu vihatasaṃsayau, tainassa vimaticchaidanāpucchā natthi| itarā pana dvai pucchā bhagavatau atthi, tāsu ayaṃ kathaitukamyatāpucchāti vaiditabbā|

idāni tāya pucchāya puṭṭhaṃ aikapuggalaṃ vibhāvaintau tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhauti āha| tattha tathāgatauti aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatau – tathā āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau, tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau, tathadassitāya tathāgatau, tathavāditāya tathāgatau, tathākāritāya tathāgatau, abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatauti|

kathaṃ bhagavā tathā āgatauti tathāgatau? yathā sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍nā purimakā sammāsambuddhā āgatā, yathā vipassī bhagavā āgatau, yathā sikhī bhagavā, yathā vaissabhū bhagavā, yathā kakusandhau bhagavā, yathā kauṇāgamanau bhagavā, yathā kassapau bhagavā āgatauti| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? yaina abhinīhāraina aitai bhagavantau āgatā, tainaiva amhākampi bhagavā āgatau| atha vā yathā vipassī bhagavā…pai॰… yathā kassapau bhagavā dānapāramiṃ pūraitvā, sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍cādhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramiṃ pūraitvā imā dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa pāramiyau pūraitvā, aṅgaparic‍cāgaṃ nayanadhanaraj‍japuttadāraparic‍cāganti imai pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajitvā, pubbayaugapubbacariyadhammakkhānañātatthacariyādayau  pūraitvā buddhicariyāya kauṭiṃ patvā āgatau, tathā amhākampi bhagavā āgatau| yathā ca vipassī bhagavā …pai॰… kassapau bhagavā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai cattārau sammappadhānai cattārau iddhipādai pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgai ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaitvā brūhaitvā āgatau, tathā amhākampi bhagavā āgatauti tathāgatau|

‘‘yathaiva  laukamhi vipassiādayau,

sabbañ‍ñubhāvaṃ munayau idhāgatā|

tathā ayaṃ sakyamunīpi āgatau,

tathāgatau vuc‍cati taina cakkhumā’’ti||

aivaṃ tathā āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau? yathā sampatijātau vipassī bhagavā gatau…pai॰… kassapau bhagavā gatau| kathañ‍ca sau gatauti? sau hi sampatijātauva samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gatau| yathāha – ‘‘sampatijātau, ānanda, baudhisattau samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gacchati saitamhi chattai anudhāriyamānai, sabbā ca disā anuvilaukaiti, āsabhiñ‍ca vācaṃ bhāsati ‘aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.207)| tañ‍cassa gamanaṃ tathaṃ ahausi avitathaṃ anaikaisaṃ visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina| yañhi sau sampatijātauva samaihi pādaihi patiṭṭhahi, idamassa caturiddhipādapaṭilābhassa pubbanimittaṃ, uttarābhimukhabhāvau pana sabbalaukuttarabhāvassa pubbanimittaṃ, sattapadavītihārau sattabaujjhaṅgaratanapaṭilābhassa, ‘‘suvaṇṇadaṇḍā vītipatanti cāmarā’’ti (su॰ ni॰ 693) aittha vuttacāmarukkhaipau pana sabbatitthiyanimmathanassa  pubbanimittaṃ, saitacchattadhāraṇaṃ arahattaphalavimuttivaravimalasaitacchattapaṭilābhassa pubbanimittaṃ, sabbadisānuvilaukanaṃ sabbañ‍ñutānāvaraṇañāṇapaṭilābhassa pubbanimittaṃ, āsabhivācābhāsanaṃ appaṭivattiyavaradhammacak‍kappavattanassa pubbanimittaṃ| tathā ayaṃ  bhagavāpi gatau| tañ‍cassa gamanaṃ kathaṃ ahausi avitathaṃ taisaṃyaiva visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘muhuttajātauva gavampatī yathā,

samaihi pādaihi phusī vasundharaṃ|

sau vik‍kamī satta padāni gautamau,

saitañ‍ca chattaṃ anudhārayuṃ marū||

‘‘gantvāna  sau satta padāni gautamau,

disā vilaukaisi samā samantatau|

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ giramabbhudīrayi,

sīhau yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau’’ti||

aivaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

atha vā yathā vipassī bhagavā…pai॰… yathā kassapau bhagavā, ayampi bhagavā tathaiva naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ pahāya gatau, abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ, avikkhaipaina uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ pahāya gatau, ñāṇaina avij‍jaṃ padālaitvā gatau, pāmauj‍jaina aratiṃ vinaudaitvā, paṭhamajjhānaina nīvaraṇakavāṭaṃ ugghāṭaitvā, dutiyajjhānaina vitak‍kavicāraṃ vūpasamaitvā, tatiyajjhānaina pītiṃ virājaitvā, catutthajjhānaina sukhadukkhaṃ pahāya, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā rūpasañ‍ñāpaṭighasañ‍ñānānattasañ‍ñāyau samatik‍kamitvā, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ samatik‍kamitvā gatau|

anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pahāya, dukkhānupassanāya sukhasañ‍ñaṃ, anattānupassanāya attasañ‍ñaṃ, nibbidānupassanāya nandiṃ, virāgānupassanāya rāgaṃ, niraudhānupassanāya samudayaṃ, paṭinissaggānupassanāya ādānaṃ, khayānupassanāya ghanasañ‍ñaṃ, vayānupassanāya  āyūhanaṃ, vipariṇāmānupassanāya dhuvasañ‍ñaṃ, animittānupassanāya nimittasañ‍ñaṃ, appaṇihitānupassanāya paṇidhiṃ, suñ‍ñatānupassanāya abhinivaisaṃ, adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya sārādānābhinivaisaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaina sammauhābhinivaisaṃ, ādīnavānupassanāya ālayābhinivaisaṃ, paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya appaṭisaṅkhaṃ, vivaṭṭānupassanāya saṃyaugābhinivaisaṃ, sautāpattimaggaina diṭṭhaikaṭṭhai kilaisai bhañ‍jitvā, sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikai kilaisai pahāya, anāgāmimaggaina aṇusahagatai kilaisai samugghātaitvā, arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai samucchinditvā gatau| aivampi tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ  tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau? pathavīdhātuyā kakkhaḷattalakkhaṇaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ, āpaudhātuyā paggharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, taijaudhātuyā uṇhattalakkhaṇaṃ, vāyaudhātuyā vitthambhanalakkhaṇaṃ, ākāsadhātuyā asamphuṭṭhalakkhaṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

rūpassa ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ, vaidanāya vaidayitalakkhaṇaṃ, sañ‍ñāya sañ‍jānanalakkhaṇaṃ, saṅkhārānaṃ abhisaṅkharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇassa vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

vitak‍kassa abhiniraupanalakkhaṇaṃ, vicārassa anumaj‍janalakkhaṇaṃ, pītiyā pharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, sukhassa sātalakkhaṇaṃ, cittaikaggatāya avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ, phassassa phusanalakkhaṇaṃ|

saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhalakkhaṇaṃ, vīriyindriyassa paggahalakkhaṇaṃ, satindriyassa  upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, samādhindriyassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyassa pajānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

saddhābalassa assaddhiyai akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ, vīriyabalassa kausaj‍jai, satibalassa muṭṭhassac‍cai, samādhibalassa uddhac‍cai, pañ‍ñābalassa avij‍jāya akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ|

satisambaujjhaṅgassa upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa pavicayalakkhaṇaṃ, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa paggahalakkhaṇaṃ, pītisambaujjhaṅgassa pharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa vūpasamalakkhaṇaṃ, samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa paṭisaṅkhānalakkhaṇaṃ|

sammādiṭṭhiyā dassanalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāsaṅkappassa abhiniraupanalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāvācāya pariggahalakkhaṇaṃ, sammākammantassa samuṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāājīvassa vaudānalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāvāyāmassa paggahalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāsatiyā upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, sammāsamādhissa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ|

avij‍jāya añ‍ñāṇalakkhaṇaṃ, saṅkhārānaṃ caitanālakkhaṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇassa vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ, nāmassa namanalakkhaṇaṃ, rūpassa ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ, saḷāyatanassa āyatanalakkhaṇaṃ, phassassa phusanalakkhaṇaṃ, vaidanāya vaidayitalakkhaṇaṃ, taṇhāya haitulakkhaṇaṃ, upādānassa gahaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, bhavassa āyūhanalakkhaṇaṃ, jātiyā nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ, jarāya jīraṇalakkhaṇaṃ, maraṇassa cutilakkhaṇaṃ|

dhātūnaṃ  suñ‍ñatālakkhaṇaṃ, āyatanānaṃ āyatanalakkhaṇaṃ, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, sammappadhānānaṃ padahanalakkhaṇaṃ, iddhipādānaṃ ijjhanalakkhaṇaṃ, indriyānaṃ adhipatilakkhaṇaṃ, balānaṃ akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ, baujjhaṅgānaṃ niyyānalakkhaṇaṃ, maggassa haitulakkhaṇaṃ|

sac‍cānaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ, samathassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ, vipassanāya anupassanālakkhaṇaṃ, samathavipassanānaṃ aikarasalakkhaṇaṃ, yuganaddhānaṃ anativattanalakkhaṇaṃ|

sīlavisuddhiyā  saṃvaraṇalakkhaṇaṃ, cittavisuddhiyā avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ, diṭṭhivisuddhiyā dassanalakkhaṇaṃ|

khayai ñāṇassa samucchaidalakkhaṇaṃ, anuppādai ñāṇassa passaddhilakkhaṇaṃ|

chandassa mūlalakkhaṇaṃ, manasikārassa samuṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ, phassassa samaudhānalakkhaṇaṃ, vaidanāya samausaraṇalakkhaṇaṃ, samādhissa pamukhalakkhaṇaṃ, satiyā ādhipataiyyalakkhaṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya tatuttariyalakkhaṇaṃ, vimuttiyā sāralakkhaṇaṃ, amataugadhassa nibbānassa pariyausānalakkhaṇaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ| aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ ñāṇagatiyā āgatau avirajjhitvā pattau anuppattauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau? tathadhammā nāma cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| yathāha – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaita’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090) vitthārau| tāni ca bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmā tathānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| abhisambaudhatthau hi aittha gatasaddau|

apica jarāmaraṇassa jātipac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau…pai॰… saṅkhārānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau| tathā avij‍jāya saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayaṭṭhau, saṅkhārānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau…pai॰… jātiyā jarāmaraṇassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau| taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavā abhisambuddhau| tasmāpi  tathānaṃ dhammānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau? bhagavā  yaṃ sadaivakai laukai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthaṃ āgacchantaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ sabbākāratau jānāti passati| aivaṃ jānatā passatā ca taina taṃ iṭṭhāniṭṭhādivasaina vā diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātaisu labbhamānakapadavasaina vā ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 616) nayaina anaikaihi nāmaihi tairasahi vāraihi dvaipañ‍ñāsāya nayaihi vibhaj‍jamānaṃ tathamaiva hauti, vitathaṃ natthi| aisa nayau sautadvārādīsupi āpāthamāgacchantaisu saddādīsu| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi, tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.24)| aivaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau| tattha tathadassīatthai tathāgatauti padasambhavau vaiditabbau|

kathaṃ  tathavāditāya tathāgatau? yaṃ rattiṃ bhagavā baudhimaṇḍai aparājitapal‍laṅkai nisin‍nau tiṇṇaṃ mārānaṃ matthakaṃ madditvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, yañ‍ca rattiṃ yamakasālānaṃ antarai anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi, aitthantarai pañ‍cacattālīsavassaparimāṇai kālai paṭhamabaudhiyāpi majjhimabaudhiyāpi pacchimabaudhiyāpi yaṃ bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… vaidal‍laṃ, sabbaṃ  taṃ atthatau ca byañ‍janatau ca anupavaj‍jaṃ anūnaṃ anadhikaṃ sabbākāraparipuṇṇaṃ rāgamadanimmadanaṃ dausamauhamadanimmadanaṃ, natthi tattha vālaggamattampi avakkhalitaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ aikamuddikāya lañchitaṃ viya aikanāḷikāya mitaṃ viya aikatulāya tulitaṃ viya ca tathamaiva hauti avitathaṃ| taināha – ‘‘yañ‍ca, cunda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati  lapati niddisati, sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti nau añ‍ñathā| tasmā tathāgatauti vuc‍catī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.188)| gadatthau hi aittha gatasaddau| aivaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau|

apica āgadanaṃ āgadau, vacananti atthau| tathau aviparītau āgadau assāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti aivampaitasmiṃ atthai padasiddhi vaiditabbā|

kathaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau? bhagavatau hi vācāya kāyau anulaumaiti kāyassapi vācā, tasmā yathāvādī tathākārī yathākārī tathāvādī ca hauti| aivaṃ bhūtassa cassa yathā vācā, kāyaupi tathā gatau, pavattauti atthau| yathā ca kāyau, vācāpi tathā gatāti tathāgatau| tainaivāha – ‘‘yathāvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)| aivaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau|

kathaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau? upari bhavaggaṃ haiṭṭhā avīciṃ pariyantaṃ katvā tiriyaṃ  aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu sabbasattai abhibhavati sīlainapi samādhināpi pañ‍ñāyapi vimuttiyāpi, na tassa tulā vā pamāṇaṃ vā atthi, atulau appamaiyyau anuttarau rājarājau daivadaivau sak‍kānamatisak‍kau brahmānamatibrahmā| taināha – ‘‘sadaivakai laukai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya  tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthu dasau vasavattī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

tatraivaṃ padasiddhi vaiditabbā – agadau viya agadau| kau panaisa? daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca| taina haisa mahānubhāvau bhisak‍kau dibbāgadaina sappai viya sabbaparappavādinau sadaivakañ‍ca laukaṃ abhibhavati| iti sabbalaukābhibhavanai tathau aviparītau daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca agadau assāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau|
