satthā añ‍ñataraṃ piṇḍacārikaṃ ‘‘imaṃ dārakaṃ pabbājaihī’’ti āṇāpaisi| thairau tassa tacapañ‍cakakammaṭṭhānaṃ ācikkhitvā pabbājaisi| sau bahuṃ buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇhitvā paripuṇṇavassau upasampadaṃ labhi| upasampan‍nau hutvā yaunisaumanasikārai kammaṃ karauntau catun‍naṃ arūpāvacarajjhānānaṃ lābhī hutvā jhānaṅgaihi vuṭṭhāya arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| iti sau sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalānaṃ aggau jātau| sau jhānasukhaina phalasukhaina  vītināmaintau cintaisi – ‘‘sak‍kā nu khau imaṃ sukhaṃ cūḷapanthakassa dātu’’nti| tatau ayyakasaiṭṭhissa santikaṃ gantvā ‘‘mahāsaiṭṭhi sacai tumhai sampaṭicchatha, ahaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ pabbājaiyya’’nti āha| pabbājaitha, bhantaiti| thairau cūḷapanthakadārakaṃ pabbājaitvā dasasu sīlaisu patiṭṭhāpaisi| cūḷapanthakasāmaṇairau bhātikassa santikai|

‘‘padumaṃ yathā kaukanadaṃ sugandhaṃ,

pātau siyā phul‍lamavītagandhaṃ|

aṅgīrasaṃ passa viraucamānaṃ,

tapantamādic‍camivantalikkhai’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.123; a॰ ni॰ 5.195) –

imaṃ gāthaṃ gaṇhāti| gahitagahitapadaṃ uparūparipadaṃ gaṇhantassa nassati| tassa imaṃ gāthaṃ gahaituṃ vāyamantassaiva cattārau māsā atik‍kantā| atha naṃ mahāpanthakau āha – ‘‘cūḷapanthaka, tvaṃ imasmiṃ sāsanai abhabbau, catūhi māsaihi aikagāthampi gahaituṃ na sak‍kausi, pabbajitakic‍caṃ pana tvaṃ kathaṃ  matthakaṃ pāpaissasi, nikkhama itau’’ti| sau thairaina paṇāmitau vihārapac‍cantai raudamānau aṭṭhāsi|

taina samayaina satthā rājagahaṃ upanissāya jīvakambavanai viharati| tasmiṃ samayai jīvakau purisaṃ paisaisi ‘‘pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ satthāraṃ nimantaihī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina mahāpanthakau bhattuddaisakau hauti| sau ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ bhikkhaṃ sampaṭicchatha, bhantai’’ti vuttau ‘‘cūḷapanthakaṃ ṭhapaitvā saisānaṃ sampaṭicchāmī’’ti āha| cūḷapanthakau  taṃ kathaṃ sutvā bhiyyausaumattāya daumanassappattau ahausi| satthā cūḷapanthakassa khaidaṃ disvā ‘‘cūḷapanthakau mayi gatai bujjhissatī’’ti gantvā avidūrai ṭhānai attānaṃ dassaitvā ‘‘kiṃ tvaṃ, panthaka, raudasī’’ti āha| bhātā maṃ, bhantai, paṇāmaitīti| panthaka, tuyhaṃ bhātikassa parapuggalānaṃ āsayānusayañāṇaṃ natthi, tvaṃ buddhavainaiyyapuggalau nāmāti iddhiyā abhisaṅkharitvā suddhaṃ cauḷakhaṇḍaṃ adāsi ‘‘imaṃ gahaitvā ‘rajauharaṇaṃ rajauharaṇa’nti vatvā bhāvaihi panthakā’’ti|

sau satthārā din‍naṃ cauḷakhaṇḍaṃ ‘‘rajauharaṇaṃ rajauharaṇa’’nti hatthaina parimaj‍jantau nisīdi| tassa parimaj‍jantassa laumāni kiliṭṭhadhātukāni jātāni| puna parimaj‍jantassa ukkhaliparipuñchanasadisaṃ  jātaṃ| sau ñāṇaparipākaṃ āgamma tattha khayavayaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā cintaisi – ‘‘idaṃ cauḷakhaṇḍaṃ pakatiyā paṇḍaraṃ parisuddhaṃ, upādin‍nakasarīraṃ nissāya kiliṭṭhaṃ jātaṃ, idaṃ cittampi aivaṃgatikamaivā’’ti| samādhiṃ bhāvaitvā cattāri rūpāvacarajjhānāni pādakāni katvā saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| sau manaumayajjhānalābhī hutvā aikau hutvā bahudhā, bahudhā hutvā aikau bhavituṃ samatthau ahausi| arahattamaggainaiva cassa taipiṭakañ‍ca cha abhiñ‍ñā ca āgamiṃsu|

punadivasai satthā aikūnaihi pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ gantvā jīvakassa nivaisanai nisīdi| cūḷapanthakau pana attanau bhikkhāya asampaṭicchitattāyaiva na gatau| jīvakau yāguṃ dātuṃ ārabhi, satthā hatthaina pattaṃ pidahi| kasmā, bhantai, na gaṇhathāti? vihārai aikau bhikkhu atthi jīvakāti| sau purisaṃ pahiṇi ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, vihārai nisin‍naṃ ayyaṃ gahaitvā aihī’’ti| cūḷapanthakatthairaupi tassa purisassa purai āgamanāyaiva bhikkhusahassaṃ  nimminitvā aikampi aikaina asadisaṃ, aikassapi ca cīvaravicāraṇādisamaṇakammaṃ añ‍ñaina asadisaṃ akāsi| sau purisau vihārai bhikkhūnaṃ bahubhāvaṃ disvā gantvā jīvakassa kathaisi – ‘‘bhantai, imasmiṃ vihārai bhikkhusaṅghau bahutarau, tatau pak‍kausitabbaṃ bhadantaṃ na jānāmī’’ti| jīvakau satthāraṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘kaunāmau, bhantai, vihārai nisin‍nabhikkhū’’ti? cūḷapanthakau nāma jīvakāti| gaccha bhau ‘‘cūḷapanthakau nāma katarau’’ti pucchitvā ānaihīti| sau vihāraṃ gantvā ‘‘cūḷapanthakau nāma, bhantai, katarau’’ti pucchi| ‘‘ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau’’ti bhikkhusahassampi kathaisi| sau punāgantvā jīvakassa kathaisi ‘‘sahassamattā bhikkhū sabbaipi ‘ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau’ti kathainti, ahaṃ ‘asukau nāma pak‍kausitabbau’ti na jānāmī’’ti| jīvakaupi paṭividdhasac‍catāya ‘‘iddhimā bhikkhū’’ti nayatau ñatvā ‘‘paṭhamaṃ kathanabhikkhumaiva ‘tumhai satthā pak‍kausatī’ti vatvā cīvarakaṇṇai gaṇha tātā’’ti āha| sau vihāraṃ gantvā tathā akāsi, tāvadaiva sahassamattā bhikkhū antaradhāyiṃsu| sau thairaṃ gahaitvā agamāsi| satthā tasmiṃ khaṇai yāguṃ gaṇhi|

dasabalai bhattakic‍caṃ katvā vihāraṃ gatai dhammasabhāyaṃ kathā udapādi ‘‘yāva mahantā vata buddhā nāma cattārau māsai aikagāthaṃ gaṇhituṃ asak‍kauntaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃmahiddhikaṃ akaṃsū’’ti| satthā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cittācāraṃ ñatvā gantvā pañ‍ñattāsanai nisaj‍ja ‘‘kiṃ vadaitha, bhikkhavai’’ti  pucchi| na bhagavā añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci kathaima, cūḷapanthakaina tumhākaṃ santikā mahālābhau laddhauti tumhākaṃyaiva guṇaṃ kathaimāti| anacchariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, idāni mayhaṃ auvādaṃ  katvā laukuttaradāyaj‍jalābhau, ayaṃ atītaipi aparipak‍kañāṇai ṭhitassa mayhaṃ auvādaṃ katvā laukiyadāyaj‍jaṃ labhīti| bhikkhū ‘‘kadā, bhantai’’ti āyāciṃsu| satthā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ atītaṃ āharitvā dassaisi|

bhikkhavai, atītai bārāṇasīnagarai brahmadattau nāma rājā raj‍jaṃ kāraisi| tasmiṃ samayai cūḷakasaiṭṭhi nāma paṇḍitau byattau sabbanimittāni  jānāti| sau aikadivasaṃ rājūpaṭṭhānaṃ gacchantau antaravīthiyaṃ matamūsikaṃ disvā tasmiṃ khaṇai nakkhattaṃ samānaitvā idamāha – ‘‘sak‍kā cakkhumatā kulaputtaina imaṃ undūraṃ gahaitvā dārabharaṇañ‍ca kātuṃ kammantai ca payaujaitu’’nti| añ‍ñatarau duggatakulaputtau taṃ saiṭṭhissa vacanaṃ sutvā ‘‘nāyaṃ ajānitvā kathaissatī’’ti mūsikaṃ gahaitvā aikasmiṃ āpaṇai biḷārassatthāya datvā kākaṇikaṃ labhi| tāya kākaṇikāya phāṇitaṃ kiṇitvā aikaina kuṭaina pānīyaṃ gaṇhitvā arañ‍ñatau āgacchantai mālākārai disvā thaukaṃ thaukaṃ phāṇitakhaṇḍaṃ datvā uḷuṅkaina pānīyaṃ adāsi| tai tassa aikaikaṃ pupphamuṭṭhiṃ adaṃsu| sau taina pupphamūlaina punadivasaipi phāṇitañ‍ca pānīyaghaṭañ‍ca gahaitvā pupphārāmamaiva gatau| tassa taṃdivasaṃ mālākārā aḍḍhaaucitakai pupphagacchai datvā agamaṃsu| sau nacirassaiva iminā upāyaina aṭṭha kahāpaṇai labhi|

puna aikasmiṃ vātavuṭṭhidivasai chaḍḍitauyyānaṃ gantvā patitadārūnaṃ rāsiṃ katvā nisin‍nau rājakumbhakārassa santikā sauḷasa kahāpaṇai labhi| sau catuvīsatiyā kahāpaṇaisu jātaisu ‘‘atthi ayaṃ upāyau mayha’’nti nagaradvāratau avidūrai ṭhānai aikaṃ pānīyacāṭiṃ ṭhapaitvā pañ‍casatai tiṇahārakai pānīyaina upaṭṭhahi| tai  āhaṃsu – ‘‘tvaṃ, samma, amhākaṃ bahupakārau, kiṃ tai karaumā’’ti? saupi ‘‘mayhaṃ kic‍cai uppan‍nai karissathā’’ti vatvā itau citau ca vicarantau thalapathakammikaina ca jalapathakammikaina ca saddhiṃ mittasanthavaṃ akāsi| tassa thalapathakammikau ‘‘svai imaṃ nagaraṃ assavāṇijakau pañ‍ca assasatāni gahaitvā āgamissatī’’ti ācikkhi| sau tassa vacanaṃ sutvā tiṇahārakānaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ datvā aikaikaṃ tiṇakalāpaṃ diguṇaṃ katvā āharāpaisi| atha sau assānaṃ nagaraṃ paviṭṭhavailāya tiṇakalāpasahassaṃ  antaradvārai rāsiṃ katvā nisīdi| assavāṇijau sakalanagarai assānaṃ cāriṃ alabhitvā tassa sahassaṃ datvā taṃ tiṇaṃ gaṇhi|

tatau katipāhac‍cayainassa samuddakammikasahāyakau āraucaisi ‘‘paṭṭanaṃ mahānāvā āgatā’’ti| sau ‘‘atthi ayaṃ upāyau’’ti aṭṭhahi  kahāpaṇaihi sabbaparivārasampan‍naṃ tāvakālikaṃ rathaṃ gahaitvā nāvāpaṭṭanaṃ gantvā aikaṃ aṅgulimuddikaṃ nāvikassa sac‍cakāraṃ datvā avidūrai ṭhānai sāṇiṃ parikkhipāpaitvā tattha nisin‍nau purisai āṇāpaisi ‘‘bāhirakaisu vāṇijaisu āgataisu tatiyaina paṭihāraina āraucaithā’’ti| ‘‘nāvā āgatā’’ti sutvā bārāṇasitau satamattā vāṇijā ‘‘bhaṇḍaṃ gaṇhāmā’’ti āgamaṃsu| bhaṇḍaṃ tumhai na labhissatha, asukaṭṭhānai nāma mahāvāṇijaina sac‍cakārau din‍nauti| tai taisaṃ sutvā tassa santikaṃ āgatā, pādamūlikapurisā purimasañ‍ñāvasaina tatiyaina pāṭihāraina taisaṃ āgatabhāvaṃ āraucaisuṃ| tai satamattāpi vāṇijā aikaikaṃ sahassaṃ datvā taina saddhiṃ nāvāya pattikā hutvā puna aikaikaṃ sahassaṃ datvā pattiṃ vissaj‍jāpaitvā bhaṇḍaṃ attanau santakaṃ akaṃsu| sau  purisau dvai satasahassāni gahaitvā bārāṇasiṃ āgantvā ‘‘katañ‍ñunā bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti aikaṃ satasahassaṃ gahaitvā cūḷasaiṭṭhissa santikaṃ gatau|

atha taṃ cūḷasaiṭṭhi ‘‘kiṃ tai, tāta, katvā idaṃ dhanaṃ laddha’’nti pucchi| sau ‘‘tumhaihi kathitaupāyai ṭhatvā catumāsabbhantaraiyaiva laddha’’nti āha| saiṭṭhi tassa vacanaṃ sutvā ‘‘idāni aivarūpaṃ dārakaṃ parasantakaṃ kātuṃ na vaṭṭatī’’ti vayappattaṃ dhītaraṃ datvā sakalakuṭumbassa sāmikaṃ akāsi| saupi kulaputtau saiṭṭhinau ac‍cayaina tasmiṃ nagarai saiṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yathākammaṃ gatau| satthā dvai vatthūni kathaitvā anusandhiṃ ghaṭaitvā abhisambuddhakālai imaṃ gāthamāha –

‘‘appakainapi maidhāvī, pābhataina vicakkhaṇau|

samuṭṭhāpaiti attānaṃ, aṇuṃ aggiṃva sandhama’’nti|| (jā॰ 1.1.4)|

iti satthā dhammasabhāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ imaṃ kāraṇaṃ dassaisi| ayaṃ dvin‍nampi mahāsāvakānaṃ pubbapatthanatau paṭṭhāya anupubbikathā| aparabhāgai pana satthā ariyagaṇaparivutau dhammāsanai nisin‍nau  manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimminantānaṃ caitauvivaṭṭakusalānañ‍ca cūḷapanthakatthairaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi, sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalānaṃ mahāpanthakanti|

subhūtitthairavatthu

201. tatiyai  araṇavihārīnanti nik‍kilaisavihārīnaṃ| raṇanti hi rāgādayau kilaisā vuc‍canti, taisaṃ abhāvaina nik‍kilaisavihārau araṇavihārau nāma| sau yaisaṃ atthi, tai araṇavihārinau| taisaṃ  araṇavihārīnaṃ subhūtitthairau aggauti| kiñ‍cāpi hi añ‍ñaipi khīṇāsavā araṇavihārinauva, thairaina pana dhammadaisanāya aitaṃ nāmaṃ laddhaṃ| añ‍ñai hi bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisaintau uddissakaṃ katvā vaṇṇaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ vā kathainti, thairau pana dhammaṃ daisaintau satthārā daisitaniyāmatau anauk‍kamitvā daisaiti, tasmā araṇavihārīnaṃ aggau nāma jātau|

202. catutthai dakkhiṇaiyyānanti dakkhiṇārahānaṃ| tattha kiñ‍cāpi añ‍ñaipi khīṇāsavā aggadakkhiṇaiyyā, thairau pana piṇḍāya carantau gharai gharai maittājhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā samāpattitau vuṭṭhāya bhikkhaṃ gaṇhāti ‘‘aivaṃ bhikkhādāyakānaṃ mahapphalaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| tasmā dakkhiṇaiyyānaṃ aggauti vuttau| attabhāvau panassa susamiddhau, alaṅkatatauraṇaṃ viya cittapaṭau viya ca ativiya viraucati| tasmā subhūtīti vuc‍cati|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayaṃ kira padumuttarai bhagavati anuppan‍naiyaiva haṃsavatīnagarai brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai nibbatti, nandamāṇavautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau tayau vaidai uggaṇhitvā tattha sāraṃ apassantau attanau parivāraihi catucattālīsāya māṇavakasahassaihi saddhiṃ pabbatapādai isipabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā pañ‍ca abhiñ‍ñā aṭṭha samāpattiyau nibbattaisi, antaivāsikaipi jhānalābhinau akāsi|

tasmiṃ samayai padumuttarau bhagavā laukai nibbattitvā haṃsavatīnagaraṃ upanissāya viharantau aikadivasaṃ pac‍cūsasamayai laukaṃ aulaukaintau nandatāpasassa antaivāsikānaṃ jaṭilānaṃ arahattūpanissayaṃ nandatāpasassa ca dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa sāvakassa ṭhānantarapatthanaṃ disvā pātauva sarīrapaṭijagganaṃ  katvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pattacīvaramādāya sāriputtatthairassa vatthumhi vuttanayainaiva  nandatāpasassa assamaṃ agamāsi| tattha  phalāphaladānañ‍ca pupphāsanapañ‍ñāpanañ‍ca niraudhasamāpattisamāpaj‍janañ‍ca vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

satthā pana niraudhā vuṭṭhitau araṇavihāriaṅgaina ca dakkhiṇaiyyaṅgaina cāti dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ aikaṃ sāvakaṃ ‘‘isigaṇassa pupphāsanānumaudanaṃ karauhī’’ti āṇāpaisi| sau attanau visayai ṭhatvā taipiṭakaṃ sammasitvā anumaudanaṃ akāsi| tassa daisanāvasānai satthā sayaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| daisanāpariyausānai sabbai catucattālīsasahassāpi tāpasā arahattaṃ pāpuṇiṃsu| nandatāpasau pana anumaudakassa bhikkhunau nimittaṃ gaṇhitvā satthu daisanānusāraina ñāṇaṃ paisaituṃ nāsakkhi| satthā ‘‘aitha, bhikkhavau’’ti saisabhikkhūnaṃ hatthaṃ pasāraisi| sabbaipi antarahitakaisamassū iddhimayaparikkhārā vassasaṭṭhikatthairā viya ahaisuṃ|

nandatāpasau tathāgataṃ vanditvā sammukhai ṭhitau āha – ‘‘bhantai, yaina bhikkhunā isigaṇassa pupphāsanānumaudanā katā, kau nāmauyaṃ tumhākaṃ sāsanai’’ti? araṇavihāriaṅgaina ca dakkhiṇaiyyaṅgaina ca aitadaggaṃ pattau aisauti| ‘‘bhantai, ahampi iminā sattāhakataina adhikārakammaina añ‍ñaṃ sampattiṃ na patthaimi, anāgatai panāhaṃ aikassa buddhassa sāsanai ayaṃ thairau viya dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā anantarāyaṃ disvā byākaritvā pak‍kāmi| nandatāpasaupi kālaina kālaṃ dasabalassa santikai dhammaṃ sutvā aparihīnajjhānau brahmalaukai nibbattau| idamassa kalyāṇakammaṃ| antarā pana kammaṃ na kathiyati|

sau  kappasatasahassaṃ atik‍kamitvā sāvatthiyaṃ sumanasaiṭṭhissa gaihai nibbatti, subhūtītissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| aparabhāgai amhākaṃ satthā laukai nibbattau rājagahaṃ upanissāya viharati| tadā anāthapiṇḍikau saiṭṭhi sāvatthiyaṃ uṭṭhānakabhaṇḍaṃ gahaitvā attanau sahāyakassa rājagahasaiṭṭhinau gharaṃ gatau satthu uppan‍nabhāvaṃ ñatvā satthāraṃ sītavanai viharantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paṭhamadassanainaiva sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāya  satthāraṃ sāvatthiṃ āgamanatthāya yācitvā pañ‍cacattālīsayaujanai maggai yaujanai yaujanai satasahassaparic‍cāgaina vihārai patiṭṭhāpaitvā sāvatthiyaṃ rājamānaina aṭṭhakarīsappamāṇaṃ jaitarājakumārassa uyyānabhūmiṃ kauṭisanthāraina kiṇitvā tattha bhagavatau vihāraṃ kāraitvā adāsi| vihāramahadivasai ayaṃ subhūtikuṭimbikau anāthapiṇḍikasaiṭṭhinā  saddhiṃ gantvā dhammaṃ suṇantau saddhaṃ paṭilabhitvā pabbaji| sau upasampan‍nau dvai mātikā paguṇaṃ katvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathāpaitvā arañ‍ñai samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā maittājhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| dhammaṃ daisaintau vuttanayainaiva dhammaṃ kathaiti, piṇḍāya carantau vuttanayainaiva maittājhānatau vuṭṭhāya bhikkhaṃ gaṇhāti| atha naṃ satthā imaṃ kāraṇadvayaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇavihārīnañ‍ca dakkhiṇaiyyānañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

khadiravaniyaraivatatthairavatthu

203. pañ‍camai ārañ‍ñakānanti arañ‍ñavāsīnaṃ| raivatau khadiravaniyauti dhammasaināpatitthairassa kaniṭṭhabhātikau| sau yathā añ‍ñai thairā arañ‍ñai vasamānā vanasabhāgaṃ  udakasabhāgaṃ bhikkhācārasabhāgañ‍ca sal‍lakkhaitvā arañ‍ñai vasanti, na aivaṃ vasi| aitāni pana sabhāgāni anādiyitvā uj‍jaṅgalasakkharapāsāṇavisamai khadiravanai paṭivasati| tasmā ārañ‍ñakānaṃ aggauti vuttau|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayaṃ kira atītai padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai nibbattau mahāgaṅgāya payāgapatiṭṭhānatitthai nāvākammaṃ karauntau paṭivasati| tasmiṃ samayai satthā satasahassabhikkhuparivārau cārikaṃ carantau payāgapatiṭṭhānatitthaṃ sampāpuṇi| sau dasabalaṃ disvā cintaisi – ‘‘mayhaṃ kālaina kālaṃ buddhadassanaṃ nāma natthi, ayaṃ mai kalyāṇakammāyūhanakkhaṇau’’ti nāvāsaṅghāṭaṃ bandhāpaitvā upari cailavitānaṃ kāraitvā gandhamālādāmāni ausāraitvā haiṭṭhā varapautthakaṃ cittattharaṇaṃ attharāpaitvā saparivāraṃ satthāraṃ paratīraṃ tāraisi|

tasmiṃ  samayai satthā aikaṃ ārañ‍ñakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadaggai ṭhapaisi| sau nāvikau taṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi aivamaivaṃ anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai ārañ‍ñakānaṃ aggaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti satthāraṃ nimantaitvā sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā satthu pādamūlai nipaj‍jitvā, ‘‘bhantai, tumhaihi aitadaggai ṭhapitau sau bhikkhu viya ahampi anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai ārañ‍ñakānaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā anantarāyaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai gautamabuddhassa sāsanai  tvaṃ ārañ‍ñakānaṃ aggau bhavissasī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi| antarā pana añ‍ñaṃ kammaṃ na kathiyati|

sau yāvajīvaṃ kalyāṇakammaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadhakkhaittai nālakabrāhmaṇagāmai sārībrāhmaṇiyā kucchimhi paṭisandhiṃ gahaitvā tiṇṇaṃ bhātikānaṃ tissan‍nañ‍ca bhaginīnaṃ sabbakaniṭṭhau hutvā nibbatti, raivatautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| athassa mātāpitarau cintaisuṃ  – ‘‘vaḍḍhitavaḍḍhitai dārakai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā naitvā pabbājainti, amhākaṃ puttaṃ raivataṃ daharamaiva gharabandhanaina bandhissāmā’’ti samānakulatau dārikaṃ ānaitvā raivatassa ayyikaṃ vandāpaitvā, ‘‘amma, tava ayyikāya mahal‍lakatarā hauhī’’ti āhaṃsu| raivatau taisaṃ kathaṃ sutvā cintaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ dārikā daharā paṭhamavayai ṭhitā, imissā kira aivaṃvidhaṃ rūpaṃ mama ayyikāya rūpasadisaṃ bhavissati, pucchissāmi tāva naisaṃ adhippāya’’nti cintaitvā āha – ‘‘tumhai kiṃ kathaithā’’ti? tāta, ‘‘ayaṃ dārikā ayyikā viya tai jaraṃ pāpuṇātū’’ti vadāmāti| sau ‘‘imissā rūpaṃ aivaṃvidhaṃ bhavissatī’’ti pucchi| tāta, kiṃ vadaisi, mahāpuñ‍ñā aivaṃvidhā hauntīti|

sau cintaisi – ‘‘idaṃ kira rūpaṃ iminā niyāmaina valittacaṃ bhavissati palitakaisaṃ khaṇḍadantaṃ, ahaṃ aivarūpai rūpai raj‍jitvā kiṃ karissāmi, mama bhātikānaṃ gatamaggamaiva gamissāmī’’ti kīḷantau viya hutvā samavayai taruṇadārakai āha – ‘‘aitha, bhau, vidhāvanikaṃ karissāmā’’ti nikkhami| tāta, maṅgaladivasai mā bahi gacchāti| sau dārakaihi saddhiṃ kīḷantau viya attanau dhāvanavārai sampattai thaukaṃ gantvā papañ‍caitvā āgacchati| puna dutiyavārai sampattai tatau turitaṃ viya gantvā āgatau, tatiyavārai sampattai ‘‘ayaṃ mai kālau’’ti ñatvā sammukhaṭṭhānainaiva palāyitvā paṃsukūlikabhikkhūnaṃ nivāsaṭṭhānaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ gantvā thairai abhivādaitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| sappurisa mayaṃ taṃ na jānāma ‘‘kassāsi puttau’’ti, tvañ‍ca alaṅkataniyāmainaiva āgatau, kau taṃ pabbājaituṃ ussahissatīti| sau ubhau bāhā paggayha ‘‘vilumpanti maṃ vilumpanti  ma’’nti mahāravaṃ viravi| itau citau ca bhikkhū san‍nipatitvā ‘‘sappurisa, imasmiṃ ṭhānai tava vatthaṃ vā piḷandhanaṃ vā kauci gaṇhantau nāma natthi, tvañ‍ca ‘vilumpantī’ti vadasi, kiṃ sandhāya vadasī’’ti? bhantai, nāhaṃ vatthālaṅkāraṃ sandhāya vadāmi, tissan‍naṃ pana mai sampattīnaṃ vilaupau vattati, taṃ sandhāya vadāmi| maṃ tāva tumhai  mā pabbājayittha, bhātaraṃ pana mai jānāthāti| kaunāmau pana tai bhātāti? gihikālai upatissau nāma, idāni pana sāriputtau nāma jātauti vadantīti| ‘‘āvusau, aivaṃ santai ayaṃ kulaputtau amhākaṃ kaniṭṭhabhātikau nāma hauti, jaiṭṭhabhātikau nau dhammasaināpati puraitaraṃyaiva āha – ‘amhākaṃ ñātakā sabbaiva micchādiṭṭhikā, yau kauci amhākaṃ ñātīti āgacchati, taṃ yaina tainupāyaina pabbājaiyyathā’ti| ayaṃ pana thairassa ajjhattabhātikau, pabbājaitha na’’nti vatvā tacapañ‍cakakammaṭṭhānaṃ ācikkhitvā pabbājayiṃsu| atha naṃ paripuṇṇavassaṃ upasampādaitvā kammaṭṭhānai yaujayiṃsu|

thairau kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā ācariyupajjhāyānaṃ avidūrai ṭhānai vuttappakāraṃ khadiravanaṃ pavisitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauti| tassa ‘‘arahattaṃ appatvā dasabalaṃ vā bhātikatthairaṃ vā na passissāmī’’ti vāyamantassaiva tayau māsā atik‍kantā, sukhumālakulaputtassa lūkhabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa cittaṃ valītaṃ nāma hauti, kammaṭṭhānaṃ vimaukkhaṃ na gataṃ| sau taimāsac‍cayaina pavāraitvā vutthavassau hutvā tasmiṃyaiva ṭhānai samaṇadhammaṃ karauti| tassa samaṇadhammaṃ karauntassa cittaṃ aikaggaṃ ahausi, sau vipassanaṃ  vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi|

athāyasmā  sāriputtau satthāraṃ āha – ‘‘bhantai, mayhaṃ kira kaniṭṭhabhātā raivatau pabbajitau, sau abhiramaiyya vā na vā, gantvā naṃ passissāmī’’ti| bhagavā raivatassa āraddhavipassakabhāvaṃ ñatvā dvai vārai paṭikkhipitvā tatiyavārai yācitau arahattaṃ pattabhāvaṃ ñatvā, ‘‘sāriputta, ahampi gamissāmi, bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaihī’’ti| thairau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātaitvā, ‘‘āvusau, satthā cārikaṃ caritukāmau, gantukāmā āgacchantū’’ti sabbaisaṃyaiva āraucaisi| dasabalassa cārikatthāya gamanakālai auhīnakabhikkhū nāma appakā haunti, ‘‘satthu suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sarīraṃ passissāma, madhuradhammakathaṃ vā suṇissāmā’’ti yaibhuyyaina gantukāmāva bahukā haunti| iti satthā mahābhikkhusaṅghaparivārau ‘‘raivataṃ passissāmī’’ti nikkhantau|

athaikasmiṃ padaisai ānandatthairau dvaidhāpathaṃ patvā bhagavantaṃ pucchi – ‘‘bhantai, imasmiṃ ṭhānai dvai maggā, kataramaggaina saṅghau gacchatū’’ti| kataramaggau, ānanda, ujukauti? bhantai, ujumaggau tiṃsayaujanau amanussapathau, parihāramaggau pana saṭṭhiyaujanikau khaimau subhikkhauti| ānanda, sīvali amhaihi saddhiṃ āgatauti? āma, bhantai, āgatauti| taina hi saṅghau ujumaggamaiva gaṇhatu, sīvalissa puñ‍ñaṃ vīmaṃsissāmāti| satthā bhikkhusaṅghaparivārau sīvalitthairassa puñ‍ñavīmaṃsanatthaṃ aṭavimaggaṃ abhiruhi| maggaṃ abhiruhanaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhāya daivasaṅghau yaujanai yaujanai ṭhānai nagaraṃ māpaitvā buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa vasanatthāya vihārai paṭiyādaisi| daivaputtā rañ‍ñā paisitakammakārā viya hutvā yāgukhaj‍jakādīni gahaitvā ‘‘kahaṃ ayyau  sīvali, kahaṃ ayyau sīvalī’’ti pucchantā gacchanti| thairau taṃ sak‍kārasammānaṃ gaṇhāpaitvā satthu santikaṃ gacchati| satthā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ paribhuñ‍ji|

imināva niyāmaina satthā sak‍kārasammānaṃ anubhavantau daivasikaṃ yaujanaparamaṃ gantvā tiṃsayaujanikaṃ kantāraṃ atik‍kamma khadiravaniyatthairassa sabhāgaṭṭhānaṃ pattau| thairau satthu āgamanaṃ ñatvā attanau vasanaṭṭhānai buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa pahaunakavihārai dasabalassa gandhakuṭiṃ rattiṭṭhānadivāṭṭhānādīni ca iddhiyā māpaitvā tathāgatassa pac‍cuggamanaṃ  gatau| satthā alaṅkatapaṭiyattaina maggaina vihāraṃ pāvisi| atha tathāgatai gandhakuṭiṃ paviṭṭhai bhikkhū vassaggaina pattasaināsanāni pavisiṃsu| daivatā ‘‘akālau āhārassā’’ti aṭṭhavidhaṃ pānakaṃ āhariṃsu| satthā saṅghaina saddhiṃ pānakaṃ pivi| imināva niyāmaina tathāgatassa sak‍kārasammānaṃ anubhavantassaiva addhamāsau atik‍kantau|

athaikac‍cai uk‍kaṇṭhitabhikkhū aikasmiṃ ṭhānai nisīditvā kathaṃ uppādayiṃsu ‘‘satthā dasabalau ‘mayhaṃ aggasāvakassa kaniṭṭhabhātā’ti vatvā aivarūpaṃ navakammikabhikkhuṃ passituṃ āgatau, imassa vihārassa santikai jaitavanamahāvihārau vā vaiḷuvanavihārādayau vā kiṃ karissanti| ayampi bhikkhu aivarūpassa navakammassa kārakau, kiṃ nāma samaṇadhammaṃ karissatī’’ti? atha satthā cintaisi – ‘‘mayi ciraṃ vasantai idaṃ ṭhānaṃ ākiṇṇaṃ bhavissati, ārañ‍ñakā bhikkhū nāma pavivaikatthikā haunti, raivatassa aphāsuvihārau bhavissatī’’ti tatau raivatassa divāṭṭhānaṃ gatau| thairau aikakauva caṅkamanakauṭiyaṃ ālambanaphalakaṃ nissāya pāsāṇaphalakai nisin‍nau satthāraṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ disvā pac‍cuggantvā vandi|

atha naṃ satthā pucchi – ‘‘raivata, imaṃ vāḷamigaṭṭhānaṃ, caṇḍānaṃ hatthiassādīnaṃ saddaṃ sutvā kinti karausī’’ti? taisaṃ mai, bhantai, saddaṃ suṇatau arañ‍ñarati nāma uppaj‍jatīti| satthā  tasmiṃ ṭhānai raivatatthairassa pañ‍cahi gāthāsataihi arañ‍ñai nivāsānisaṃsaṃ nāma kathaitvā punadivasai avidūrai ṭhānai piṇḍāya caritvā raivatatthairaṃ nivattaitvā yaihi bhikkhūhi thairassa avaṇṇau kathitau, taisaṃ kattarayaṭṭhiupāhanātailanāḷichattānaṃ pamussanabhāvaṃ akāsi| tai attanau parikkhāratthāya nivattā āgatamaggainaiva gacchantāpi taṃ ṭhānaṃ sal‍lakkhaituṃ na sak‍kaunti| paṭhamaṃ hi tai alaṅkatapaṭiyattaina maggaina gantvā taṃdivasaṃ pana visamamaggaina gacchantā tasmiṃ tasmiṃ ṭhānai uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdanti, jāṇukaina gacchanti| tai gumbai ca gacchai ca kaṇṭakai ca maddantā attanau vasitasabhāgaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā tasmiṃ tasmiṃ khadirakhāṇukai attanau chattaṃ sañ‍jānanti, upāhanaṃ kattarayaṭṭhiṃ tailanāḷiṃ sañ‍jānanti| tai tasmiṃ samayai ‘‘iddhimā  ayaṃ bhikkhū’’ti ñatvā attanau parikkhārai ādāya ‘‘dasabalassa paṭiyattasak‍kārau nāma aivarūpau hautī’’ti vadantā āgamaṃsu|

puratau gatabhikkhū, visākhā upāsikā, attanau gaihai nisin‍nakālai pucchati – ‘‘manāpaṃ nu khau, bhantai, raivatatthairassa vasanaṭṭhāna’’nti? manāpaṃ upāsikai nandanavanacittalatādipaṭibhāgaṃ taṃ saināsananti| atha naisaṃ sabbapacchatau āgatabhikkhū pucchi – ‘‘manāpaṃ, ayyā, raivatatthairassa vasanaṭṭhāna’’nti| mā puccha upāsikai, kathaituṃ ayuttaṭṭhānamaitaṃ, uj‍jaṅgalaṃ sakkharapāsāṇavisamaṃ khadiravanaṃ aitaṃ, tattha sau bhikkhu viharatīti| visākhā, purimānañ‍ca pacchimānañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kathaṃ  sutvā ‘‘kaisaṃ nu khau kathā sac‍cā’’ti pacchābhattai gandhamālaṃ ādāya dasabalassa upaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā, vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā satthāraṃ pucchi – ‘‘bhantai, raivatatthairassa vasanaṭṭhānaṃ aikac‍cai, ayyā, vaṇṇainti, aikac‍cai nindanti, kiṃ nāmaitaṃ, bhantai’’ti? visākhai ramaṇīyaṃ vā hautu mā vā, yasmiṃ ṭhānai ariyānaṃ cittaṃ ramati, tadaiva ṭhānaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ nāmāti vatvā imaṃ gāthamāha –

‘‘gāmai vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, nin‍nai vā yadi vā thalai|

yattha arahantau viharanti, taṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyaka’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 98; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.261)|

atha satthā aparabhāgai jaitavanamahāvihārai ariyagaṇamajjhai nisin‍nau thairaṃ ārañ‍ñakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

kaṅkhāraivatatthairavatthu

204. chaṭṭhai  jhāyīnanti jhānalābhīnaṃ jhānābhiratānaṃ| sau kira thairau yā jhānasamāpattiyau dasabalau samāpaj‍jati, tatau appataraṃ ṭhapaitvā bahutarā samāpaj‍jati| tasmā jhāyīnaṃ aggau nāma jātau| kaṅkhāyanabhāvaina kaṅkhāraivatauti vuc‍cati| kaṅkhā nāma kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, kuk‍kuc‍cakauti atthau| kiṃ pana añ‍ñai kuk‍kuc‍cakā natthīti? atthi, ayaṃ pana thairau kappiyaipi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppādaisi| tainassa kuk‍kuc‍cakatā atipākaṭā jātāti kaṅkhāraivatautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gatau|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayaṃ kira padumuttarabuddhakālai purimanayainaiva mahājanaina saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ  suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ jhānābhiratānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi anāgatai aivarūpaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti daisanāvasānai satthāraṃ nimantaitvā purimanayainaiva sattāhaṃ mahāsak‍kāraṃ katvā bhagavantaṃ āha – ‘‘bhantai, ahaṃ iminā adhikārakammaina na  añ‍ñaṃ sampattiṃ patthaimi, yathā pana sau tumhaihi itau sattadivasamatthakai bhikkhu jhāyīnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapitau, aivaṃ ahampi anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai jhāyīnaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā anāgataṃ aulaukaitvā samijjhanabhāvaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai kappasatasahassāvasānai gautamau nāma buddhau uppaj‍jissati, tassa sāsanai tvaṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau bhavissasī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

sau yāvajīvaṃ kalyāṇakammaṃ katvā kappasatasahassaṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsaritvā amhākaṃ bhagavatau kālai sāvatthinagarai mahābhaugakulai nibbattau pacchābhattaṃ dhammassavanatthaṃ gacchantaina mahājanaina saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dasabalassa dhammakathaṃ sutvā saddhaṃ paṭilabhitvā pabbajitau upasampadaṃ labhitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathāpaitvā jhānaparikammaṃ karauntau jhānalābhī hutvā jhānamaiva pādakaṃ katvā arahattaphalaṃ pāpuṇi| sau dasabalaina samāpaj‍jitabbasamāpattīnaṃ appatarā ṭhapaitvā bahutarā samāpaj‍jantau ahaurattaṃ jhānaisu ciṇṇavasī ahausi| atha naṃ aparabhāgai satthā imaṃ guṇaṃ gahaitvā jhāyīnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| ‘‘akappiyau, āvusau guḷau, akappiyā muggā’’ti (mahāva॰ 272) aivaṃ pana kappiyaisvaiva vatthūsu  kuk‍kuc‍cassa uppāditatāya kuk‍kuc‍casaṅkhātāya kaṅkhāya bhāvaina kaṅkhāraivatauti saṅkhaṃ gatauti|

sauṇakauḷivisatthairavatthu

205. sattamai āraddhavīriyānanti paggahitavīriyānaṃ paripuṇṇavīriyānaṃ| sauṇau kauḷivisauti sauṇauti tassa nāmaṃ, kauḷivisauti gauttaṃ| kauṭivaissauti vā atthau, issariyaina kauṭippattassa vaissakulassa dārakauti adhippāyau| yasmā pana añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  vīriyaṃ nāma vaḍḍhaitabbaṃ hauti, thairassa pana hāpaitabbamaiva ahausi| tasmā aisa āraddhavīriyānaṃ aggau nāma jātau|

tassa  pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayaṃ kira atītai padumuttarabuddhakālai saiṭṭhikulai nibbatti, sirivaḍḍhakumārautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau purimanayainaiva vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ āraddhavīriyānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi anāgatai aivarūpaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti daisanāpariyausānai dasabalaṃ nimantaitvā sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā vuttanayainaiva patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā tassa patthanāya samijjhanabhāvaṃ disvā purimanayainaiva byākaritvā vihāraṃ gatau|

saupi sirivaḍḍhasaiṭṭhi yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau kappasatasahassaṃ atik‍kamitvā imasmiṃ kappai parinibbutai kassapadasabalai anuppan‍nai amhākaṃ bhagavati bārāṇasiyaṃ kulagaihai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| sau attanau sahāyakaihi saddhiṃ gaṅgāyaṃ kīḷati| tasmiṃ samayai aikau jiṇṇacīvarikau pac‍caikabuddhau ‘‘bārāṇasiṃ upanissāya gaṅgātīrai paṇṇasālaṃ katvā vassaṃ upagacchissāmī’’ti udakaina samupabyūḷhai daṇḍakai ca val‍liyau ca saṃkaḍḍhati| ayaṃ kumārau sahāyakaihi saddhiṃ gantvā abhivādaitvā ṭhitau, ‘‘bhantai, kiṃ karauthā’’ti pucchi| kumāra upakaṭṭhai antauvassai pabbajitānaṃ vasanaṭṭhānaṃ nāma laddhuṃ vaṭṭatīti| ‘‘bhantai, aj‍jaiva aikadivasaṃ ayyau yathā tathā āgamaitu, ahaṃ svai ayyassa vasanaṭṭhānaṃ karissāmī’’ti āha| pac‍caikabuddhau ‘‘tassaiva kumārassa saṅgahaṃ karissāmī’’ti āgatattā adhivāsaisi| sau tassa adhivāsanaṃ viditvā gatau punadivasai sak‍kārasammānaṃ  saj‍jaitvā pac‍caikabuddhassa āgamanaṃ aulaukaintau aṭṭhāsi| pac‍caikabuddhaupi ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau aj‍ja bhikkhācāraṃ labhissāmī’’ti āvaj‍jaintau ñatvā tassaiva gaihadvāraṃ agamāsi|

kumārau pac‍caikabuddhaṃ disvā sampiyāyamānau pattaṃ ādāya bhikkhaṃ datvā ‘‘imaṃ antauvassaṃ mayhaṃ gaihadvāramaiva āgacchatha, bhantai’’ti paṭiñ‍ñaṃ gahaitvā pac‍caikabuddhai bhattakic‍caṃ katvā pak‍kantai attanau sahāyakaihi saddhiṃ gantvā aikadivasainaiva pac‍caikabuddhassa vasanapaṇṇasālañ‍ca caṅkamanañ‍ca rattiṭṭhānadivāṭṭhānāni ca kārāpaitvā adāsi| tassaiva paṇṇasālaṃ pavisanavailāya  haritūpalittāya bhūmiyā ‘‘pādaisu kalalaṃ mā laggī’’ti attanau pārupanaṃ satasahassagghanakaṃ rattakambalaṃ bhūmattharaṇaṃ santharitvā kambalassa vaṇṇaina saddhiṃ pac‍caikabuddhassa sarīrappabhaṃ aikasadisaṃ disvā ativiya pasan‍nau hutvā āha – ‘‘yathā tumhaihi ak‍kantakālatau paṭṭhāya imassa kambalassa ativiya pabhā viraucati, aivamaiva mayhampi nibbattanibbattaṭṭhānai hatthapādānaṃ vaṇṇau bandhujīvakapupphavaṇṇau hautu, satakkhattuṃ vihatakappāsapaṭalaphassasadisauva phassau hautū’’ti| sau taimāsaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ upaṭṭhahitvā pavāritakālai ticīvaraṃ adāsi| pac‍caikabuddhau paripuṇṇapattacīvarau gandhamādanamaiva gatau|

saupi kulaputtau daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau amhākaṃ bhagavatau kālai kāḷacampānagarai upasaiṭṭhissa gharai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| tassa paṭisandhiggahaṇakālatau paṭṭhāya saiṭṭhikulaṃ anaikāni  paṇṇākārasahassāni āgacchanti| jātadivasai ca sakalanagaraṃ aikasak‍kārasammānaṃ ahausi| athassa nāmaggahaṇadivasai mātāpitarau ‘‘amhākaṃ puttau attanau nāmaṃ gaṇhitvāva āgatau, rattasuvaṇṇarasaparisittā viyassa sarīracchavī’’ti sauṇakumārautvaivassa nāmaṃ akaṃsu|

athassa saṭṭhi dhātiyau upanaitvā daivakumāraṃ viya naṃ sukhaina vaḍḍhaisuṃ| tassa aivarūpaṃ āhāravidhānaṃ ahausi – saṭṭhikarīsamattaṃ ṭhānaṃ kasitvā tividhaina udakaina pausainti| kaidārai pavisantīsu udakamātikāsu khīraudakassa ca gandhaudakassa ca anaikāni cāṭisahassāni āsiñ‍canti| sālisīsānaṃ khīraggahaṇakālai sukādīnaṃ pāṇānaṃ ucchiṭṭhakaraṇanivāraṇatthaṃ vīhigabbhānaṃ sukhumālabhāvatthañ‍ca pariyantaparikkhaipai ca antarantarā ca thambhai nikhanitvā upari daṇḍakai datvā kilañ‍jaihi chādaitvā samantā sāṇiyā parikkhipitvā sabbapariyantai ārakkhaṃ gaṇhanti| sassai nipphan‍nai kauṭṭhai catujātigandhaihi paribhaṇḍaṃ katvā upari uttamagandhaihi paribhāvainti| anaikasahassapurisā khaittaṃ auruyha sālisīsāni vaṇṭaisu chinditvā muṭṭhimuṭṭhiyau katvā raj‍jukaihi bandhitvā sukkhāpainti| tatau kauṭṭhakassa haiṭṭhimatalai gandhai santharitvā upari sālisīsāni santharanti| aivaṃ aikantarikaṃ katvā santharantā kauṭṭhakaṃ pūraitvā dvāraṃ pidahanti, tivassasampattakālai kauṭṭhakaṃ vivaranti| vivaṭakālai sakalanagaraṃ sugandhagandhikaṃ hauti| sālimhi pahatai dhuttā thusai kiṇitvā gaṇhanti, kuṇḍakaṃ pana cūḷupaṭṭhākā labhanti| musalaghaṭṭitakai sālitaṇḍulai vicinitvā gaṇhanti| tai suvaṇṇahīrakapacchiyaṃ pakkhipitvā satakālaṃ parissāvaitvā gahitai pak‍kuthitajātirasai aikavāraṃ pakkhipitvā uddharanti, pamukhaṭṭhānaṃ sumanapupphasadisaṃ hauti| taṃ bhaujanaṃ suvaṇṇasarakai pakkhipitvā pak‍kuthitaappaudakamadhupāyāsapūritassa rajatathālassa upari katvā ādāya gantvā saiṭṭhiputtassa puratau ṭhapainti|

sau attanau yāpanamattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā gandhavāsitaina udakaina mukhaṃ vikkhālaitvā hatthapādai dhauvati| athassa dhautahatthapādassa nānappakāraṃ mukhavāsaṃ upanainti| tassa ak‍kamanaṭṭhānai varapautthakacittattharaṇaṃ attharanti| hatthapādatalānissa bandhujīvakapupphavaṇṇāni haunti, satakālavihatakappāsassa viya phassau, pādatalaisu maṇikuṇḍalāvattavaṇṇāni laumāni jāyiṃsu| sau kassacidaiva kujjhitvā ‘‘ājānāhi bhūmiṃ ak‍kamissāmī’’ti vadati| tassa vayappattassa tiṇṇaṃ utūnaṃ anucchavikai tayau pāsādai kāraitvā nāṭakāni ca upaṭṭhāpaisuṃ| sau mahāsampattiṃ anubhavantau daivau mañ‍ñai paṭivasati|

atha amhākaṃ satthari sabbañ‍ñutaṃ patvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kai rājagahaṃ upanissāya viharantai pādalaumadassanatthaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina pak‍kausāpaitvā asītiyā gāmiyasahassaihi saddhiṃ satthu santikaṃ pahitau dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau satthāraṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| atha naṃ bhagavā ‘‘anuñ‍ñātausi mātāpitūhī’’ti pucchitvā ananuñ‍ñātabhāvaṃ sutvā ‘‘na khau, sauṇa, tathāgatā mātāpitūhi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ puttaṃ pabbājaintī’’ti paṭikkhipi| sau ‘‘sādhu bhagavā’’ti tathāgatassa vacanaṃ sirasā  sampaṭicchitvā mātāpitūnaṃ santikaṃ gantvā anujānāpaitvā satthu santikaṃ āgamma añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau santikai pabbaji| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau, vitthāratau panassa pabbaj‍jāvidhānaṃ pāḷiyaṃ (mahāva॰ 243) āgatamaiva|

tassa pabbaj‍jañ‍ca upasampadañ‍ca labhitvā rājagahai viharantassa sambahulā ñātisālauhitā  ca sandiṭṭhasambhattā ca sak‍kārasammānaṃ āharanti, rūpanipphattiyā  vaṇṇaṃ kathainti, añ‍ñaipi janā passituṃ āgacchanti| thairau cintaisi – ‘‘mama santikaṃ bahū janā āgacchanti, kammaṭṭhānai vā vipassanāya vā kammaṃ kātuṃ kathaṃ sakkhissāmi, yaṃnūnāhaṃ satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathāpaitvā sītavanasusānaṃ gantvā samaṇadhammaṃ karaiyyaṃ| tatra hi susānanti jigucchitvā bahū janā nāgamissanti, aivaṃsantai mama kic‍caṃ matthakaṃ pāpuṇissatī’’ti satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathāpaitvā sītavanaṃ gantvā samaṇadhammaṃ kātuṃ ārabhi| sau cintaisi – ‘‘mayhaṃ sarīraṃ paramasukhumālaṃ, na khau pana sak‍kā sukhainaiva sukhaṃ pāpuṇituṃ, kāyaṃ kilamaitvāpi samaṇadhammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti| tatau ṭhānacaṅkamamaiva adhiṭṭhāya padhānamakāsi| tassa sukhumālānaṃ pādatalānaṃ antantaihi phauṭā uṭṭhāya bhij‍jiṃsu, caṅkamau aikalauhitauva ahausi| pādaisu avahantaisu jaṇṇukaihipi hatthaihipi vāyamitvā caṅkamati| aivaṃ vīriyaṃ daḷhaṃ karauntaupi aubhāsamattampi nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntau cintaisi – ‘‘sacai añ‍ñaupi āraddhavīriyau bhavaiyya, mādisauva bhavaiyya| ahaṃ khau pana aivaṃ vāyamantaupi maggaṃ vā phalaṃ vā uppādaituṃ na sak‍kaumi, addhā naivāhaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, na vipañ‍citañ‍ñū, na naiyyau, padaparamaina mayā bhavitabbaṃ| kiṃ mai pabbaj‍jāya, hīnāyāvattitvā  bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jissāmi puñ‍ñāni ca karissāmī’’ti|

tasmiṃ samayai satthā thairassa vitak‍kaṃ ñatvā sāyanhasamayai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau tattha gantvā lauhitaina phuṭṭhaṃ caṅkamaṃ disvā thairaṃ vīṇauvādaina (mahāva॰ 243) auvaditvā vīriyasamathayaujanatthāya tassa kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathaitvā gijjhakūṭamaiva gatau| sauṇatthairaupi dasabalassa sammukhā auvādaṃ labhitvā nacirassaiva arahattai patiṭṭhāsi| atha satthā aparabhāgai jaitavanai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau thairaṃ āraddhavīriyānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

sauṇakuṭikaṇṇatthairavatthu

206. aṭṭhamai kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānanti vāk‍karaṇaṃ vuc‍cati vacanakiriyā, madhuravacanānanti atthau| ayañhi thairau dasabalaina saddhiṃ aikagandhakuṭiyā tathāgatassa  madhuraina saraina dhammakathaṃ kathaisi| athassa satthā sādhukāraṃ adāsi| tasmā sau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggau  nāma jātau| sauṇauti tassa nāmaṃ, kauṭiagghanakaṃ pana kaṇṇapiḷandhanaṃ dhāraisi| tasmā kuṭikaṇṇauti vuc‍cati, kauṭikaṇṇauti atthau|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayampi padumuttarabuddhakālai purimanayainaiva mahājanaina saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhatvā satthu dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti cintaitvā dasabalaṃ nimantaitvā sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā, ‘‘bhantai, yaṃ bhikkhuṃ tumhai itau  sattadivasamatthakai kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapayittha, ahampi imassa adhikārakammassa phalaina anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai tathārūpau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā tassa anantarāyaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai gautamabuddhassa sāsanai kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggau bhavissasī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

saupi yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā kappasatasahassaṃ daivaisu ca manussaisu ca saṃsarantau amhākaṃ dasabalassa uppattitau puraitaramaiva daivalaukā cavitvā kāḷiyā nāma kuraragharikāya upāsikāya kucchismiṃ paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| sā paripak‍kai gabbhai rājagahanagarai attanau kulanivaisanaṃ āgatā|

tasmiṃ samayai amhākaṃ satthā sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau isipatanai dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaisi| dhammacak‍kappavattanai dasasahassacak‍kavāḷadaivatā san‍nipatiṃsu| tattha aikau aṭṭhavīsatiyā yakkhasaināpatīnaṃ abbhantarai sātāgirau nāma yakkhau dasabalassa dhammakathaṃ sutvā sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāya cintaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau ayaṃ aivaṃ madhuradhammakathā mama sahāyaina haimavataina sutā na sutā’’ti? sau daivasaṅghassa antarai aulaukaintau taṃ apassitvā ‘‘addhā mama sahāyau tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ uppan‍nabhāvaṃ na jānāti, gacchāmi dasabalassa caiva vaṇṇaṃ kathaissāmi, paṭividdhadhammañ‍ca āraucaissāmī’’ti attanau parisāya saddhiṃ rājagahamatthakaina tassa santikaṃ pāyāsi|

haimavataupi  tiyaujanasahassaṃ himavantaṃ akālapupphitaṃ disvā ‘‘mama sahāyaina sātāgiraina saddhiṃ himavantakīḷitaṃ kīḷissāmī’’ti attanau parisāya saddhiṃ rājagahamatthakainaiva  pāyāsi| taisaṃ dvin‍nampi aggabalakāyā kulagharikāya kāḷiupāsikāya nivaisanamatthakai samāgantvā ‘‘tumhai kassa parisā, mayaṃ sātāgirassa| tumhai kassa parisā, mayaṃ haimavatassā’’ti āhaṃsu| tai haṭṭhatuṭṭhāva gantvā taisaṃ yakkhasaināpatīnaṃ āraucayiṃsu| taipi taṃkhaṇañ‍ñaiva  upāsikāya nivaisanamatthakai samāgacchiṃsu| sātāgirau haimavataṃ āha – ‘‘kahaṃ, samma, gacchasī’’ti? tava santikaṃ sammāti| kiṃkāraṇāti? himavantaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā tayā saddhiṃ tattha kīḷissāmīti| tvaṃ pana, samma, kahaṃ gacchasīti? tava santikaṃ, sammāti| kiṃkāraṇāti? tvaṃ himavantassa kaina pupphitabhāvaṃ jānāsīti? na jānāmi, sammāti| suddhaudanamahārājassa puttau siddhatthakumārau dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ kampaitvā paṭividdhasabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇau dasasahassacak‍kavāḷadaivatānaṃ majjhai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaisi| tassa pavattitabhāvaṃ na jānāsīti? na jānāmi, sammāti| tvaṃ aittakamaiva ṭhānaṃ pupphitanti añ‍ñāsi, tassa pana purisassa sak‍kāratthāya sakaladasasahassacak‍kavāḷaṃ aikamālāguḷasadisaṃ aj‍ja jātaṃ sammāti| mālā tāva pupphantu, tayā sau satthā akkhīni pūraitvā diṭṭhauti| āma, samma, satthā ca mai diṭṭhau, dhammau ca sutau, amatañ‍ca pītaṃ| ahaṃ ‘‘aitaṃ amatadhammaṃ tampi jānāpaissāmī’’ti tava santikaṃ āgatausmi, sammāti| taisaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kathaintānaṃyaiva upāsikā sirisayanatau uṭṭhāya nisin‍nā taṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ sutvā saddai nimittaṃ gaṇhi| ‘‘ayaṃ saddau uddhaṃ, na haiṭṭhā, amanussabhāsitau, nau manussabhāsitau’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā auhitasautā paggahitamānasā hutvā nisīdi| tatau –

‘‘aj‍ja pan‍narasau upausathau (iti sātāgirau yakkhau),

dibbā ratti upaṭṭhitā|

anaumanāmaṃ satthāraṃ,

handa passāma gautama’’nti|| (su॰ ni॰ 153) –

aivaṃ  sātāgiraina vuttai –

‘‘kac‍ci manau supaṇihitau (iti haimavatau yakkhau),

sabbabhūtaisu tādinau|

kac‍ci  iṭṭhai aniṭṭhai ca,

saṅkappassa vasīkatā’’ti|| (su॰ ni॰ 154)|

aivaṃ  haimavatau satthu kāyasamācārañ‍ca ājīvañ‍ca manausamācārañ‍ca pucchi| pucchitaṃ pucchitaṃ sātāgirau vissaj‍jaisi| aivaṃ satthu sarīravaṇṇaguṇavaṇṇakathanavasaina haimavatasuttantai niṭṭhitai haimavatau sahāyakassa dhammadaisanānusāraina ñāṇaṃ paisaitvā sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhahi|

atha, kāḷī upāsikā, parassa dhammai daisīyamānai tathāgataṃ adiṭṭhapubbāva hutvā anussavappasādaṃ uppādaitvā parassa vaḍḍhitaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jamānā viya sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāsi| sā sabbamātugāmānaṃ antarai paṭhamakasautāpan‍nā sabbajaiṭṭhikā ahausi| tassā saha sautāpattibhāvaina tamaiva rattiṃ gabbhavuṭṭhānaṃ jātaṃ, paṭiladdhadārakassa nāmaggahaṇadivasai sauṇauti nāmaṃ akāsi| sā yathāruciyā kulagaihai vasitvā kulagharamaiva agamāsi|

tasmiṃ samayai mahākac‍cānatthairau taṃ nagaraṃ upanissāya upavattai pabbatai paṭivasati| upāsikā thairaṃ upaṭṭhāti| thairau nibaddhaṃ tassā nivaisanaṃ gacchati| sauṇadārakaupi nibaddhaṃ thairassa santikai vicarantau vissāsikau ahausi| sau aparaina samayaina thairassa santikai pabbaji| thairau taṃ upasampādaitukāmau tīṇi vassāni gaṇaṃ pariyaisitvā upasampādaisi| sau upasampan‍nau kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathāpaitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ patvā thairassaiva santikai suttanipātaṃ uggaṇhitvā vutthavassau pavāraitvā satthāraṃ passitukāmau hutvā upajjhāyaṃ āpucchi| thairau āha – ‘‘sauṇa, tayi gatai satthā taṃ aikagandhakuṭiyaṃ vasāpaitvā dhammaṃ ajjhaisissati, tvaṃ dhammaṃ kathaissasi| satthā tava dhammakathāya pasīditvā tuyhaṃ varaṃ dassati| tvaṃ varaṃ gaṇhantau imañ‍ca imañ‍ca gaṇhāhi, mama vacanaina dasabalassa pādai vandāhī’’ti| sau upajjhāyaina anuñ‍ñātau  mātuupāsikāya gaihaṃ gantvā āraucaisi| sāpi ‘‘sādhu, tāta, tvaṃ dasabalaṃ passituṃ gacchantau imaṃ kambalaṃ āharitvā satthu vasanagandhakuṭiyā bhūmattharaṇaṃ katvā attharāhī’’ti kambalaṃ adāsi| sauṇatthairau taṃ ādāya saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā anupubbaina satthu vasanaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā dasabalassa buddhāsanai nisin‍navailāyamaiva upasaṅkamitvā abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| satthā taina saddhiṃ paṭisanthāraṃ katvā ānandatthairaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ānanda, imassa bhikkhussa  saināsanaṃ jānāhī’’ti| thairau satthu adhippāyaṃ ñatvā antaugandhakuṭiyaṃyaiva bhūmattharaṇaṃ ussāraintau viya atthari|

atha khau bhagavā bahudaivarattiṃ ajjhaukāsai vītināmaitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi, āyasmāpi khau sauṇau bahudaivarattiṃ ajjhaukāsai vītināmaitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| satthā pacchimayāmai sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaitvā pac‍cūsasamayai vuṭṭhāya nisīditvā ‘‘aittakaina kālaina sauṇassa kāyadarathau paṭippassaddhau bhavissatī’’ti ñatvā āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ ajjhaisi – ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ bhikkhu dhammau bhāsitu’’nti| sauṇatthairau madhurassaraina aikabyañ‍janampi avināsaintau aṭṭhakavaggiyāni suttāni (su॰ ni॰ 772 ādayau) abhāsi| kathāpariyausānai bhagavā sādhukāraṃ datvā ‘‘suggahitau tai bhikkhu dhammau, mayā daisitakālai ca aj‍ja ca aikasadisāva daisanā, kiñ‍ci ūnaṃ vā adhikaṃ vā natthī’’ti pasan‍nabhāvaṃ pakāsaisi| sauṇatthairaupi ‘‘ayaṃ aukāsau’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā upajjhāyassa vacanaina dasabalaṃ vanditvā vinayadharapañ‍camaina gaṇaina upasampadaṃ ādiṃ katvā sabbai varai yāci, satthā adāsi| puna thairau mātuupāsikāya vacanaina vanditvā ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, upāsikāya tumhākaṃ vasanagandhakuṭiyaṃ bhūmattharaṇatthaṃ kambalau  pahitau’’ti kambalaṃ datvā uṭṭhāyāsanā satthāraṃ vanditvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau, vitthāratau pana thairassa pabbaj‍jaṃ ādiṃ katvā sabbaṃ suttai āgatamaiva|

iti thairau satthu santikā aṭṭha varai labhitvā upajjhāyassa santikaṃ gantvā sabbaṃ taṃ pavattiṃ āraucaisi| punadivasai mātuupāsikāya nivaisanadvāraṃ gantvā bhikkhāya aṭṭhāsi| upāsikā ‘‘puttau kira mai dvārai ṭhitau’’ti sutvā vaigaina āgantvā abhivādaitvā hatthatau pattaṃ gahaitvā  antaunivaisanai nisīdāpaitvā bhaujanaṃ adāsi| atha naṃ bhattakic‍capariyausānai āha – ‘‘diṭṭhau tai, tāta, dasabalau’’ti? āma upāsikaiti| vanditau tai mama vacanaināti? āma vanditau, saupi ca mai kambalau tathāgatassa vasanaṭṭhānai bhūmattharaṇaṃ katvā atthatauti| kiṃ, tāta, tayā kira satthu dhammakathā kathitā, satthārā ca tai sādhukārau din‍nauti? tayā kathaṃ ñātaṃ upāsikaiti? tāta, mayhaṃ gaihai adhivatthā daivatā dasabalaina tuyhaṃ sādhukāraṃ din‍nadivasai ‘‘sakaladasasahassacak‍kavāḷai daivatā sādhukāraṃ adaṃsū’’ti āha – tāta, tayā kathitadhammakathaṃ buddhānaṃ kathitaniyāmainaiva mayhampi kathaituṃ pac‍cāsīsāmīti| thairau mātu kathaṃ sampaṭicchi| sā tassa adhivāsanaṃ viditvā dvārai maṇḍapaṃ kāraitvā dasabalassa kathitaniyāmainaiva attanau dhammakathaṃ  kathāpaisīti vatthu aittha samuṭṭhitaṃ| satthā aparabhāgai ariyagaṇamajjhai nisin‍nau thairaṃ kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

sīvalitthairavatthu

207. navamai  lābhīnaṃ yadidaṃ sīvalīti ṭhapaitvā tathāgataṃ lābhīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sīvalitthairau aggauti dassaiti| tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayampi atītai padumuttarabuddhakālai vuttanayainaiva vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ lābhīnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi anāgatai aivarūpaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti dasabalaṃ nimantaitvā purimanayainaiva sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā ‘‘bhagavā ahampi iminā adhikārakammaina añ‍ñaṃ sampattiṃ na patthaimi, anāgatai pana aikassa buddhassa sāsanai ahampi tumhaihi sau aitadaggai ṭhapitabhikkhu viya lābhīnaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā anantarāyaṃ disvā ‘‘ayaṃ tai patthanā anāgatai gautamassa buddhassa sāsanai samijjhissatī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

saupi kulaputtau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau vipassībuddhakālai bandhumatīnagaratau avidūrai aikasmiṃ gāmai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| tasmiṃ samayai bandhumatīnagaravāsinau rañ‍ñā saddhiṃ sākacchitvā sākacchitvā dasabalassa  dānaṃ dainti| tai aikadivasaṃ sabbaiva aikatau hutvā dānaṃ daintā ‘‘kiṃ nu khau amhākaṃ dānamukhai natthī’’ti madhuñ‍ca guḷadadhiñ‍ca na addasaṃsu| tai ‘‘yatau kutauci āharissāmā’’ti janapadatau nagaraṃ pavisanamaggai purisaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ| tadā aisa kulaputtau attanau gāmatau guḷadadhivārakaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘kiñ‍cidaiva āharissāmī’’ti nagaraṃ gacchantau mukhaṃ dhauvitvā ‘‘dhautahatthapādau pavisissāmī’’ti phāsukaṭṭhānaṃ aulaukaintau naṅgalasīsamattaṃ nimmakkhikaṃ daṇḍakamadhuṃ disvā ‘‘puñ‍ñaina mai idaṃ uppan‍na’’nti gahaitvā nagaraṃ pavisati| nāgaraihi ṭhapitapurisau taṃ disvā, ‘‘bhau purisa, kassimaṃ āharasī’’ti  pucchi| na kassaci sāmi, vik‍kiṇituṃ pana mai idaṃ ānītanti| taina hi, bhau purisa, imaṃ kahāpaṇaṃ gahaitvā aitaṃ madhuñ‍ca guḷadadhiñ‍ca daihīti|

sau cintaisi – ‘‘idaṃ na bahumūlaṃ, ayañ‍ca aikappahārainaiva bahuṃ daiti, vīmaṃsituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti| tatau naṃ ‘‘nāhaṃ aikakahāpaṇaina daimī’’ti āha| yadi aivaṃ, dvai gahaitvā daihīti| dvīhipi na daimīti| aitainupāyaina vaḍḍhantaṃ vaḍḍhantaṃ sahassaṃ pāpuṇi| sau cintaisi – ‘‘atiañchituṃ na vaṭṭati, hautu tāva, imassa kattabbakic‍caṃ pucchissāmī’’ti| atha naṃ āha – ‘‘idaṃ na bahuagghanakaṃ, tvañ‍ca bahuṃ daisi, kaina kammaina idaṃ gaṇhāsī’’ti? idha, bhau, nagaravāsinau rañ‍ñā saddhiṃ paṭivirujjhitvā vipassīdasabalassa dānaṃ daintā idaṃ dvayaṃ dānamukhai apassantā pariyaisanti| sacai idaṃ dvayaṃ na labhissanti, nāgarānaṃ parājayau bhavissati| tasmā sahassaṃ datvā gaṇhāmīti| kiṃ panaitaṃ nāgarānamaiva vaṭṭati, na añ‍ñaisaṃ dātuṃ vaṭṭatīti? yassa kassaci dātuṃ avāritamaitanti| atthi pana tai kauci nāgarānaṃ dānai aikadivasaṃ sahassaṃ dātāti? natthi sammāti| imaisaṃ pana dvin‍naṃ sahassagghanakabhāvaṃ jānāsīti? āma jānāmīti| taina hi gaccha, nāgarānaṃ ācikkha – ‘‘aikau purisau imāni dvai mūlaina na daiti, sahatthainaiva dātukāmau, tumhai imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ kāraṇā nirussuk‍kā hauthā’’ti| tvaṃ pana mai imasmiṃ dānamukhai jaiṭṭhakabhāvassa kāyasakkhī hauhīti|

sau gāmavāsī paribbayatthaṃ gahitamāsakaina pañ‍cakaṭukaṃ gahaitvā cuṇṇaṃ katvā dadhitau kañ‍jiyaṃ vāhaitvā tattha madhupaṭalaṃ  pīḷaitvā pañ‍cakaṭukacuṇṇaina yaujaitvā aikasmiṃ paduminipattai pakkhipitvā taṃ saṃvidahitvā ādāya  dasabalassa avidūrai ṭhānai nisīdi| mahājanaina āhariyamānassa sak‍kārassa antarai attanau pattavāraṃ aulaukayamānau aukāsaṃ ñatvā satthu santikaṃ gantvā ‘‘bhagavā ayaṃ mayhaṃ duggatapaṇṇākārau, imaṃ mai anukampaṃ paṭic‍ca gaṇhathā’’ti| satthā tassa anukampaṃ paṭic‍ca catumahārājadattiyaina sailamayaina pattaina taṃ paṭiggahaitvā yathā aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassassa diyyamānaṃ na khīyati, aivaṃ adhiṭṭhāsi| saupi kulaputtau niṭṭhitabhattakic‍caṃ bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ ṭhitau āha – ‘‘diṭṭhau mai bhagavā aj‍ja bandhumatīnagaravāsikaihi tumhākaṃ sak‍kārau āhariyamānau, ahampi imassa kammassa nissandaina nibbattanibbattabhavai lābhaggayasaggappattau bhavaiyya’’nti| satthā ‘‘aivaṃ hautu kulaputtā’’ti vatvā tassa ca nagaravāsīnañ‍ca bhattānumaudanaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

saupi kulaputtau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai suppavāsāya rājadhītāya kucchismiṃ paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| paṭisandhiggahaṇatau paṭṭhāya sāyaṃ pātañ‍ca paṇṇākārasatāni pāpuṇanti, suppavāsā sampattiṃ gacchati| atha naṃ puñ‍ñavīmaṃsanatthaṃ hatthaina bījapacchiṃ  phusāpainti, aikaikabījatau salākasatampi salākasahassampi nigacchati| aikakarīsakhaittatau pañ‍ñāsampi saṭṭhipi sakaṭāni uppaj‍janti| kauṭṭhapūraṇakālaipi kauṭṭhadvāraṃ hatthaina phusāpainti, rājadhītāya puñ‍ñaina gaṇhantānaṃ gahitagahitaṭṭhānaṃ puna pūrati| paripuṇṇabhattakumbhitaupi ‘‘rājadhītāya puñ‍ña’’nti  vatvā yassa kassaci daintānaṃ yāva na uk‍kaḍḍhanti, na tāva bhattaṃ khīyati| dārakai kucchigataiyaiva satta vassāni atik‍kamiṃsu|

gabbhai pana paripak‍kai sattāhaṃ mahādukkhaṃ anubhausi| sā sāmikaṃ āmantaitvā ‘‘purai maraṇā jīvamānāva dānaṃ dassāmī’’ti satthu santikaṃ paisaisi – ‘‘gaccha imaṃ pavattiṃ satthu āraucaitvā satthāraṃ nimantaihi, yañ‍ca satthā vadaiti, taṃ sādhukaṃ upalakkhaitvā āgantvā mayhaṃ kathaihī’’ti| sau gantvā tassā sāsanaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| satthā ‘‘sukhinī hautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā, sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyatū’’ti āha| rājā taṃ sutvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā antaugāmābhimukhau pāyāsi| tassa purai āgamanāyaiva suppavāsāya kucchitau  dhamakaraṇā udakaṃ viya gabbhau nikkhami, parivāraitvā nisin‍najanau assumukhauva hasituṃ āraddhau| haṭṭhatuṭṭhau mahājanau rañ‍ñau puttasāsanaṃ āraucaituṃ agamāsi|

rājā taisaṃ iṅgitaṃ disvāva ‘‘dasabalaina kathitakathā nipphan‍nā mañ‍ñai’’ti cintaisi| sau āgantvā satthu sāsanaṃ rājadhītāya āraucaisi| rājadhītā ‘‘tayā nimantitaṃ jīvitabhattamaiva maṅgalabhattaṃ bhavissati, gaccha sattāhaṃ dasabalaṃ nimantaihī’’ti| rājā tathā akāsi| sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa mahādānaṃ pavattayiṃsu| dārakau sabbaisaṃ ñātīnaṃ santattacittaṃ nibbāpaintau jātauti sīvalidārakautvaivassa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau satta vassāni gabbhai vasitattā jātakālatau paṭṭhāya sabbakammakkhamau ahausi| dhammasaināpati sāriputtau sattamai divasai taina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ akāsi| satthāpi dhammapadai gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘yaumaṃ  palipathaṃ duggaṃ, saṃsāraṃ mauhamac‍cagā|

tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau jhāyī, anaijau akathaṃkathī|

anupādāya nibbutau, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇa’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 414)|

atha naṃ thairau aivamāha – ‘‘kiṃ pana tayā aivarūpaṃ dukkharāsiṃ anubhavitvā pabbajituṃ na  vaṭṭatī’’ti? labhamānau pabbajaiyyaṃ, bhantaiti| suppavāsā taṃ dārakaṃ thairaina saddhiṃ kathaintaṃ disvā ‘‘kiṃ nu khau mai puttau dhammasaināpatinā saddhiṃ kathaitī’’ti thairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchi – ‘‘mayhaṃ puttau tumhaihi saddhiṃ kiṃ kathaiti, bhadantai’’ti? attanā anubhūtaṃ gabbhavāsadukkhaṃ kathaitvā tumhaihi anuñ‍ñātau pabbajissāmīti vadatīti| sādhu, bhantai, pabbājaitha nanti| thairau taṃ vihāraṃ naitvā tacapañ‍cakakammaṭṭhānaṃ datvā pabbājaintau, ‘‘sīvali, na tuyhaṃ añ‍ñaina auvādaina kammaṃ atthi, tayā satta vassāni anubhūtadukkhamaiva pac‍cavaikkhāhī’’ti| bhantai, pabbājanamaiva tumhākaṃ bhārau, yaṃ pana mayā kātuṃ sak‍kā, tamahaṃ jānissāmīti| sau paṭhamakaisavaṭṭiyā auhāritakkhaṇaiyaiva sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāsi, dutiyāya auhāritakkhaṇai sakadāgāmiphalai, tatiyāya anāgāmiphalai| sabbaisaṃyaiva pana kaisānaṃ auraupanañ‍ca arahattasacchikiriyā ca apacchā apurimā ahausi| tassa pabbajitadivasatau paṭṭhāya bhikkhusaṅghassa cattārau pac‍cayā yadicchakaṃ uppaj‍janti| aivaṃ aittha vatthu samuṭṭhitaṃ|

aparabhāgai  satthā sāvatthiṃ agamāsi| thairau satthāraṃ abhivādaitvā, ‘‘bhantai, mayhaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vīmaṃsissāmi, pañ‍ca mai bhikkhusatāni daithā’’ti āha| gaṇha, sīvalīti| sau pañ‍casatai bhikkhū gahaitvā himavantābhimukhaṃ gacchantau aṭavimaggaṃ gacchati| tassa paṭhamaṃ diṭṭhā nigraudhai adhivatthā daivatā satta divasāni dānaṃ adāsi| iti sau –

‘‘nigraudhaṃ paṭhamaṃ passi, dutiyaṃ paṇḍavapabbataṃ|

tatiyaṃ aciravatiyaṃ, catutthaṃ varasāgaraṃ||

‘‘pañ‍camaṃ himavantaṃ sau, chaṭṭhaṃ chaddantupāgami|

sattamaṃ gandhamādanaṃ, aṭṭhamaṃ atha raivata’’nti||

sabbaṭṭhānaisu satta satta divasānaiva dānaṃ adaṃsu| gandhamādanapabbatai pana nāgadattadaivarājā nāma sattadivasaisu aikadivasaṃ khīrapiṇḍapātaṃ adāsi, aikadivasaṃ sappipiṇḍapātaṃ adāsi| bhikkhusaṅghau āha – ‘‘āvusau, imassa daivarañ‍ñau naiva dhainuyau duyhamānā pañ‍ñāyanti, na dadhinimmathanaṃ, kutau tai, daivarāja, imaṃ uppaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘bhantai, kassapadasabalassa kālai khīrasalākabhattadānassaitaṃ  phala’’nti daivarājā āha| aparabhāgai satthā khadiravaniyaraivatassa pac‍cuggamanaṃ aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā thairaṃ attanau sāsanai lābhaggayasaggappattānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

vak‍kalitthairavatthu

208. dasamai saddhādhimuttānanti saddhāya adhimuttānaṃ, balavasaddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vak‍kalitthairau aggauti dassaiti| añ‍ñaisaṃ hi saddhā vaḍḍhaitabbā hauti, thairassa pana hāpaitabbā jātā| tasmā sau saddhādhimuttānaṃ aggauti vuttau| vak‍kalīti panassa nāmaṃ|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayampi hi atītai padumuttarabuddhakālai vuttanayainaiva  vihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ saddhādhimuttānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā ‘‘mayāpi anāgatai aivarūpaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti vuttanayainaiva satthāraṃ nimantaitvā sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā dasabalaṃ vanditvā, ‘‘bhantai, ahampi iminā adhikārakammaina tumhaihi saddhādhimuttānaṃ aitadaggai ṭhapitabhikkhu viya anāgatai  aikassa buddhassa sāsanai saddhādhimuttānaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā tassa anantarāyaṃ disvā byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

saupi yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau amhākaṃ satthukālai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi, vak‍kalītissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vuddhippattau tayau vaidai uggaṇhitvā dasabalaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaparivutaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ carantaṃ disvā satthu sarīrasampattiṃ aulaukaintau sarīrasampattidassanaina atittau dasabalaina saddhiṃyaiva vicarati| vihāraṃ gacchantaina saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā sarīranipphattiṃ aulaukaintauva tiṭṭhati| dhammasabhāyaṃ nisīditvā dhammaṃ kathaintassa sammukhaṭṭhānai ṭhitau dhammaṃ suṇāti| sau saddhaṃ paṭilabhitvā ‘‘agāramajjhai vasantau nibaddhaṃ dasabalassa dassanaṃ na labhissāmī’’ti pabbaj‍jaṃ yācitvā satthu santikai pabbaji|

tatau paṭṭhāya ṭhapaitvā āhārakaraṇavailaṃ avasaisakālai yattha ṭhitaina sak‍kā dasabalaṃ passituṃ, tattha ṭhitau yaunisaumanasikāraṃ pahāya dasabalaṃ aulaukaintauva viharati| satthā tassa ñāṇaparipākaṃ āgamaintau dīghampi addhānaṃ tasmiṃ rūpadassanavasainaiva vicarantai kiñ‍ci avatvā ‘‘idānissa  ñāṇaṃ paripākagataṃ, sak‍kā aitaṃ baudhaitu’’nti ñatvā aivamāha – ‘‘kiṃ tai, vak‍kali, iminā pūtikāyaina diṭṭhaina, yau khau, vak‍kali, dhammaṃ passati, sau maṃ passati| yau maṃ passati, sau dhammaṃ passati| dhammañhi, vak‍kali, passantau maṃ passati, maṃ passantau dhammaṃ passatī’’ti|

satthari aivaṃ auvadantaipi thairau dasabalassa dassanaṃ pahāya naiva añ‍ñattha gantuṃ sak‍kauti| tatau satthā ‘‘nāyaṃ bhikkhu saṃvaigaṃ alabhitvā bujjhissatī’’ti upakaṭṭhāya vassūpanāyikāya rājagahaṃ gantvā vassūpanāyikadivasai ‘‘apaihi, vak‍kalī’’ti thairaṃ paṇāmaiti| buddhā ca nāma ādaiyyavacanā haunti, tasmā thairau satthāraṃ paṭippharitvā ṭhātuṃ asak‍kauntau taimāsaṃ dasabalassa sammukhai āgantuṃ avisahantau ‘‘kiṃ dāni sak‍kā kātuṃ, tathāgatainamhi paṇāmitau, sammukhībhāvaṃ na labhāmi, kiṃ mayhaṃ jīvitainā’’ti gijjhakūṭapabbatai papātaṭṭhānaṃ abhiruhi| satthā tassa kilamanabhāvaṃ ñatvā ‘‘ayaṃ bhikkhu mama santikā assāsaṃ alabhantau  maggaphalānaṃ upanissayaṃ nāsaiyyā’’ti attānaṃ dassaituṃ aubhāsaṃ vissaj‍jaisi| athassa satthu diṭṭhakālatau paṭṭhāya aiva mahantaṃ saukasal‍laṃ pahīnaṃ| satthā sukkhataḷākai aughaṃ āharantau viya vak‍kalitthairassa balavapītisaumanassaṃ uppādaituṃ dhammapadai imaṃ gāthamāha –

‘‘pāmauj‍jabahulau bhikkhu, pasan‍nau buddhasāsanai|

adhigacchai padaṃ santaṃ, saṅkhārūpasamaṃ sukha’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 381)|

vak‍kalitthairassa ca ‘‘aihi, vak‍kalī’’ti hatthaṃ pasāraisi| thairau ‘‘dasabalau mai diṭṭhau, aihīti avhāyanampi laddha’’nti balavapītiṃ uppādaitvā ‘‘kutau gacchāmī’’ti attanau gamanabhāvaṃ ajānitvāva dasabalassa sammukhai ākāsai pakkhanditvā paṭhamapādaina pabbatai ṭhitauyaiva satthārā vuttagāthaṃ  āvaj‍jaintau ākāsaiyaiva pītiṃ vikkhambhaitvā saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ patvā tathāgataṃ vandamānauva autari| aparabhāgai satthā ariyagaṇamajjhai nisin‍nau thairaṃ saddhādhimuttānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

dutiyavaggavaṇṇanā|

14. aitadaggavaggau

(14) 3. tatiyaaitadaggavaggau

209. tatiyavaggassa  paṭhamai sikkhākāmānanti tissau sikkhā kāmayamānānaṃ sampiyāyitvā sikkhantānanti atthau| rāhulauti attanau puttaṃ rāhulatthairaṃ dassaiti| thairau kira pabbajitadivasatau paṭṭhāya pātauva uṭṭhahantau hatthapūraṃ vālikaṃ ukkhipitvā ‘‘ahau vatāhaṃ aj‍ja dasabalassa caiva ācariyupajjhāyānañ‍ca santikā aittakaṃ auvādañ‍caiva anusāsaniñ‍ca labhaiyya’’nti patthaiti| tasmā sikkhākāmānaṃ aggau nāma jātauti|

210. dutiyai saddhāpabbajitānanti saddhāya pabbajitānaṃ| raṭṭhapālauti raṭṭhaṃ pālaituṃ samatthau, bhin‍naṃ vā raṭṭhaṃ sandhāraituṃ samatthai kulai jātautipi  raṭṭhapālauti saṅkhaṃ gatau| sau hi satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau cuddasabhattacchaidai katvā mātāpitarau pabbaj‍jaṃ anujānāpaitvā pabbajitau| tasmā saddhāpabbajitānaṃ aggau nāma jātau|

rāhula-raṭṭhapālatthairavatthu

imaisaṃ pana ubhin‍nampi thairānaṃ pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – aitai kira dvaipi atītai padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai gahapatimahāsālakulai nibbattiṃsu| taisaṃ daharakālai nāmaṃ vā gauttaṃ vā na kathiyati| vayappattā pana gharāvāsai patiṭṭhāya attanau attanau pitu ac‍cayaina ubhaupi attanau attanau  ratanakauṭṭhāgārakammikai pak‍kausāpaitvā aparimāṇaṃ dhanaṃ disvā – ‘‘imaṃ aittakaṃ dhanarāsiṃ ayyakapayyakādayau attanā saddhiṃ gahaitvā gantuṃ nāsakkhiṃsu, amhaihi dāni yaina kainaci upāyaina imaṃ dhanaṃ gahaitvā gantuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti tai ubhaupi janā catūsu ṭhānaisu kapaṇaddhikādīnaṃ mahādānaṃ dātuṃ āraddhā| aikau attanau dānaggai āgatāgatajanaṃ pucchitvā yāgukhaj‍jakādīsu yassa yaṃ paṭibhāti, tassa taṃ adāsi, tassa tainaiva kāraṇaina āgatapākauti  nāmaṃ jātaṃ| itarau apucchitvāva gahitagahitabhājanaṃ pūraitvā pūraitvā daiti, tassapi tainaiva kāraṇaina anaggapākauti nāmaṃ jātaṃ, appamāṇapākauti atthau|

tai ubhaupi aikadivasaṃ pātauva mukhadhauvanatthaṃ bahigāmaṃ agamaṃsu| tasmiṃ samayai himavantatau dvai mahiddhikā tāpasā bhikkhācāratthāya ākāsaina āgantvā taisaṃ sahāyakānaṃ avidūrai autaritvā ‘‘mā nau aitai passiṃsū’’ti aikapassai aṭṭhaṃsu| tai ubhaupi janā taisaṃ lābubhājanādiparikkhāraṃ saṃvidhāya antaugāmaṃ sandhāya bhikkhāya gatānaṃ santikaṃ āgamma vandiṃsu| atha nai tāpasā ‘‘kāya vailāya āgatattha mahāpuñ‍ñā’’ti āhaṃsu| tai ‘‘adhunāva, bhantai’’ti vatvā  taisaṃ hatthatau lābubhājanaṃ gahaitvā attanau attanau gaihaṃ naitvā bhattakic‍capariyausānai nibaddhaṃ bhikkhāgahaṇatthaṃ paṭiñ‍ñaṃ gaṇhiṃsu|

taisu aikau tāpasau sapariḷāhakāyadhātukau hauti| sau attanau ānubhāvaina mahāsamuddaudakaṃ dvaidhā katvā pathavindharanāgarājassa bhavanaṃ gantvā divāvihāraṃ nisīdati| sau utusappāyaṃ gahaitvā pac‍cāgantvā attanau upaṭṭhākassa gaihai bhattānumaudanaṃ karauntau ‘‘pathavindharanāgabhavanaṃ viya  hautū’’ti vadati| atha naṃ aikadivasaṃ upaṭṭhākau pucchi – ‘‘bhantai, tumhai anumaudanaṃ karauntā ‘pathavindharanāgabhavanaṃ viya hautū’ti vadatha, mayamassa atthaṃ na jānāma, kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti idaṃ, bhantai’’ti? āma, kuṭumbiya ahaṃ ‘‘tumhākaṃ sampatti pathavindharanāgarājasampattisadisā hautū’’ti vadāmīti| kuṭumbikau tatau paṭṭhāya pathavindharanāgarājabhavanai cittaṃ ṭhapaisi|

itarau tāpasau tāvatiṃsabhavanaṃ gantvā suñ‍ñai sairisakavimānai divāvihāraṃ karauti| sau āgacchantau gacchantau ca sak‍kassa daivarājassa sampattiṃ disvā attanau upaṭṭhākassa anumaudanaṃ karauntau ‘‘sak‍kavimānaṃ viya hautū’’ti vadati| atha naṃ saupi kuṭumbiyau itarau sahāyakau taṃ tāpasaṃ viya pucchi| sau tassa vacanaṃ sutvā sak‍kabhavanai cittaṃ ṭhapaisi| tai ubhaupi patthitaṭṭhānaisuyaiva nibbattā|

pathavindharabhavanai nibbattau pathavindharanāgarājā nāma jātau| sau nibbattakkhaṇai attanau attabhāvaṃ disvā ‘‘amanāpassa vata mai ṭhānassa kulupakatāpasau vaṇṇaṃ kathaisi, uraina parisak‍kitvā  vicaraṇaṭṭhānamaitaṃ, nūna sau añ‍ñaṃ ṭhānaṃ na jānātī’’ti vippaṭisārī ahausi| athassa taṃkhaṇaiyaiva alaṅkatapaṭiyattāni nāganāṭakāni sabbadisāsu tūriyāni paggaṇhiṃsu| sau tasmiṃyaiva khaṇai taṃ attabhāvaṃ vijahitvā māṇavakavaṇṇī ahausi| anvaddhamāsañ‍ca cattārau mahārājānau sak‍kassa upaṭṭhānaṃ gacchanti| tasmā saupi virūpakkhaina nāgarañ‍ñā saddhiṃ sak‍kassa upaṭṭhānaṃ gatau| sak‍kau taṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ disvā sañ‍jāni| atha naṃ samīpai āgantvā ṭhitakālai ‘‘kahaṃ nibbattausi  sammā’’ti pucchi| mā kathaisi, mahārāja, uraina parisak‍kanaṭṭhānai nibbattaumhi, tumhai pana kalyāṇamittaṃ labhitthāti| samma, tvaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhānai nibbattaumhī’’ti mā vitak‍kayi, padumuttaradasabalau laukai nibbattau, tassa adhikārakammaṃ katvā imaṃyaiva ṭhānaṃ patthaihi, ubhau sukhaṃ vasissāmāti| sau ‘‘aivaṃ, daiva, karissāmī’’ti gantvā padumuttaradasabalaṃ nimantaitvā attanau nāgabhavanai nāgaparisāya saddhiṃ sabbarattiṃ sak‍kārasammānaṃ saj‍jaisi|

satthā punadivasai uṭṭhitai aruṇai attanau upaṭṭhākaṃ sumanatthairaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sumana, aj‍ja tathāgatau dūraṃ bhikkhācāraṃ gamissati, mā puthuj‍janabhikkhū āgacchantu, taipiṭakā paṭisambhidāppattā chaḷabhiñ‍ñāva āgacchantū’’ti| thairau satthu vacanaṃ sutvā sabbaisaṃ āraucaisi| satthārā saddhiṃ satasahassā bhikkhū ākāsaṃ pakkhandiṃsu| pathavindharau nāgaparisāya saddhiṃ dasabalassa pac‍cuggamanaṃ āgatau satthāraṃ parivāraitvā samuddamatthakai maṇivaṇṇā ūmiyau maddamānaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ aulaukaitvā āditau satthāraṃ, pariyausānai saṅghanavakaṃ tathāgatassa puttaṃ uparaivatasāmaṇairaṃ nāma aulaukaintau ‘‘anacchariyau saisasāvakānaṃ aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau, imassa pana taruṇabāladārakassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau ativiya acchariyau’’ti pītipāmauj‍jaṃ uppādaisi|

athassa bhavanai dasabalai nisin‍nai saisabhikkhūsu kauṭitau paṭṭhāya nisīdantaisu satthu sammukhaṭṭhānaiyaiva uparaivatasāmaṇairassa āsanaṃ pāpuṇi| nāgarājā yāguṃ daintaupi khaj‍jakaṃ daintaupi sakiṃ dasabalaṃ aulaukaiti, sakiṃ uparaivatasāmaṇairaṃ| tassa kira sarīrai satthu sarīrai viya dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni pañ‍ñāyanti| tatau nāgarājā ‘‘ayaṃ sāmaṇairau buddhānaṃ sadisau pañ‍ñāyati, kiṃ nu khau  hautī’’ti avidūrai nisin‍naṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ pucchi – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, sāmaṇairau dasabalassa kiṃ hautī’’ti? puttau, mahārājāti| sau cintaisi – ‘‘mahā  vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, aivarūpassa saubhaggappattassa tathāgatassa puttabhāvaṃ labhi| sarīrampissa aikadaisaina buddhānaṃ sarīrasadisaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, mayāpi anāgatai aivarūpaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ datvā, ‘‘bhantai, ahaṃ imassa adhikārakammassānubhāvaina ayaṃ uparaivatau viya anāgatai aikassa buddhassa puttau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| satthā  anantarāyaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai gautamabuddhassa puttau bhavissasī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

pathavindharaupi puna addhamāsai sampattai virūpakkhaina saddhiṃ sak‍kassa upaṭṭhānaṃ gatau| atha naṃ samīpai ṭhitaṃ sak‍kau pucchi – ‘‘patthitau tai, samma, ayaṃ daivalaukau’’ti? na patthitau mahārājāti| kiṃ dausaṃ addasāti? dausau natthi, mahārāja, ahaṃ pana dasabalassa puttaṃ uparaivatasāmaṇairaṃ passiṃ| tassa mai diṭṭhakālatau paṭṭhāya añ‍ñattha cittaṃ na nami, svāhaṃ ‘‘anāgatai aikassa buddhassa aivarūpau puttau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsiṃ| tvampi, mahārāja, aikaṃ patthanaṃ karauhi, tai mayaṃ nibbattaṭṭhānai na vinā bhavissāmāti| sak‍kau tassa vacanaṃ sampaṭicchitvā aikaṃ mahānubhāvaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā ‘‘katarakulā nu khau nikkhamitvā ayaṃ kulaputtau pabbajitau’’ti āvaj‍jaintau ‘‘ayaṃ bhin‍naṃ raṭṭhaṃ sandhāraituṃ samatthassa kulassa puttau hutvā cuddasa bhattacchaidai katvā mātāpitarau pabbaj‍jaṃ anujānāpaitvā pabbajitau’’ti añ‍ñāsi| ñatvā ca pana ajānantau viya dasabalaṃ pucchitvā sattāhaṃ mahāsak‍kāraṃ katvā, ‘‘bhantai, ahaṃ imassa kalyāṇakammassa nissandaina tumhākaṃ sāsanai ayaṃ kulaputtau viya anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai saddhāpabbajitānaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ  akāsi| satthā anantarāyaṃ disvā ‘‘tvaṃ, mahārāja, anāgatai gautamassa buddhassa sāsanai saddhāpabbajitānaṃ aggau bhavissasī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi| sak‍kaupi attanau daivapuramaiva gatau|

tai ubhaupi nibbattaṭṭhānatau cavitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantā anaikasahassakappai atik‍kamiṃsu| itau pana dvānavutikappamatthakai phussau nāma buddhau laukai udapādi| tassa pitā mahindau nāma rājā ahausi, vaimātikā tayau kaniṭṭhabhātarau| rājā divasai divasai ‘‘mayhaṃyaiva buddhau mayhaṃ dhammau mayhaṃ saṅghau’’ti mamāyantau sayamaiva dasabalaṃ nibaddhaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhaujaiti|

athassa aikadivasaṃ pac‍cantau kupitau| sau puttai āmantaisi – ‘‘tātā, pac‍cantau kupitau, tumhaihi vā mayā vā gantabbaṃ| yadi ahaṃ gacchāmi, tumhaihi iminā niyāmaina dasabalau paricaritabbau’’ti| tai tayaupi aikappahārainaiva āhaṃsu – ‘‘tāta, tumhākaṃ gamanakic‍caṃ natthi, mayaṃ  caurai vidhamissāmā’’ti pitaraṃ vanditvā pac‍cantaṃ gantvā caurai vidhamitvā vijitasaṅgāmā hutvā nivattiṃsu| tai antarāmaggai pādamūlikaihi saddhiṃ mantayiṃsu – ‘‘tātā, amhākaṃ gatakkhaṇaiyaiva pitā varaṃ dassati, kataraṃ varaṃ gaṇhāmā’’ti? ayyā, tumhākaṃ pitu ac‍cayaina dul‍labhaṃ nāma natthi, tumhākaṃ pana jaiṭṭhabhātikaṃ phussabuddhaṃ paṭijagganavaraṃ gaṇhathā’’ti āhaṃsu| tai ‘‘kalyāṇaṃ tumhaihi vutta’’nti sabbaipi aikacittā hutvā gantvā pitaraṃ addasaṃsu| tadā pitā taisaṃ pasīditvā varaṃ adāsi| tai ‘‘taimāsaṃ tathāgataṃ paṭijaggissāmā’’ti varaṃ yāciṃsu| rājā ‘‘ayaṃ dātuṃ na sak‍kā, añ‍ñaṃ varaṃ gaṇhathā’’ti āha| tāta, amhākaṃ añ‍ñaina varaina kic‍caṃ natthi, sacai tumhai dātukāmā, aitaṃyaiva  nau varaṃ daithāti| rājā taisu punappunaṃ kathaintaisu attanā paṭiñ‍ñātattā ‘‘na sak‍kā na dātu’’nti cintaitvā āha – ‘‘tātā, ahaṃ tumhākaṃ varaṃ daimi, apica khau pana buddhā nāma durāsadā haunti sīhā viya aikacarā, dasabalaṃ paṭijaggantā appamattā bhavaiyyāthā’’ti|

tai cintayiṃsu – ‘‘amhaihi tathāgataṃ paṭijaggantaihi anucchavikaṃ katvā paṭijaggituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti sabbaipi aikacittā hutvā dasasīlāni samādāya nirāmagandhā hutvā satthu dānaggaparivahanakai tayau purisai ṭhapayiṃsu| taisu aikau dhanadhañ‍ñuppādakau ahausi, aikau māpakau, aikau dānasaṃvidhāyakau| taisu dhanadhañ‍ñuppādakau pac‍cuppan‍nai bimbisārau mahārājā jātau, māpakau visākhau upāsakau, dānasaṃvidhāyakau raṭṭhapālatthairauti| sau tattha yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivapurai nibbattau| ayaṃ pana rāhulatthairau nāma kassapadasabalassa kālai kikissa kāsirañ‍ñau jaiṭṭhaputtau hutvā nibbatti, pathavindharakumārautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| tassa satta bhaginiyau ahaisuṃ| tā dasabalassa satta parivaiṇāni kārayiṃsu| pathavindharau auparaj‍jaṃ labhi| sau tā bhaginiyau āha – ‘‘tumhaihi kāritaparivaiṇaisu mayhampi aikaṃ daithā’’ti| bhātika, tumhai uparājaṭṭhānai ṭhitā, tumhaihi nāma amhākaṃ dātabbaṃ, tumhai añ‍ñaṃ parivaiṇaṃ karauthāti| sau tāsaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā pañ‍ca vihārasatāni kāraisi| pañ‍ca parivaiṇasatānītipi vadanti| sau tattha yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivapurai nibbatti| imasmiṃ pana buddhuppādai pathavindharakumārau amhākaṃ baudhisattassa  aggamahaisiyā kucchismiṃ paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi, tassa sahāyakau kururaṭṭhai thul‍lakauṭṭhitanigamai raṭṭhapālasaiṭṭhigaihai nibbatti|

atha  amhākaṃ dasabalau abhisambaudhiṃ patvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kau anupubbaina kapilavatthuṃ āgantvā  rāhulakumāraṃ pabbājaisi| tassa pabbaj‍jāvidhānaṃ pāḷiyaṃ (mahāva॰ 105) āgatamaiva| aivaṃ pabbajitassa panassa satthā abhiṇhaauvādavasaina rāhulauvādasuttaṃ abhāsi| rāhulaupi pātauva vuṭṭhāya hatthaina vālukaṃ ukkhipitvā ‘‘dasabalassa caiva ācariyupajjhāyānañ‍ca santikā aj‍ja aittakaṃ auvādaṃ labhaiyya’’nti vadati| bhikkhusaṅghamajjhai kathā udapādi ‘‘auvādakkhamau vata rāhulasāmaṇairau pitu anucchavikau puttau’’ti| satthā bhikkhūnaṃ cittācāraṃ ñatvā ‘‘mayi gatai aikā dhammadaisanā ca vaḍḍhissati, rāhulassa ca guṇau pākaṭau bhavissatī’’ti gantvā dhammasabhāyaṃ buddhāsānai nisin‍nau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā’’ti| rāhulasāmaṇairassa auvādakkhamabhāvaṃ kathaima bhagavāti| satthā imasmiṃ ṭhānai ṭhatvā rāhulassa guṇadīpanatthaṃ migajātakaṃ āharitvā kathaisi –

‘‘migaṃ tipal‍latthamanaikamāyaṃ,

aṭṭhakkhuraṃ aḍḍharattā papāyiṃ|

aikaina sautaina chamā’ssasantau,

chahi kalāhitibhauti bhāginaiyyau’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.1.16)|

athassa sattavassikasāmaṇairakālai ‘‘mā haiva khau rāhulau daharabhāvaina kīḷanatthāyapi sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’’ti ambalaṭṭhiyarāhulauvādaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 2.107 ādayau) daisaisi| aṭṭhārasavassikasāmaṇairakālai tathāgatassa pacchatau piṇḍāya pavisantassa satthu caiva attanau ca rūpasampattiṃ disvā gaihasitaṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaintassa ‘‘yaṃkiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpa’’ntiādinā  nayaina mahārāhulauvādasuttantaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 2.113) kathaisi| saṃyuttakai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.121) pana rāhulauvādaupi aṅguttarai (a॰ ni॰ 4.177) rāhulauvādaupi thairassa vipassanācārauyaiva| athassa satthā ñāṇaparipākaṃ ñatvā avassikabhikkhukālai andhavanai nisin‍nau cūḷarāhulauvādaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.416 ādayau) kathaisi| daisanāpariyausānai rāhulatthairau kauṭisatasahassadaivatāhi saddhiṃ arahattaṃ pāpuṇi, sautāpan‍nasakadāgāmianāgāmidaivatānaṃ  gaṇanā natthi| atha satthā aparabhāgai ariyasaṅghamajjhai nisin‍nau thairaṃ imasmiṃ sāsanai sikkhākāmānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi|

satthari  pana kururaṭṭhai cārikāya nikkhamitvā thul‍lakauṭṭhitaṃ anuppattai raṭṭhapālau kulaputtau satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau mātāpitarau anujānāpaitvā dasabalaṃ upasaṅkamitvā satthu āṇattiyā añ‍ñatarassa thairassa santikai pabbaji| tassa pabbajitadivasatau paṭṭhāya saiṭṭhigahapati bhikkhū attanau nivaisanadvāraina gacchantai disvā ‘‘kiṃ tumhākaṃ imasmiṃ gaihai kammaṃ, aikauva puttakau ahausi, taṃ gaṇhitvā gatattha, idāni kiṃ karissathā’’ti ak‍kausati paribhāsati| satthā addhamāsaṃ thul‍lakauṭṭhitai vasitvā puna sāvatthimaiva agamāsi| tatthāyasmā raṭṭhapālau yaunisau manasikarauntau kammaṃ katvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| sau satthāraṃ anujānāpaitvā mātāpitarau dassanatthaṃ thul‍lakauṭṭhitaṃ gantvā tattha sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya carantau pitu nivaisanai ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ labhitvā taṃ amataṃ viya paribhuñ‍jantau pitarā nimantitau adhivāsaitvā dutiyadivasai  pitu nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā alaṅkatapaṭiyattai itthijanai asubhasañ‍ñaṃ uppādaitvā ṭhitakauva dhammaṃ daisaitvā jiyā muttau viya nārācau ākāsaṃ uppatitvā kaurabyarañ‍ñau migacīraṃ gantvā maṅgalasilāpaṭṭai nisin‍nau dassanatthāya āgatassa rañ‍ñau catupārijuñ‍ñapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ dhammaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 2.304) daisaitvā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau puna satthu santikaṃyaiva āgatau| aivamaitaṃ vatthu samuṭṭhitaṃ| atha satthā aparabhāgai ariyagaṇamajjhai nisin‍nau thairaṃ imasmiṃ sāsanai saddhāpabbajitānaṃ kulaputtānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

kuṇḍadhānatthairavatthu

211. tatiyai paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ gaṇhantānanti sabbapaṭhamaṃ salākagāhakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kuṇḍadhānatthairau aggauti dassaiti| sau kira thairau mahāsubhaddāya nimantitadivasai tathāgatai ugganagaraṃ gacchantai ‘‘aj‍ja satthā dūraṃ bhikkhācāraṃ gamissati, puthuj‍janā salākaṃ mā gaṇhantu, pañ‍casatā  khīṇāsavāva gaṇhantū’’ti vuttai paṭhamamaiva sīhanādaṃ naditvā salākaṃ gaṇhi| cūḷasubhaddāya nimantitadivasai tathāgatai sākaitaṃ gacchantaipi pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ antarai paṭhamamaiva salākaṃ gaṇhi, sunāparantajanapadaṃ gacchantaipi| imaihi kāraṇaihi thairau paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ gaṇhantānaṃ aggau nāma jātau| kuṇḍadhānauti panassa nāmaṃ|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayaṃ kira padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagaihai  nibbattau vuttanayainaiva vihāraṃ gantvā dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ gaṇhantānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā buddhānaṃ adhikārakammaṃ  katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaitvā satthārā anantarāyaṃ disvā byākatau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivaisu ca manussaisu ca saṃsarantau kassapabuddhakālai bhūmaṭṭhakadaivatā hutvā nibbatti| dīghāyukabuddhānañ‍ca nāma na anvaddhamāsikau upausathau hauti| vipassīdasabalassa hi chabbassantarai chabbassantarai upausathau ahausi, kassapadasabalau pana chaṭṭhai chaṭṭhai māsai pātimaukkhaṃ ausāraisi| tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ausāraṇakālai disāvāsikā dvai sahāyakā bhikkhū ‘‘upausathaṃ karissāmā’’ti gacchanti| ayaṃ bhummadaivatā cintaisi – ‘‘imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ maitti ativiya daḷhā, kiṃ nu khau bhaidakai sati bhij‍jaiyya, na bhij‍jaiyyā’’ti? taisaṃ aukāsaṃ aulaukayamānā taisaṃ avidūrainaiva gacchati|

athaikau thairau aikassa hatthai pattacīvaraṃ datvā sarīravaḷañ‍janatthaṃ udakaphāsukaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā dhautahatthapādau hutvā gumbasabhāgatau nikkhamati| bhummadaivatā tassa thairassa pacchatau pacchatau uttamarūpā itthī hutvā kaisai vidhunitvā saṃvidhāya bandhantī viya piṭṭhitau paṃsuṃ puñchamānā viya sāṭakaṃ saṃvidhāya nivāsayamānā viya ca hutvā thairassa padānupadikā hutvā gumbatau nikkhantā| aikamantai ṭhitau sahāyakatthairau imaṃ kāraṇaṃ disvā daumanassajātau ‘‘naṭṭhau dāni mai iminā bhikkhunā saddhiṃ dīgharattānugatau sinaihau| sacāhaṃ aivaṃvidhabhāvaṃ jānaiyyaṃ, aittakaṃ addhānaṃ iminā saddhiṃ vissāsaṃ na karaiyya’’nti cintaitvā āgacchantassaivassa ‘‘handāvusau, tuyhaṃ pattacīvaraṃ, tādisaina pāpaina sahāyaina saddhiṃ aikamaggaṃ na gacchāmī’’ti āha| taṃ kathaṃ sutvā tassa  laj‍jibhikkhunau hadayaṃ tikhiṇasattiṃ gahaitvā viddhaṃ viya ahausi| tatau naṃ āha – ‘‘āvusau, kiṃ nāmaitaṃ vadasi, ahaṃ aittakaṃ kālaṃ duk‍kaṭamattampi āpattiṃ na jānāmi| tvaṃ pana maṃ aj‍ja ‘pāpau’ti vadasi, kiṃ tai diṭṭha’’nti? kiṃ añ‍ñaina diṭṭhaina, kiṃ tvaṃ aivaṃvidhaina alaṅkatapaṭiyattaina mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikaṭṭhānai hutvā nikkhantauti? natthaitaṃ, āvusau, mayhaṃ, nāhaṃ aivarūpaṃ mātugāmaṃ passāmīti| tassa yāvatatiyaṃ kathaintassāpi itarau thairau kathaṃ asaddahitvā attanā diṭṭhakāraṇaṃyaiva atthaṃ gahaitvā taina saddhiṃ aikamaggaina agantvā añ‍ñaina maggaina satthu santikaṃ gatau| itaraupi añ‍ñaina maggaina satthu santikaṃyaiva gatau|

tatau bhikkhusaṅghassa upausathāgāraṃ pavisanavailāya sau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ upausathaggai sañ‍jānitvā  ‘‘imasmiṃ upausathaggai aivarūpau nāma pāpabhikkhu atthi, nāhaṃ taina saddhiṃ upausathaṃ karissāmī’’ti nikkhamitvā bahi aṭṭhāsi| bhummadaivatā ‘‘bhāriyaṃ mayā kammaṃ kata’’nti mahal‍lakaupāsakavaṇṇaina tassa santikaṃ gantvā ‘‘kasmā, bhantai, ayyau imasmiṃ ṭhānai ṭhitau’’ti āha| upāsaka, imaṃ upausathaggaṃ aikau pāpabhikkhu paviṭṭhau, ahaṃ taina saddhiṃ upausathaṃ na karaumīti vatvā nikkhamitvā bahi ṭhitaumhīti| bhantai, mā aivaṃ gaṇhatha, parisuddhasīlau aisa bhikkhu| tumhaihi diṭṭhamātugāmau nāma ahaṃ, mayā tumhākaṃ vīmaṃsanatthāya ‘‘daḷhā nu khau imaisaṃ thairānaṃ maitti, nau daḷhā’’ti laj‍jialaj‍jibhāvaṃ aulaukaintaina taṃ kammaṃ katanti| kau pana tvaṃ sappurisāti? ahaṃ aikā bhummadaivatā, bhantaiti| daivaputtau kathaintauva dibbānubhāvaina  ṭhatvā thairassa pādaisu patitvā ‘‘mayhaṃ, bhantai, khamatha, aitaṃ dausaṃ thairau na jānāti, upausathaṃ karauthā’’ti thairaṃ yācitvā upausathaggaṃ pavaisaisi| sau thairau upausathaṃ tāva aikaṭṭhānai akāsi, mittasanthavavasaina na puna taina saddhiṃ aikaṭṭhānai ahausīti| imassa thairassa kammaṃ na kathiyati, cuditakatthairau pana aparāparaṃ vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau arahattaṃ pāpuṇi|

bhummadaivatā tassa kammassa nissandaina aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ apāyatau na muc‍cittha| sacai pana kālaina kālaṃ manussattaṃ āgacchati, añ‍ñaina yaina kainaci katau dausau tassaiva upari patati| sau amhākaṃ bhagavatau  kālai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbatti, dhānamāṇavautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau tayau vaidai uggaṇhitvā mahal‍lakakālai satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbaji, tassa upasampan‍nadivasatau paṭṭhāya aikā alaṅkatapaṭiyattā itthī tasmiṃ gāmaṃ pavisantai saddhiṃyaiva gāmaṃ pavisati, nikkhamantai nikkhamati| vihāraṃ pavisantaipi pavisati, tiṭṭhantaipi tiṭṭhatīti aivaṃ nic‍cānubandhā pañ‍ñāyati| thairau taṃ na passati, tassa pana purimassa kammassa nissandaina sā añ‍ñaisaṃ upaṭṭhāti|

gāmai yāgubhikkhaṃ dadamānā itthiyau, ‘‘bhantai, ayaṃ aikau yāguuḷuṅkau tumhākaṃ, aikau imissā amhākaṃ sahāyikāyā’’ti parihāsaṃ karaunti| thairassa mahatī vihaisā hauti| vihāraṃ gatampi naṃ sāmaṇairā caiva daharabhikkhū ca parivāraitvā ‘‘dhānau kauṇḍau jātau’’ti parihāsaṃ karaunti| athassa tainaiva kāraṇaina kuṇḍadhānatthairauti nāmaṃ jātaṃ| sau uṭṭhāya samuṭṭhāya taihi kayiramānaṃ kaiḷiṃ sahituṃ asak‍kauntau ummādaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘tumhai kauṇḍā, tumhākaṃ upajjhāyā kauṇḍā, ācariyā kauṇḍā’’ti vadati| atha naṃ satthu āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘kuṇḍadhānau daharasāmaṇairaihi saddhiṃ aivaṃ pharusavācaṃ vadatī’’ti| satthā taṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhikkhū’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti vuttai ‘‘kasmā aivaṃ vadaisī’’ti āha| bhantai, nibaddhaṃ vihaisaṃ asahantau aivaṃ kathaimīti| ‘‘tvaṃ pubbai katakammaṃ yāvaj‍jadivasā jīrāpaituṃ na sak‍kausi, puna aivarūpaṃ pharusaṃ mā vada bhikkhū’’ti vatvā āha –

‘‘māvauca pharusaṃ kañ‍ci, vuttā paṭivadaiyyu taṃ|

dukkhā hi sārambhakathā, paṭidaṇḍā phusaiyyu taṃ||

‘‘sacai nairaisi attānaṃ, kaṃsau upahatau yathā|

aisa pattausi nibbānaṃ, sārambhau tai na vij‍jatī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 133-134)|

imañ‍ca pana tassa thairassa mātugāmaina saddhiṃ vicaraṇabhāvaṃ kausalarañ‍ñaupi kathayiṃsu| rājā ‘‘gacchatha, bhaṇai, vīmaṃsathā’’ti paisaitvā sayampi mandainaiva parivāraina saddhiṃ thairassa vasanaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā aikamantai aulaukaintau aṭṭhāsi| tasmiṃ khaṇai thairau sūcikammaṃ karauntau nisin‍nau hauti, sāpissa itthī avidūrai ṭhānai ṭhitā viya pañ‍ñāyati|

rājā  taṃ disvā ‘‘atthidaṃ kāraṇa’’nti tassā ṭhitaṭṭhānaṃ agamāsi| sā tasmiṃ āgacchantai thairassa vasanapaṇṇasālaṃ paviṭṭhā viya ahausi| rājāpi  tāya saddhiṃyaiva paṇṇasālaṃ pavisitvā sabbattha aulaukaintau adisvā ‘‘nāyaṃ mātugāmau, thairassa aikau kammavipākau’’ti sañ‍ñaṃ katvā paṭhamaṃ thairassa samīpaina gacchantaupi thairaṃ avanditvā tassa kāraṇassa abhūtabhāvaṃ ñatvā āgamma thairaṃ vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, piṇḍakaina na kilamathā’’ti pucchi| thairau ‘‘vaṭṭati mahārājā’’ti āha| ‘‘jānāmi, bhantai, ayyassa kathaṃ, aivarūpaina ca parik‍kilaisaina saddhiṃ carantānaṃ tumhākaṃ kai nāma pasīdissanti, itau paṭṭhāya vau katthaci gamanakic‍caṃ natthi, ahaṃ catūhi pac‍cayaihi upaṭṭhahissāmi, tumhai yaunisaumanasikārai mā pamaj‍jitthā’’ti nibaddhaṃ bhikkhaṃ paṭṭhapaisi| thairau rājānaṃ upatthambhakaṃ labhitvā  bhaujanasappāyaina aikaggacittau hutvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| tatau paṭṭhāya sā itthī antaradhāyi|

mahāsubhaddā ugganagarai micchādiṭṭhikulai vasamānā ‘‘satthā maṃ anukampatū’’ti upausathaṃ adhiṭṭhāya nirāmagandhā hutvā uparipāsādatalai ṭhitā ‘‘imāni pupphāni antarai aṭṭhatvā dasabalassa matthakai vitānaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhantu, dasabalau imāya sañ‍ñāya svai pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ mayhaṃ bhikkhaṃ gaṇhatū’’ti sac‍cakiriyaṃ katvā aṭṭha sumanapupphamuṭṭhiyau vissaj‍jaisi| pupphāni gantvā dhammadaisanāvailāya satthu matthakai vitānaṃ hutvā aṭṭhaṃsu| satthā taṃ sumanapupphavitānaṃ disvā cittainaiva subhaddāya bhikkhaṃ adhivāsaitvā punadivasai aruṇai uṭṭhitai ānandatthairaṃ āha – ‘‘ānanda, mayaṃ aj‍ja dūraṃ bhikkhācāraṃ gamissāma, puthuj‍janānaṃ adatvā ariyānaṃyaiva salākaṃ daihī’’ti| thairau bhikkhūnaṃ  āraucaisi – ‘‘āvusau, satthā aj‍ja dūraṃ bhikkhācāraṃ gamissati, puthuj‍janā mā gaṇhantu, ariyāva salākaṃ gaṇhantū’’ti| kuṇḍadhānatthairau ‘‘āharāvusau, salāka’’nti paṭhamaṃyaiva hatthaṃ pasāraisi| ānandā ‘‘satthā tādisānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ salākaṃ na dāpaiti, ariyānaṃyaiva dāpaitī’’ti vitak‍kaṃ uppādaitvā gantvā satthu āraucaisi| satthā ‘‘āharāpaintassa salākaṃ daihī’’ti āha| thairau cintaisi – ‘‘sacai kuṇḍadhānassa salākā dātuṃ na  yuttā assa, atha satthā paṭibāhaiyya, bhavissati aikaṃ kāraṇa’’nti| ‘‘kuṇḍadhānassa salākaṃ dassāmī’’ti gamanaṃ abhinīhari| kuṇḍadhānatthairau tassa purai āgamanāva abhiñ‍ñāpādakaṃ catutthajjhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitvā iddhiyā ākāsai ṭhatvā ‘‘āharāvusau ānanda, satthā maṃ jānāti, mādisaṃ bhikkhuṃ paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ gaṇhantaṃ na satthā vāraitī’’ti hatthaṃ pasāraitvā salākaṃ gaṇhi| satthā taṃ aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā thairaṃ imasmiṃ sāsanai paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ gaṇhantānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

vaṅgīsatthairavatthu

212. catutthai paṭibhānavantānanti sampan‍napaṭibhānānaṃ vaṅgīsatthairau aggauti dassaiti| ayaṃ kira thairau dasabalassa santikaṃ upasaṅkamantau cakkhupathatau paṭṭhāya candaina saddhiṃ upamaitvā, sūriyaina, ākāsaina, mahāsamuddaina, hatthināgaina, sīhaina migarañ‍ñā saddhiṃ upamaitvāpi anaikaihi padasataihi  padasahassaihi satthu vaṇṇaṃ vadantauyaiva upasaṅkamati| tasmā paṭibhānavantānaṃ aggau nāma jātau|

tassa pañhakammai ayamanupubbikathā – ayampi kira padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai mahābhaugakulai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhitvā purimanayainaiva vihāraṃ  gantvā dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ paṭibhānavantānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā satthu adhikārakammaṃ katvā ‘‘ahampi anāgatai paṭibhānavantānaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ katvā satthārā byākatau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbatti| vaṅgīsamāṇavautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau tayau vaidai uggaṇhantau ācariyaṃ ārādhaitvā chavasīsamantaṃ nāma sikkhitvā chavasīsaṃ nakhaina ākauṭaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ sattau asukayauniyaṃ nāma nibbattau’’ti jānāti|
